Result: 得到无限体积压力关于耦合律的βd-Lipschitz界及定量热力学极限；证明周期体积下几乎必然收敛等价于单体积压力律的完全收敛；证明E|J|^{1+1/d}<∞保证该普适耦合结论；证明该指数在一致幂矩假设下最优：对任意1≤q<1+1/d存在有限q阶矩的中心对称律，使规范嵌套体积几乎必然收敛，而同边际的重采样体积仅依概率收敛；一维中一阶矩条件对有限极限压力亦必要。

Conclusion: 淬火压力的热力学极限在1-Wasserstein意义下对耦合律具有定量稳定性，其几乎必然普适收敛由E|J|^{1+1/d}<∞刻画且在一致幂矩假设下最优，一维情形一阶矩为必要，从而完整界定无序分布尾行为对极限存在性与普适性的影响。

Abstract: We study the quenched pressure of the nearest-neighbor Edwards--Anderson Ising model with free and periodic boundary conditions. First, we prove that the infinite-volume pressure is $βd$-Lipschitz in the coupling law for the $1$-Wasserstein distance, yielding quantitative thermodynamic limits for spatially inhomogeneous disorder. Second, for periodic volumes, we prove that almost-sure convergence under every joint coupling of the finite-volume disorder arrays is equivalent to complete convergence of the one-volume pressure laws. Third, we prove that $\mathbb{E}\vert{}J\vert{}^{1+1/d}<\infty$ guarantees this universal-coupling conclusion. We further show that this exponent is optimal among uniform power-moment assumptions: for every $1\leq q<1+1/d$, there is a centered symmetric law with finite $q$-th moment for which canonical nested volumes converge almost surely, whereas independently resampled volumes with the same fixed-volume marginals converge in probability but not almost surely. Finally, in dimension one, we prove that the first-moment condition is also necessary for a finite limiting pressure.

</details>


### [88] [Euler-Poincare Reduction: A Geometric Treatment of the Relativistic Spinning Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09747)
*Burak Gul,O. Teoman Turgut*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将重陀螺视为半直积群上Euler-Poincaré约化的经典范例，证明自由相对论自旋粒子（定义在Poincaré群上）是其精确的洛伦兹类比，时空位置充当被平流量；并阐明外加电磁场导致部分约化，以及左平凡化坐标构成一种几何选择机制，使自旋-场耦合表现为质量的动力学重定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论自旋粒子的李群描述在哈密顿（辛）力学框架中已相当成熟，但缺少一个显式、透明的拉格朗日Euler-Poincaré约化表述；作者希望借助重陀螺这一经典范例，为相对论自旋粒子建立直接的几何对应，从而统一揭示两者的对称性结构。

Method: 在半直积群上采用拉格朗日Euler-Poincaré约化：将自由相对论自旋粒子置于Poincaré群上，把时空位置作为被平流量（advected quantity），与重陀螺中重力方向被平流的角色对应；进一步考察外加电磁场的情形引入部分约化，并分析左平凡化坐标对相互作用拉格朗日量的选择作用。

Result: 建立了自由相对论自旋粒子与重陀螺之间的精确洛伦兹类比；说明时空位置即被平流量；显示外加电磁场导致部分约化而非完全约化；并证明左平凡化坐标会自然筛选出一类相互作用拉格朗日量，其中自旋-场耦合等价于粒子质量的动力学重定义。

Conclusion: 显式的拉格朗日Euler-Poincaré约化为相对论自旋粒子提供了与重陀螺平行的透明几何图景，既阐明了两个系统的内在对称性，也解释了电磁场引入部分约化的原因，并揭示了坐标平凡化方式的几何选择意义。

Abstract: The heavy top is a classic example of Euler-Poincare reduction on a semidirect product group, demonstrating how an advected quantity-the direction of gravity-couples to rotational motion. While the description of relativistic spinning particles via Lie group configurations is well established in Hamiltonian (symplectic) mechanics, we demonstrate that an explicit Lagrangian Euler-Poincare reduction provides a direct, transparent parallel to the heavy top. Specifically, we show that the free relativistic spinning particle on the Poincare group is the precise Lorentzian analog of this system, with spacetime position being the advected quantity. This geometric unification not only illuminates the underlying symmetry of both systems but also clarifies why the introduction of external electromagnetic fields leads to a partial reduction. Finally, we establish that the transition to left-trivialized coordinates acts as a geometric selection mechanism, naturally favoring a class of interaction Lagrangians where spin-field coupling manifests as a dynamical redefinition of the particle's mass.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用Wilson算法并行生成大尺度均匀生成树,通过Majumdar–Dhar燃烧双射映射为独立循环构型,消除样本自相关并实现快速收敛;在方形(单子格)、蜂窝(双子格)和kagome格点上数值计算体相关函数;并通过晶格Green函数推导高度-1概率的闭式解析表达式进行交叉验证。

Conclusion: 数值方法验证了LFCFT预言,并可用于解析困难的格点;kagome格点上首次系统研究体相关函数,为后续研究提供了新工具与基准。

Abstract: We numerically study the bulk correlation functions in the two-dimensional Abelian sandpile model, aiming both to compare with the predictions of logarithmic conformal field theory and to extend the analysis to lattices where analytical methods are difficult to apply. Wilson's algorithm efficiently generates large-scale uniform spanning trees in parallel, which can be mapped to independent recurrent configurations via the Majumdar--Dhar burning bijection, eliminating sample autocorrelations and yielding fast convergence. On the square (single-sublattice) and honeycomb (two-sublattice) lattices our results agree well with the analytical predictions. For the kagome lattice we provide the first systematic numerical study of the bulk correlation functions, and cross-check the bulk height-1 probability against a closed-form analytical expression that we also derive here via the lattice Green function.

</details>

### [90] [Hamiltonian fingerprints of a collective mode across the BCS-BEC crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09223)

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作追踪了一个双层吸引Hubbard模型中层奇（layer-odd）集体响应，从弱耦合杂化锚定区演化到配对区，揭示其驱动机制从单粒子层间杂化向集体配对超交换的渡越过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体激发可在其微观自由度发生深刻变化时依然存在，但如何在费米子到复合玻色子的渡越过程中追踪这种集体响应、并刻画其微观驱动机制的变化，仍缺乏定量理解。作者意图借助一个可控的双层模型来系统研究这一演化。

Method: 采用精确的弱耦合杂化分析作为锚定点，结合无符号问题的行列式量子蒙特卡洛（DQMC）与高斯响应理论；在吸引Hubbard双层模型中追踪层奇集体响应，无需解析延拓即可提取多体谱尺度。

Result: 弱耦合下，隧道耦合相同层的精确恒等式将相对相位高斯核零点固定在2t_h，且与层内跃迁无关；DQMC显示相互作用驱动的多体谱尺度软化，高斯响应理论给出连续极点解释并表明响应从层密度特征向相对配对相位特征转移；配对区中特征响应尺度对层内跃迁呈逐渐增大的分数敏感性，且相互作用依赖部分趋近1/U规律。

Conclusion: 该结果展示了集体激发可跨费米子到复合玻色子渡越被持续追踪，同时对微观驱动机制的变化施加约束。

Abstract: Collective excitations can survive profound changes in the microscopic degrees of freedom that generate them. We track a layer-odd collective response from an exact weak-coupling hybridisation anchor into the paired regime of an attractive Hubbard bilayer and find that its Hamiltonian sensitivities evolve in a manner consistent with a crossover from single-particle interlayer hybridisation towards collective pair superexchange. At weak coupling, an exact identity for identical tunnel-coupled layers fixes a relative-phase Gaussian kernel zero at $2t_h$, independent of the intralayer hopping. Sign-free determinant quantum Monte Carlo reveals an interaction-driven softening of the many-body spectral scale without analytic continuation, while Gaussian response theory supplies the continuous pole interpretation and shows a shift from layer-density to relative-pair-phase character. In the paired regime, the characteristic response scale acquires a growing fractional sensitivity to the intralayer hopping and an interaction dependence partway towards the $1/U$ law. These results show how a collective excitation can be tracked across a fermion-to-composite-boson crossover while constraining changes in its microscopic drive.

</details>


### [91] [Two-magnon response from light scattering in altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09287)
*Shuyi Li,Lexu Zhao,Chunjing Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

Method: 采用线性自旋波理论并结合领头1/S量子修正，计算不同偏振通道下二维d波交错磁体的有限动量双磁子光散射强度。

Result: 磁子劈裂使双磁子连续谱上边缘及谱特征在X=(π,0)处相对常规反铁磁体向高能移动，位移正比于|δJ2|，而布里渊区中心响应不变；磁子-磁子相互作用虽将谱权重向低能重分布，但高能动量选择性重构能标保持稳健，并产生线性理论中不存在的低能双峰结构；在K=(π/2,π/2)处，相互作用双磁子共振在反铁磁相中二重简并，有限交错磁交换各向异性解除简并，两峰间距正比于|δJ2|。

Conclusion: 上述特征能标源于双磁子扇区本身而非特定散射算符，表明有限动量双磁子谱学可用于识别绝缘磁体中的交错磁序，并推动对候选材料的动量分辨共振非弹性X射线散射研究。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is a recently established class of magnetic order that combines fully compensated moments with momentum-dependent spin splitting, yet identifying its spectroscopic fingerprints remains an open challenge. In this work, we investigate finite-momentum two-magnon excitations in a two-dimensional $d$-wave altermagnet by calculating the two-magnon light scattering intensity in different polarization channels. Using linear spin-wave theory and further incorporating the leading $1/S$ quantum corrections, we demonstrate that the characteristic magnon splitting of altermagnets shifts the upper edge of the two-magnon continuum and the corresponding spectral features to higher energy at $\boldsymbol{X}=(π,0)$ relative to the conventional antiferromagnet, by an amount linear in the exchange anisotropy $|δJ_2|$, while leaving the response at the Brillouin-zone center unchanged. Although magnon-magnon interactions strongly redistribute spectral weight toward lower energies, the energy scale associated with the high-energy momentum-selective reconstruction remains robust. The interactions additionally generate a pronounced low-energy two-peak structure that has no counterpart within linear spin-wave theory. At $\boldsymbol{K}=(π/2,π/2)$, we show that the interacting two-magnon resonance is twofold degenerate in the conventional antiferromagnetic phase, while a finite altermagnetic exchange anisotropy lifts this degeneracy, producing two peaks whose separation is linear in $|δJ_2|$. The characteristic energy scales underlying both features are intrinsic to the two-magnon sector rather than to a specific scattering operator. These findings highlight the potential of finite-momentum two-magnon spectroscopy for identifying altermagnetic order in insulating magnets and motivate momentum-resolved resonant inelastic x-ray scattering studies of candidate altermagnetic materials.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome金属中费米能级附近的平带引发了多种关联电子相，但其微观起源难以厘清：真实描述需要同时考虑多轨道与竞争相互作用，材料细节掩盖了本质物理。因此作者希望剥离材料特异性，寻找平带区域的核心物理。

Conclusion: 平带简并本身即可内生产生异常自旋涨落，本文为理解平带Kagome体系中的关联效应建立了最小理论框架。

Abstract: Kagome metals exhibit a rich interplay of topology, electronic correlations, and lattice dynamics. Recent discoveries of Kagome materials with a flat band near the Fermi level have revealed a variety of correlated electronic phases. However, elucidating their microscopic origin remains challenging, as realistic descriptions require accounting for multiple orbitals and competing interactions on an equal footing. To disentangle correlation effects from material-specific details and identify the essential physics of the flat-band regime, we study the single-orbital Kagome-Hubbard model at flat-band fillings using dynamical mean-field theory. We find strong signatures of flat-band ferromagnetism, consistent with exact and mean-field ground-state results. Moreover, we uncover an unconventional quasi-ordered phase in which a partially filled spin-polarized flat band pinned at the Fermi level gives rise to persistent local spin fluctuations down to zero temperature, in striking contrast to the classical behavior expected for a conventional ferromagnet. Our results demonstrate that these anomalous fluctuations are an intrinsic consequence of the flat-band degeneracy and establish a minimal framework for understanding correlation effects in flat-band Kagome systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDW态由电子-声子耦合产生，同时包含周期晶格畸变与电子调制，因而对晶格应变敏感，可通过应变进行调控。NbSe₃是典型CDW体系，具有链状结构及145K、59K两个CDW转变，其波矢分别沿链轴b方向及与b成倾斜角。作者希望厘清拉伸应力如何影响其晶格结构与CDW性质，以及两者之间的关联。

Method: 采用X射线衍射与电输运测量相结合的手段，对NbSe₃单晶沿链轴b方向以及垂直于链轴方向施加拉伸应力，系统追踪晶格结构参数（泊松系数、杨氏模量、晶格常数）与电阻随应变的演化，并分析CDW临界温度的变化。

Result: 晶格结构表现出反常的泊松系数和强各向异性的杨氏模量；电阻随应变的演化显示两个CDW临界温度均发生移动，且与晶格参数变化强烈相关。两个CDW转变在外加应力下呈现不同行为，作者认为带曲率(band curvature)的改变导致了所观测到的输运特征。

Conclusion: 在准一维CDW体系NbSe₃中，拉伸应力可有效调控晶格结构与CDW态，两个CDW转变对应力的响应存在差异，其输运行为变化可由带曲率修正解释，说明应变是调控CDW相变的重要自由度。

Abstract: The Charge Density Wave (CDW) state is a perfect example of a combined structural and electronic state, both characterized by a periodic lattice distortion and an electronic modulation of condensed electrons, resulting from electron-phonon coupling. They are thus prone to be tuned by lattice strain. NbSe3 is a prototypical example of CDW states, with a chain-like structure displaying two CDWs at 145K and 59K, with wavevectors along and inclined with respect to the chain axis b. Here, we report on the evolution of the lattice structure and CDW properties in the quasi-one-dimensional charge-density-wave system NbSe3 under tensile stresses applied along and perpendicular to the chains axis b by a combination of X-ray diffraction and transport measurements. We find that the lattice structure show exotic Poissons coefficients and strongly anisotropic Youngs moduli while the evolution of the electrical resistance demonstrates shifts of both charge-density-wave critical temperatures that are strongly correlated with the lattice parameters. The two CDW transitions display different behaviours under applied stresses, and suggest that a modification of the band curvature leads to the observed transport signatures.

</details>


### [94] [Emergent Electronic Bond-Order Wave in a Quasi-One-Dimensional Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09383)
*Honghao Wang,Tristan R. Cao,Pedro Schlottmann,Gang Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在准一维材料Ba9Rh8O24中发现了电子键序波（BOW），其通过不等价Rh-Rh键的反转在180 K发生转变，并表现出非线性I-V、顺时针迟滞和非易失忆阻开关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维体系中强的量子涨落通常抑制对称破缺态，使得集体序难以稳定。作者试图探究以结构键作为活性电子自由度能否克服一维涨落并建立长程有序。

Method: 对Ba9Rh8O24单晶进行X射线衍射测量，结合热容测量和介电响应表征，并测量其电流-电压（I-V）特性以考察电学可重构行为。

Result: 单晶XRD揭示在TA = 180 K处不等价Rh-Rh键发生反转：短键与长键互换身份，而非传统的Peierls二聚化。该键反转伴随热容异常和介电响应的显著重组，冷却时介电损耗被强烈抑制。BOW表现出强非线性、频率依赖的I-V特性、顺时针迟滞及非易失忆阻开关。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了从动态"键液体"向刚性但电学可重构BOW的转变，确立键中心电子序作为一维量子物质的新组织原则。

Abstract: Collective order is notoriously difficult to stabilize in one dimension, where strong fluctuations suppress symmetry-breaking states. Here we report the emergence of an electronic bond-order wave (BOW) in the quasi-one-dimensional (1D) material Ba9Rh8O24, in which structural bonds become active electronic degrees of freedom that overcome 1D fluctuations to establish long-range order. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction uncovers a striking inversion of inequivalent Rh-Rh bonds across a transition TA = 180 K, where short and long bonds interchange their identities rather than undergoing conventional Peierls dimerization. This bond inversion coincides with a heat-capacity anomaly and a profound reorganization of the dielectric response with strong suppression of dielectric loss upon cooling. The BOW exhibits strongly nonlinear, frequency-dependent I-V characteristics, clockwise hysteresis, and nonvolatile memristive switching. These findings uncover an unprecedented transformation from a dynamic bond liquid to a rigid yet electrically reconfigurable BOW, establishing bond-centered electronic order as a new organizing principle for 1D quantum matter.

</details>


### [95] [Anomalous Hall Response Induced by Correlated Disorder in the Breathing Kagome Lattice Mn$_{3}$Sn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09699)
*Tsung-Han Yang,Seng Huat Lee,Hengxin Tan,Yuanpeng Zhang,Benjamin A. Frandsen,Václav Petříček,Huibo Cao,Daniel Olds,Matthew G. Tucker,Jiaqiang Yan,Binghai Yan,Zhiqiang Mao,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 近化学计量比Mn₃Sn在逆三角相（T_N1≈440 K以下）出现有限的σ_yx及增强的σ_zx、σ_yz，而在锥形相（T_N2≈280 K以下）中所有AHE分量消失；关联无序与局域晶格畸变、非共面Mn磁矩耦合，打破了平均对称性禁戒，成为激活拓扑输运的自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为宏观输运张量受材料平均晶体学与磁对称性约束。在kagome反铁磁Weyl半金属Mn₃₊δX中，平均六方结构与共面逆三角磁序本应禁戒反常霍尔电导σ_yx，因此需要探究为何近化学计量比样品仍表现出非零AHE及其微观机制。

Method: 对近化学计量比Mn₃Sn进行输运测量（σ_yx、σ_zx、σ_yz随温度的演化）；采用总散射与磁对分布函数（mPDF）分析局域结构畸变与磁矩取向；结合第一性原理计算验证晶格-自旋耦合畸变对σ_yx的激活作用。

Result: 在逆三角相（低于T_N1≈440 K）观测到有限σ_yx并伴随大磁滞，σ_zx、σ_yz亦增强；在振幅调制锥形相（低于T_N2≈280 K）中所有AHE分量消失。mPDF揭示关联的正交畸变与非共面Mn磁矩；第一性原理表明该耦合的晶格-自旋畸变激活了平均对称性禁戒的σ_yx。

Conclusion: 关联无序是一种主动的对称性破缺自由度，可产生仅凭Bragg平均结构无法解释的拓扑输运；其效应需与能够支撑Berry曲率的长程逆三角反铁磁序协同才能在逆三角相中显现，并在T_N2以下随该长程序消失而失效。

Abstract: Macroscopic transport tensors are generally constrained by the average crystallographic and magnetic symmetries of a material. In the kagome antiferromagnetic Weyl semimetals Mn$_{3+δ}X$ ($X=$~Sn or Ge), previous studies showed that the anomalous Hall conductivity $σ_{yx}$ is forbidden by the average \hexsg{} structure and coplanar inverse-triangular magnetic order. Here we report that nearly stoichiometric Mn$_3$Sn nevertheless exhibits a finite $σ_{yx}$ with large hysteresis, together with enhanced $σ_{zx}$ and $σ_{yz}$, in the inverse-triangular phase below $T_{\mathrm{N1}}\approx 440~\mathrm{K}$, whereas all AHE components vanish in the amplitude-modulated conical phase below $T_{\mathrm{N2}}\approx 280~\mathrm{K}$. Total scattering and magnetic pair distribution function analysis reveal correlated orthorhombic distortions and noncoplanar Mn moments. First-principles calculations show that this coupled lattice-spin distortion activates the average symmetry forbidden $σ_{yx}$ within the inverse-triangular phase. Its disappearance below $T_{\mathrm{N2}}$ indicates that the correlated disorder must cooperate with a long-range inverse-triangular antiferromagnetic order capable of supporting Berry curvature. Our results establish correlated disorder as an active symmetry-breaking degree of freedom that enables topological transport inaccessible from the Bragg-average structure alone.

</details>


### [96] [Orbital-selective electronic modes induced by doping and originating from distinct spin excitations: an essential feature of orbital-selective Mott transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09741)
*Masanori Kohno*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过向Kanamori-Hubbard模型中的轨道简并spin-1反铁磁绝缘体掺杂，发现带隙中以轨道选择方式出现电子模式，其色散呈动量移动的spin模式特征，构成轨道选择Mott转变（OSMT）的本质特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道选择Mott转变（OSMT）的本质仍不明确，亟需揭示其涌现物理特征。

Method: 在Kanamori-Hubbard模型中，对轨道简并的spin-1反铁磁绝缘体进行掺杂（空穴掺杂），研究带隙内电子模式的涌现行为及色散关系。

Result: 掺杂后带隙中出现电子模式，且以轨道选择方式分布：源于常规spin激发（含低能模式）的电子模式出现在掺杂轨道，而源于轨道间spin激发的模式出现在未掺杂轨道。这些模式呈现动量移动的spin模式色散关系。

Conclusion: 这些涌现电子模式（尤其是轨道间spin激发产生的模式）是OSMT的本质特征，为理解OSMT提供了新的判别依据。

Abstract: The nature of the orbital-selective Mott transition (OSMT) remains elusive. This study shows that, by doping an orbitally degenerate spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulator in the Kanamori-Hubbard model, electronic modes emerge in the band gap in an orbital-selective manner, exhibiting momentum-shifted spin-mode dispersion relations. Electronic modes originating from conventional spin excitations, including the low-energy one, appear in the doped orbital, while those from inter-orbital spin excitations appear in the undoped orbital. These emergent modes constitute an essential feature of the OSMT.

</details>


### [97] [Semi-Dirac States and Quantum Linear Magnetoresistance in Helimagnetic Pnictide MnP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09781)
*Prasanta Chowdhury,Jyotirmoy Sau,Sanat Kumar Adhikari,Sourav Chowdhury,Peter Bencok,Matthias Gutmann,Souvik Chatterjee,Saurav Giri,Manoranjan Kumar,Subham Majumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合实验与第一性原理计算，研究高质量MnP单晶中随磁相变化的磁输运行为，揭示非共线磁性与电子拓扑的耦合机制，并解释了低温下接近800%的线性正磁阻起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑与磁性材料中的大线性正磁阻（LPMR）机制尚存争议，尤其在非共线自旋体系中，自旋相关散射使电荷输运分析复杂化。MnP作为具有多重场诱导磁转变的螺旋磁性二元磷族化合物，为研究复杂磁性与电子拓扑相互作用提供了理想平台。

Method: 对高质量MnP单晶进行系统的磁输运测量（Hall测量、磁阻测量），并结合第一性原理计算，分析SCR、FAN和FM2各磁相下的能带结构演化。

Result: Hall测量显示高温下由斜散射主导的反常霍尔效应，并在非共线fan（FAN）相和低温screw（SCR）相中观察到有限拓扑霍尔效应。低温下观察到巨大的非饱和LPMR，在4 K、15 T时接近800%，且在场极化铁磁（FM2）态中呈现显著线性场依赖。第一性原理计算揭示Y点处存在强各向异性的半狄拉克型能带，该能带从SCR到FAN再到FM2态逐渐接近费米能级。

Conclusion: 由此形成的小费米口袋可在实验可及磁场下进入极端量子极限区，为所观测到的LPMR提供了基于Abrikosov量子磁阻理论的微观解释框架。

Abstract: Large linear positive magnetoresistance (LPMR) in topological and magnetic materials remains a subject of intense debate, particularly in noncollinear spin systems where spin-dependent scattering complicates charge transport. Manganese phosphide (MnP), a helimagnetic binary pnictide with multiple field-induced magnetic transitions, provides a useful platform to investigate the interplay between complex magnetism and electronic topology. Here, we present a comprehensive experimental and theoretical investigation of phase-dependent magnetotransport in high-quality MnP single crystals. Hall measurements reveal an anomalous Hall effect dominated by skew scattering at high temperatures and a finite topological Hall effect in the noncollinear fan (FAN) and low-temperature screw (SCR) phases. At low temperatures, we observe a large, non-saturating LPMR reaching nearly 800 percent at 4 K and 15 T, with a pronounced linear field dependence in the field-polarized ferromagnetic (FM2) state. First-principles calculations reveal a strongly anisotropic semi-Dirac-like band at the Y point that progressively approaches the Fermi level from the SCR to FAN and FM2 states. Our analysis indicates that the resulting small Fermi pocket can access the extreme quantum-limit regime at experimentally accessible fields, providing a microscopic framework for the observed LPMR within Abrikosov's quantum magnetoresistance theory.

</details>


### [98] [SIM-GRAPH: A universal guide to symmetric interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09996)
*R. C. Verstraten,C. Morais Smith*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出SIM-GRAPH方法，利用离散对称性投影相互作用以约化格点数，从而对相互作用量子系统的基态可观测值实现指数级加速，并给出两种扩展以权衡计算成本与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限量子系统中对称性通常为离散的（如反射与旋转对称性），而标准精确对角化受限于希尔伯特空间随格点数指数增长，需要利用这些对称性来高效求解基态可观测值。

Method: SIM-GRAPH（Symmetric Ising Models - Graph Reduction And Projected Hamiltonians）：将所有相互作用投影到系统的对称子集上，从而有效约化格点数目；并引入两种扩展以拓宽方法适用性并实现计算成本与精度之间的可控权衡。

Result: 通过对称子空间投影实现格点数约化，相比标准精确对角化获得指数级加速。

Conclusion: 对称性可被系统性地用于高效计算相互作用量子系统的基态可观测值，所提方法及其扩展在计算成本与精度上具备可控性。

Abstract: Symmetry plays a central role throughout physics, from Fourier analysis on discrete lattices to the classification of elementary particles in the standard model. In finite quantum systems, symmetries are often discrete, such as reflection and rotational symmetries. Here, we present the SIM-GRAPH method (Symmetric Ising Models - Graph Reduction And Projected Hamiltonians), which uses such symmetries to efficiently calculate ground-state observables of interacting quantum systems. By projecting all interactions onto a symmetric subset of the system, the method effectively reduces the number of sites, thus providing an exponential speedup over standard exact diagonalization. Furthermore, we introduce two extensions that broaden the applicability of the method and allow for a controlled trade-off between computational cost and accuracy.

</details>


### [99] [Non-equilibrium dissipative stabilization of s- and d-wave superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10062)
*Aleksey Lunkin,Yury Holubeu,Denis Golež,Zala Lenarčič*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在耦合两个热浴的BCS超导体中，通过破坏细致平衡的弱耦合稳定出高度非平衡稳态，从而在热浴温度远高于平衡临界温度时仍存在非热配对能隙，且该效应可用于s波与d波超导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在非平衡条件下稳定超导性，突破平衡态临界温度的限制。

Method: 将BCS超导体与两个热浴耦合，其中一个热浴温度可远高于平衡Tc，利用弱耦合打破细致平衡条件，建立非平衡稳态，并用广义Gibbs系综（以Bogoliubov准粒子化学势参数化）描述该稳态。

Result: 出现非热配对能隙；该非平衡稳态在s波和d波超导体中均表现出强非微扰效应，并讨论了在腔基实验中的可能实现。

Conclusion: 非平衡态工程可在超出平衡Tc的温度下稳定超导态，且该机制具有普适性并可能通过腔量子电动力学平台实现。

Abstract: Stabilizing superconductivity beyond its equilibrium regime has been one of the quests of nonequilibrium state engineering. We report the presence of nonthermal pairing gap in BCS superconductors coupled to two thermal baths, where one of the baths can be at a temperature well above the equilibrium $T_c$. Superconducting state is enabled by a highly non-equilibrium steady state stabilized by weak coupling to baths that break detailed balance condition. The steady-state is well described by a generalized Gibbs ensemble parametrized with chemical potentials for associated Bogoliubov quasiparticle. A strong, non-perturbative effect is reported for both $s$-wave and $d$-wave superconductors, and its possible realization in cavity-based experiments is discussed.

</details>


### [100] [Fast, Accurate, and Scalable Fermionic Neural Networks via Translation Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10186)
*David D. Dai,Yen-Ting Lin,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过将哈密顿量对称性精确内嵌到神经量子态设计中，提出TorFormer波函数，在二维电子气上同时提升训练效率与变分能量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经量子态（NQS）虽能高精度求解量子多体问题，但训练慢、能量精度有限，尤其在相变区分等对能量差异极敏感的场景下不足。作者假设：若让神经网络波函数成为哈密顿量对称性的精确本征态，可同时加速训练并降低变分能量。

Method: 针对二维电子气，设计TorFormer——一种总动量为精确本征值的神经网络波函数，将对称性以精确约束形式内嵌于网络结构中。在无监督条件下同时描述费米液体与Wigner晶体，并在大系统尺寸下与基于Psiformer的参考方法比较。

Result: 在N=91、r_s=30.0与40.0下，TorFormer仅训练8K步即超越此前需100K步的最佳NQS。在r_s=40.0处，去除平凡Madelung项后总能量改进0.12%，远大于区分不同相的微小能量差；相对Slater-Jastrow-backflow扩散蒙特卡洛的能量下降约为此前最佳NQS的9.8倍。

Conclusion: 将哈密顿量对称性精确编码进神经量子态可显著提升训练效率与变分精度，表明NQS能够高效且准确地求解大规模量子多体问题。

Abstract: We demonstrate that designing a neural quantum state to be an exact eigenstate of the Hamiltonian's symmetries significantly improves both training speed and final variational energy. For the 2D electron gas, we design TorFormer, a neural network wavefunction which is an exact eigenstate of the total momentum. TorFormer describes both the Fermi liquid and Wigner crystal with no supervision and significantly outperforms Psiformer-based references up to large system sizes. For $r_s = 30.0$ and $40.0$ at $N=91$, we compare TorFormer trained for $8\mathrm{K}$ steps against the previous best NQS, which required $100\mathrm{K}$ training steps. Our improvement to the total energy at $r_s = 40.0$, excluding the trivial Madelung part, is $0.12\%$---enormous compared to the tiny differences separating phases. Relative to Slater-Jastrow-backflow diffusion Monte Carlo, TorFormer's energy decrease is roughly $9.8$ times that of the previous best NQS. Our work demonstrates that neural quantum states can both accurately and efficiently solve large-scale problems.

</details>


### [101] [Gauge mean-field theories of the underscreened Kondo lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10443)
*Ewan Scott,Michal Kwasigroch*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过单一控制参数 $N$ 与无偏变分拟设，将自旋-$1$ 欠屏蔽 Kondo 晶格中磁性序与 Kondo 杂化共存的多种平均场解耦方案统一并与 Read-Newns 理论及 Nozières 强耦合理论联系起来，给出相图及不同方案在杂化强度与总磁化上的关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-$1$ 欠屏蔽 Kondo 晶格中磁序与 Kondo 杂化可共存，是重要且具挑战性的问题；此前已有多种平均场解耦方案被提出，但缺乏统一框架，难以比较其有效性与差异，也无法清晰联系 Read-Newns 理论与 Nozières 强耦合理论。

Method: 引入单一控制参数 $N$ 与无偏变分拟设，将此前提出的解耦方案统一并连接到标准 Read-Newns 理论（其中 $1/N$ 下涨落很小）；计算大-$N$ 极限附近的修正，并通过寻找重费米子金属情形下对数衰减的残余铁磁 Hund 相互作用与 Nozières 强耦合理论建立联系；绘制以 $N$ 和 Kondo 耦合为变量的基态相图。

Result: 不同平均场理论在共存相的杂化强度与总磁化上存在关键差异；在统一变分理论框架内，此前提出的解耦方案对应于要么从一开始取 $N=2$，要么进行 $1/N$ 展开后再外推到 $N=2$；并找到与 Nozières 强耦合理论一致的对数衰减残余铁磁 Hund 相互作用。

Conclusion: 该统一变分理论澄清了自旋-$1$ 欠屏蔽 Kondo 晶格中各种平均场解耦方案的关系与差异，建立了与 Read-Newns 及 Nozières 理论的联系，并总结了理论向 $S>1$ 的推广。

Abstract: Various mean-field decoupling schemes have been introduced thus far to study the important and challenging problem of the spin-$1$ underscreened Kondo lattice where magnetic order can coexist with Kondo hybridization. We use a single control parameter $N$ and an unbiased variational ansatz to unify and connect the previously proposed decouplings to standard Read-Newns theory, where fluctuations are small in $1/N$. We compute the corrections around the large-$N$ limit. In particular, we make contact with Nozières strong-coupling theory by finding the residual ferromagnetic Hund interaction that decays logarithmically in the case of a heavy-fermion metal. We map out the ground state phase diagram as a function of $N$ and the Kondo coupling. We find crucial differences between the previously proposed mean-field theories in the strength of the hybridization and total magnetization of the coexistent phase. We show that, within our unifying variational theory, the previously proposed decouplings correspond to either taking $N=2$ from the start, or performing a $1/N$ expansion and then extrapolating to $N=2$. Finally, we summarize the generalization of our theory to $S>1$.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

### [102] [A proof of Hare's $ε$ unfair conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09215)
*Tho Nguyen Xuan,Duc Hiep Pham,Huong Pham Lan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Hare提出的$ε$-unfair猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hare在关于不公平0--1多项式猜想的研究中提出了$ε$-unfair猜想,该问题尚未得到解决,本文旨在对其给出证明。

Method: 通过数学证明方法,对Hare提出的$ε$-unfair猜想进行证明。

Result: 成功证明了Hare的$ε$-unfair猜想。

Conclusion: Hare的$ε$-unfair猜想成立。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove Hare's $ε$-unfair conjecture in [Computational progress on the unfair 0--1 polynomial conjecture, Experiment. Math. (2025), https://doi.org/10.1080/10586458.2025.2527786].

</details>


### [103] [The density of long runs of non-$r$-free integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09221)
*Xinyang Jiang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文精确确定了 $r$-free 数间隙频率的渐近常数，给出 $\log(1/D_k)$ 的完整渐近展开，并改进了此前关于 Mirsky 常数 $\alpha(h)$ 的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 Mirsky 常数 $\alpha(h)$（squarefree 数中间隙 $h$ 的密度）仅有单侧估计 $\log\alpha(h)\le-\frac54 h\log\log h+O(h)$，缺乏精确的两侧渐近和显式线性系数，因此需要更精细的方法来确定 $D_k$ 及 $\alpha(h)$ 的渐近行为。

Method: 上界利用 $p\le z$ 且 $\prod_{p\le z}p^r\le\sqrt k$ 的素数筛出长度 $k$ 的窗口，证明至少 $(\frac{1}{\zeta(r)}+o(1))k$ 个位置被超过 $k^{1/r}$ 的素数之 $r$ 次幂覆盖，并将注入求和 $m!e_m$（$m\sim k/\zeta(r)$）用鞍点法求值；下界使用 Chung-Erdős 不等式恢复 $m!e_m$ 的整体贡献而非仅最大项，从而显式给出线性系数。

Result: 证明 $\log(1/D_k)=\frac{r-1}{\zeta(r)}k\log k+\frac{r}{\zeta(r)}k\log\log k-C_rk+o(k)$，其中 $C_r=\frac{1}{\zeta(r)}((r-1)\log\zeta(r)+r-1+r\log\frac{\pi}{r\sin(\pi/r)})$；$r=2$ 时 $C_2=\frac{6}{\pi^2}(1+\log\frac{\pi^4}{24})=1.4595513\dots$。由此确定 Mirsky 常数 $\alpha(h)$ 至同样精度，改进因子为 $\log h/\log\log h$ 量级且为两侧估计。还证明第一矩阈值为 $\frac{\zeta(r)}{r-1}\frac{\log x}{\log\log x}(1-\frac{(1+o(1))\log\log\log x}{(r-1)\log\log x})$，$r=2$ 时常数为 $\pi^2/6$；并证明任意长度 $k$ 的覆盖系统模数至少为 $\exp((\frac{r}{\zeta(r)}+o(1))k\log k)$。

Conclusion: 该结果说明中国剩余构造只给出 $\zeta(r)/r$（$r=2$ 时为 $\pi^2/6$ 的一半），而 Erdős 的下界等价于：首段 $k$ 个连续非 $r$-free 整数出现在第一矩阈值处，而非覆盖系统最小模数所设定的尺度。

Abstract: Fix $r\ge2$ and let $D_k$ be the natural density of the $n$ such that none of $n+1,\dots,n+k$ is $r$-free. We prove that $\log(1/D_k)=\frac{r-1}{ζ(r)}k\log k+\frac{r}{ζ(r)}k\log\log k-C_rk+o(k)$, where $C_r=\frac{1}{ζ(r)}((r-1)\logζ(r)+r-1+r\log\fracπ{r\sin(π/r)})$; for $r=2$ this is $C_2=\frac{6}{π^2}(1+\log\frac{π^4}{24})=1.4595513\dots$. This determines Mirsky's constant $α(h)$, the density of the $n$ with $s_{n+1}-s_n=h$ in the squarefree numbers, to the same precision. Previously the only known estimate was $\logα(h)\le-\frac54 h\log\log h+O(h)$; ours improves this by a factor of order $\log h/\log\log h$, and is two-sided.
  The upper bound rests on the observation that the primes $p\le z$ with $\prod_{p\le z}p^r\le\sqrt k$ sieve a window of length $k$ with no freedom of alignment, so that at least $(1/ζ(r)+o(1))k$ positions must be covered by $r$-th powers of distinct primes exceeding $k^{1/r}$. The resulting sum over injections is $m!e_m$ with $m\sim k/ζ(r)$, evaluated by a saddle point. For the lower bound the Chung-Erdos inequality recovers the full contribution of $m!e_m$ rather than merely its largest term, which is what makes the linear coefficient explicit.
  As a consequence the first-moment threshold for gaps between $r$-free numbers is $\frac{ζ(r)}{r-1}\frac{\log x}{\log\log x}(1-\frac{(1+o(1))\log\log\log x}{(r-1)\log\log x})$; for $r=2$ the constant is $π^2/6$, that of Erdos's 1951 lower bound. We also prove that every covering system of length $k$ has modulus at least $\exp((\frac{r}{ζ(r)}+o(1))k\log k)$, so the Chinese remainder construction yields only $ζ(r)/r$, half of $π^2/6$ when $r=2$. Erdos's bound is thus equivalent to the assertion that the first run of $k$ consecutive non-$r$-free integers occurs at the first-moment threshold, not at the scale set by the minimal modulus of a covering system.

</details>


### [104] [Spectral Algebras of Abelian Cayley Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09222)
*Deep Bhattacharjee,Priyabrata Mandal,Ushashi Bhattacharya*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了有限阿贝尔群 Cayley 图在特征不整除群阶的域上的谱代数 $\mathscr{A_K}(Cay(G,S))$，证明其经特征轨道分解可分解为 $K$ 的扩张域的半单积，并给出维数、幂等元计数、显式本原幂等元计算及相关新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cayley 图的邻接矩阵谱代数是代数图论与代数组合的核心对象，已有结果多集中于循环群或有理数域等特例。本文旨在对一般有限阿贝尔群、一般特征不整除群阶的域，给出谱代数的统一结构定理与可计算描述。

Method: 利用阿贝尔离散傅里叶变换将邻接矩阵 $A$ 对角化；借助 Galois 群在特征群 $\widehat{G}$ 上的作用把特征值 $\lambda_\chi=\sum_{s\in S}\chi(s)$ 划分为轨道；再由中国剩余定理将无平方最小多项式转化为 Wedderburn 乘积，从而得到谱代数按轨道的域扩张半单分解，并用 $K[x]$ 中的 Bezout 算法显式计算本原幂等元。

Result: 谱代数 $\mathscr{A_K}(Cay(G,S))$ 分解为对应各 $Gal(\overline{K}/K)$-特征值轨道的域扩张半单积；其维数等于不同特征值的个数，幂等元个数为 $2^r$（$r$ 为轨道数）；本原幂等元可由 Bezout 算法显式求得。在 $\mathbb Q$ 上每个 Wedderburn 分量是 $\mathbb Q(\zeta_N)$ 的实子域。新结果包括：Cayley 图笛卡尔积的张量积比较；初等阿贝尔群 $(\mathbb Z/p)^k$ 的谱代数分析（$p\le3$ 时为有理，$p\ge5$ 需实分圆扩张）；对 $\mathbb Z/6\times\mathbb Z/2$、$\mathbb Z/5\times\mathbb Z/2$ 等非循环群的轨道分析。循环情形回复 $\mathscr{A}_{\mathbb{Q}}(C_n)\cong\prod_{d\mid n}\mathbb Q(\zeta_d)^+$；Hamming 立方体给出 $\mathscr{A}_\mathbb{Q}(\mathbb Q_k)\cong\mathbb{Q}^{k+1}$；并处理了特征 $p$ 情形。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般有限阿贝尔群 Cayley 图的谱代数提供了统一的半单 Wedderburn 分解理论，刻画了其维数与幂等元结构，给出可算法化的本原幂等元构造，并将循环群、初等阿贝尔群、Hamming 立方体及特征 $p$ 情形纳入同一框架。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite abelian group of order $N$, $S \subseteq G \setminus \{0\}$ a symmetric connection set, and $K$ a field with $char(K) \nmid N$. The spectral algebra $\mathscr{A_K}(Cay(G,S)) = K[A]$ generated by the adjacency matrix of the Cayley graph is proved to decompose, via the character-orbit decomposition, as a semisimple product of field extensions of $K$, one factor for each $Gal(\overline{K}/K)$-orbit of the eigenvalues $λ_χ= \sum_{s \in S} χ(s)$. The proof uses the abelian discrete Fourier transform to diagonalise $A$, the Galois action on the character group $\widehat{G}$ to partition eigenvalues into orbits, and the Chinese Remainder Theorem to convert the squarefree minimal polynomial into a Wedderburn product. The dimension of $\mathscr{A_K}(Cay(G,S))$ equals the number of distinct eigenvalues, the idempotent count is $2^r$ where $r$ is the orbit number, and primitive idempotents are computed explicitly via the Bezout algorithm in $K[x]$. Over $\mathbb Q$, every Wedderburn summand is a real subfield of the cyclotomic field $\mathbb Q(ζ_N)$. New results include: a tensor-product comparison for Cartesian products of Cayley graphs; a systematic analysis of the spectral algebra for elementary abelian groups $(\mathbb Z/p)^k$ (rational for $p \le 3$, requiring real cyclotomic extensions for $p \ge 5$); and a worked orbit analysis for non-cyclic groups including $\mathbb Z/6 \times \mathbb Z/2$ and $\mathbb Z/5 \times \mathbb Z/2$. The cyclic case recovers the companion result $\mathscr{A}_{\mathbb{Q}}(C_n) \cong \prod_{d \mid n} \mathbb Q(ζ_d)^+$; the Hamming cube gives $\mathscr{A}_\mathbb{Q}(\mathbb Q_k) \cong \mathbb{Q}^{k+1}$. The characteristic-$p$ case is also treated.

</details>


### [105] [Triple intersections for algebraic curves on the real torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09231)
*Danilo Avaro*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究实代数平面曲线在实环面上的投影是否可产生无穷多个三重交点，完整分类了三条直线的情形，并对高次曲线给出了有限性与无穷性两类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Corvaja与Zannier的一个问题启发，探讨实代数平面曲线投影到实环面后三重交点的无穷性问题。

Method: 对三条直线情形进行细致分析并给出完整分类；对高次曲线，利用Siegel与Levin关于曲线上整点的定理推导有限性结果；同时构造具有无穷多三重交点的显式高次曲线例子。

Result: 三条直线情形得到完整分类；高次曲线获得若干有限性结果；并构造出无穷多三重交点的显式高次曲线反例。

Conclusion: 曲线投影的三重交点无穷性与曲线次数及几何结构密切相关，直线情形可完全刻画，而高次曲线则同时存在有限性与无穷性两种现象。

Abstract: Motivated by a question of Corvaja and Zannier, we investigate whether the projections on the real torus of three real algebraic plane curves admit infinitely many triple intersections. We analyze the case of three lines in detail, obtaining a complete classification. For higher-degree curves, we derive several finiteness results using Siegel's and Levin's theorems on integral points on curves. On the other hand, we construct explicit examples of higher-degree curves with infinitely many triple intersections.

</details>


### [106] [Cyclotomy of symmetric polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09232)
*Laura De Carli,Maurizio Laporta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了齐次对称多项式在单位根处取值的一些新恒等式，并将其部分公式应用于分圆多项式，获得新认识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 齐次对称多项式与分圆多项式是代数与组合数学中的重要对象，作者希望揭示二者在单位根取值情形下的新的代数关系。

Method: 在单位根处对齐次对称多项式进行求值，推导并证明若干新的恒等式，进而将所得公式用于分圆多项式的分析。

Result: 建立了一组关于齐次对称多项式在单位根处取值的新恒等式，并由此得到关于分圆多项式的新结论。

Conclusion: 这些恒等式为理解对称多项式与分圆多项式之间的联系提供了新工具与新视角。

Abstract: We prove some new identities for homogeneous symmetric polynomials when evaluated at roots of unity. Some of these formulas are applied to obtain new insights onto cyclotomic polynomials.

</details>


### [107] [Effective Euler Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09260)
*Jianqiang Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在深度1和2下显式构造了与经典Euler和对应的有限Euler和（有效Euler和），并在附录中通过推广Kaneko-Zagier的启发式论证给出深度2偶权重的候选，证明了其与Kaneko-Zagier启发式定义的有效双zeta值一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euler和（交错多重zeta值）在数学与理论物理中有重要作用。受Kaneko和Zagier关于MZV的猜想启发，作者此前提出了有限Euler和空间与经典Euler和空间模ζ(2)乘积之间的同构猜想，但该猜想对应的具体有限元素尚未被显式构造。

Method: 在深度1和2下显式构造对应于经典Euler和的有限元素（有效Euler和）；在附录中推广Kaneko和Zagier对双zeta情形的启发式论证，给出深度2偶权重下的另一组候选有效Euler和；并借助Kina在MZV设置下独立得到的部分结果进行证明。

Result: 显式构造了深度1和2的有效Euler和；在附录中给出深度2偶权重的候选有效Euler和；证明了Kaneko-Zagier启发式定义的有效双zeta值与本文构造一致。

Conclusion: 本文推进了有限Euler和与经典Euler和同构猜想的验证，在低深度情形下给出了显式对应，并厘清了与Kaneko-Zagier启发式定义的一致性；Kina的独立结果在MZV设置下部分印证了本文工作。

Abstract: Euler sums, also called alternating multiple zeta values (MZVs), have been shown to play important roles in many research areas in mathematics and theoretical physics. Motivated by a similar conjecture for MZVs by Kaneko and Zagier, the author proposed a conjectural isomorphism between the space of finite Euler sums and the space of classical ones modulo $ζ(2)$-products. In this paper, we explicitly construct those finite elements (for which we call effective Euler sums) that correspond to the classical Euler sums under this conjecture in depth one and two. In the appendix, we offer another group of plausible candidates of effective Euler sums when depth is two and weight is even, by extending the heuristic argument of Kaneko and Zagier for the double zeta case. Kina recently obtained independently some the same results in the MZV setting. In particular, using one of his results we prove in the appendix that Kaneko and Zagier's heuristically defined effective double zeta values coincides with ours.

</details>


### [108] [The addition on totally positive integers uniquely determines the totally real number field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09346)
*Vitezslav Kala,Ritoprovo Roy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明全实域K的全正代数整数加法半群$\mathcal O_K^+$唯一决定K，并给出其以不可分解元为生成元、以显式关系为生成关系的表示；方法推广到一类离散半群（半格）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全正代数整数在加法半群结构下能否唯一决定全实域K，并刻画其全部加法关系，从而揭示该半群与其数域之间的内在对应。

Method: 将$\mathcal O_K^+$视为抽象加法半群，通过找出一组不可分解元作为生成元，并显式构造一组生成关系，给出该半群的表示；同时把该表示方法推广到更一般的离散半群（作者称为semilattices）。

Result: 证明$\mathcal O_K^+$作为抽象加法半群唯一决定全实域K；并以不可分解元为生成元、以具体显式关系为生成关系给出$\mathcal O_K^+$的完整表示；该表示在更一般的半格类离散半群中同样成立。

Conclusion: 全正代数整数加法半群的结构足以唯一刻画全实域K，且其加法关系可由一套显式生成关系完整描述；相关结论可推广至更一般的半格型离散半群。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a totally real number field. We prove that the totally positive algebraic integers $\mathcal O_K^+$ of $K$, viewed as an abstract additive semigroup, uniquely determine $K$. We also describe all the additive relations by giving a presentation of $\mathcal O_K^+$ in terms of indecomposables as generators and a certain concrete set of generating relations. In fact, we obtain this presentation in a more general class of discrete semigroups which we call semilattices.

</details>


### [109] [Generic Manin-Mumford](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09354)
*Lior Bary-Soroker,Borys Kadets*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究代数数集合 $\mathcal{S}$ 下的非可能相交问题：对广义 Laguerre 多项式根等经典族，证明不可约曲线含无穷多 $\mathcal{S}^2$ 点的分类结果，并给出随机多项式根几乎必然成立的类似结论，证明依赖统一的 Galois 理论判别准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Ihara–Serre–Tate 定理与 Manin–Mumford 猜想刻画了单位根情形下的非可能相交现象。本文旨在将这类结果推广到更一般的代数数集合（如广义 Laguerre 多项式根），并探讨随机多项式根是否也满足该性质，从而寻求统一的判别框架。

Method: 对代数数集合 $\mathcal{S}\subset\overline{\mathbb{Q}}$，考察 $\mathbb{A}^m_\mathbb{C}$ 中使 $V(\mathbb{C})\cap\mathcal{S}^m$ 在 $V$ 中 Zariski 稠密的簇 $V$。核心工具是一个统一的 Galois 理论判别准则，用以保证非可能相交性质；并据此分析广义 Laguerre 多项式根族及随机多项式根族。

Result: 对广义 Laguerre 多项式 $L_n^{(\alpha)}(x)$ 的根（有限多个 $\alpha\in\mathbb{Q}$）等经典族，非可能相交定理成立。当 $m=2$ 时，不可约曲线含无穷多 $\mathcal{S}^2$ 点当且仅当其为 $x_1=x_2$、$x_1=s$ 或 $x_2=s$（$s\in\mathcal{S}$）。此外，对度数增长、高度有界的随机多项式根集合，类似结论几乎必然成立。

Conclusion: 本文推广了单位根情形的 Ihara–Serre–Tate 定理与 Manin–Mumford 猜想，建立了广义 Laguerre 多项式根及随机多项式根的非可能相交理论，其统一证明基于 Galois 理论判别准则，为更广泛的代数数集合提供了框架。

Abstract: Given a collection of algebraic numbers $\mathcal{S}\subset \overline{\mathbb{Q}}$ we study the varieties $V$ in $\mathbb{A}^m_\mathbb{C}$ such that $V(\mathbb{C})\cap\mathcal{S}^m$ is Zariski-dense in $V$. We show that for many classical families of algebraic numbers $\mathcal{S}$---such as the family of roots of generalized Laguerre polynomials $L_n^{(α)}(x)$, for a finite collection of $α\in \mathbb{Q}$---an unlikely intersections theorem holds. For example, in the case $m=2$, we prove that an irreducible curve in $\mathbb{A}^2_\mathbb{C}$ has infinitely many points from $\mathcal{S}^2$ if and only if it is of the form $x_1=x_2,$ or $x_1=s$, or $x_2=s$ for a fixed $s \in \mathcal{S}$. This is an analogue of the classical theorems of Ihara, Serre, and Tate, treating the case of $\mathcal{S}$ consisting of the roots of unity, and of the Manin--Mumford conjecture. We also show that a similar result holds almost surely for roots of a collection of random polynomials of growing degree and bounded height. The proofs rely on a uniform Galois-theoretic criterion ensuring the unlikely intersection property.

</details>


### [110] [Summing the reciprocal of the polynomial appearing in Fermat's Last Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02112)
*Ariel Edery*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究互反和 S(m,N)=Σ 1/(x^N+y^N−z^N)，发现其主要部分 D(m,N)=(2m−1)H_m^{(N)} 近似线性为 2ζ(N)m，余项 R(m,N) 在 N≥4 收敛，而 N=3 时可能对数发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费马大定理保证 f=x^N+y^N−z^N 永不为零（N≥3），故 1/f 无奇点，可研究其有限三重和 S(m,N)。作者希望给出 S(m,N) 的简洁解析表达式，并厘清其线性行为的来源及余项 R(m,N) 的收敛性。

Method: 将 S(m,N) 拆分为主项 D(m,N) 与余项 R(m,N)：主项有解析式 (2m−1)H_m^{(N)}，其中 H_m^{(N)}→ζ(N)；对余项进行数值分析，并将其与 Cauchy 主值积分（CPV integral）比较作解析估计。

Result: S(m,N)≈(2m−1)H_m^{(N)}，基本关于 m 线性，斜率约 2ζ(N)，且线性行为从 m=1 起成立而非仅渐近。N=3 时在 8≤m≤12 出现轻微偏离，源于费马近失（x^3+y^3−z^3=±1，z≠x,y）使 R(m,3) 在 m=9 跳变。数值显示 R(m,N) 对 N≥4 收敛，N=3 难以判断；CPV 积分分析表明 R(m,3) 很可能对数发散，N≥4 收敛。

Conclusion: S(m,N) 几乎等于其解析主项，呈线性增长，斜率由 2ζ(N) 决定；余项的收敛性依赖 N：N≥4 收敛，N=3 疑似对数发散。结论部分提出了若干值得进一步研究的问题。

Abstract: Consider the polynomial $f=x^N+y^N-z^N$ where $x,\,y$ and $z$ are positive integers and $N \ge 3$ is an integer. By Fermat's Last Theorem, $f$ is never zero so that its reciprocal, $1/f$, has no singularities. We therefore study the finite sum of the reciprocal: $S(m,N)=\sum_{x=1}^m\sum_{y=1}^m\sum_{z=1}^{m}\frac{1}{f}$. The terms $1/f$ can be positive, negative and their magnitude is less than unity. A key observation is that $S(m,N)$ can be split into two convenient parts: a dominant contribution $D(m,N)$ that has a simple analytical expression and a remainder $R(m,N)$ which is more complicated but negligible compared to $D(m,N)$. Therefore, $S(m,N)$ is almost identical to $D(m,N)$. The analytical expression for $D(m,N)$ is $(2\,m-1)\,H_m^{(N)}$ where $H_m^{(N)}=\sum_{x=1}^m\frac{1}{x^N}$ approaches quickly the Riemann zeta function $ζ(N)$ as $m$ increases. Therefore, the original sum $S(m,N)$ has a simple expression: it is basically linear in $m$ with slope equal to $2\,ζ(N)$. Its linear behavior is not an asymptotic result; plots of $S(m,N)$ vs. $m$ for different $N$ show a straight line starting at $m=1$. $S(m,N)$ deviates slightly from a straight line over a small interval $8\le m\le 12$ for the case $N=3$. This slight deviation is due to Fermat near misses where $x^3+y^3-z^3=\pm 1$ (for $z\ne x$ and $z\ne y$); these create a jump in the remainder $R(m,3)$ at $m=9$. We make a numerical and analytical study of the remainder $R(m,N)$. From the numerical analysis, $R(m,N)$ converges for $N\ge 4$ but it was harder to tell whether $N=3$ converged. An analytical study based on a comparison of $R(m,N)$ to its Cauchy principal value integral, shows that $R(m,3)$ likely diverges logarithmically. It also shows that $R(m,N)$ converges for $N\ge 4$ in agreement with the numerical analysis. We discuss in the conclusion some interesting questions for future investigation.

</details>


### [111] [Regular Arithmetic Functions, Volume I. Theory, Applications, Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09366)
*Benoit Cloitre*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文是正则算术函数（RAF）理论两卷本的第一卷，提出以单一定义方程刻画核函数性质的框架，核心为"正则指数"α(G)，并以Ingham核将黎曼假设等价重构为α=1/2的正则性问题，同时给出十七个核的实例清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图建立一套统一理论，用一个内蕴于核本身的量（正则指数）来刻画算术函数的性态；其动机来自类比、实验与观察，并希望借此把黎曼假设等看似不同的问题纳入同一框架，同时改进正交递归展开衰减界等结果。

Method: 设二元整变量函数G(n,k)满足G(n,n)≠0，对给定β通过逐阶求解线性方程组∑_{k≤n} a_k G(n,k)=n^{-β} 定义序列(a_k)，无需假设条件或收敛性讨论；考察部分和∑_{k≤n} a_k 的行为随指数的变化——在临界指数以下复现强制速率、以上则吸收该速率，该转折点即定义为核的正则指数α(G)，属于核本身的内蕴量。

Result: 黎曼假设成立当且仅当Ingham核 G(n,k)=(k/n)⌊n/k⌋ 是正则指数为1/2的正则算术函数；此外，同一理论在另一类问题上改进了单位分解正交递归展开的已知衰减界；文中给出十七个核组成的"画廊"以展示该概念的可操作性，并对猜想性、条件性与开放性内容单独标注并汇集成册。

Conclusion: 第一卷确立正则算术函数理论及其正则指数的基本框架与应用，第二卷将专门研究Ingham核，并通过"规范系统"（gauged system）建立其与Hasse-Weil zeta函数世界的联系。

Abstract: This is the first of two volumes on regular arithmetic functions, and an introduction to their theory.
  A regular arithmetic function (RAF) is a kernel whose properties come from a single defining equation. Let $G(n,k)$ be a function of two integer variables with $G(n,n)\neq 0$, and for each $β$ define a sequence $(a_k)$ by $\sum_{k\le n} a_k G(n,k)=n^{-β}$, solved rank by rank with nothing to assume and no convergence to establish. Then $G$ is regular when the partial sums $\sum_{k\le n} a_k$ change behaviour at one exponent. Below it they reproduce the forced rate, above it they absorb it. That tipping point is the regularity index $α(G)$, a quantity belonging to the kernel itself.
  The index came out of analogies, experiment and observation, and it is arithmetic by nature. Its most visible application is the Riemann hypothesis, which holds if and only if Ingham's kernel $G(n,k)=(k/n)\lfloor n/k\rfloor$ is a RAF of index $1/2$. It is not the only one. On a problem of a quite different kind the same theory improves the known decay bound for the orthorecursive expansion of unity. Seventeen kernels are worked out in a gallery, so that the notion can be handled by the reader. What is conjectural, conditional or open is marked as such and collected in a register. Volume II is given over to that kernel alone, with its connection to the world of Hasse-Weil zeta functions through a gauged system.

</details>


### [112] [What Does MMLU Actually Measure? A Psychometric Audit of Difficulty Structure in Aggregate Benchmark Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09372)
*Dana Paquin,Riddhiman Jain*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文用心理测量学方法证明MMLU总分主要衡量事实检索能力而非推理能力，并揭示基准内部STEM与非STEM子集的难度映射不一致，建议分维度报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMLU被广泛用作衡量通用AI能力的基准，但其总分究竟测量何种能力缺乏严格验证。作者质疑将检索与推理两种本质不同的能力通过单一测试分数来评估存在固有缺陷。

Method: 基于项目反应理论（IRT），在14,042道MMLU题目上对1,000个开放权重语言模型进行题目难度校准；将难度对确定性的、可文本抽取的结构复杂度框架做回归；采用带学科聚类协方差的联合Wald检验；并在响应模型中控制多项选择猜测下限。

Result: Wald检验显示MMLU混合了本质上可分离的构念；结构复杂度到难度的映射在STEM与非STEM分区之间不具不变性。聚合排行榜名次与非STEM准确率的相关性高于STEM准确率，若按总分选取Top-50模型用于推理密集型任务，会错配约22%的STEM适任模型。控制猜测下限后，能力更强的模型在推理深度增加时性能下降更陡。

Conclusion: MMLU总分对检索能力与推理稳定性赋予了不均衡的权重，无意中偏向为检索而优化的模型。作者发布其确定性框架作为可复现的审计工具，并建议采用分维度（disaggregated）报告取代单一聚合分数。

Abstract: Although MMLU is widely adopted as a benchmark for calibrating general AI capabilities, we psychometrically demonstrate that its aggregate score primarily evaluates a model's factual retrieval capacity rather than its reasoning ability. By calibrating item difficulty for 1,000 open-weights language models over 14,042 MMLU test items using Item Response Theory, we show that evaluating both abilities via a single test is inherently flawed. Difficulty is then regressed on a deterministic, text-extractable framework of structural complexity. Applying a joint Wald test with subject-clustered covariances demonstrates that the MMLU conflates fundamentally separable constructs. The mapping from structural complexity to difficulty is not invariant across the benchmark's STEM and non-STEM partitions. This finding has practical consequences. Aggregate leaderboard ranks track non-STEM accuracy more closely than STEM accuracy, so selecting a Top-50 model on the aggregate for a reasoning-intensive deployment displaces roughly 22% of the STEM-appropriate choices. Furthermore, when controlling for the multiple-choice guessing floor natively inside the response model, we find that higher-ability models continue to degrade more steeply under increased reasoning depth. The MMLU aggregate therefore weights retrieval capacity and reasoning stability unequally, inadvertently favoring models optimized for retrieval. We release our deterministic framework as a reproducible auditing instrument and recommend disaggregated reporting.

</details>


### [113] [Models of Elliptic Curves with Maximal 2-adic Image Subject to a Rational 2-Isogeny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09509)
*Roberto Hernandez*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对具有有理2-同源的非CM椭圆曲线，显式构造了2-adic像尽可能大的曲线模型，并以此明确验证了这类曲线的“一般性”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有有理2-同源的非CM椭圆曲线在理论上具有特殊地位，其2-adic伽罗瓦像在约束下能达到的最大规模需要显式刻画；此前相关结论多为隐式或一般性陈述，本文旨在将这一“一般性”显式化，便于后续计算与应用。

Method: 使用模曲线及其j-映射进行参数化与构造，并结合文献中近期关于同源图的分类结果，来确定约束条件下2-adic像的最大可能情形并给出具体曲线模型。

Result: 给出了具有有理2-同源且2-adic像尽可能大的非CM椭圆曲线的显式模型，并说明这些曲线是generic的，使该事实得到明确体现。

Conclusion: 通过模曲线、j-映射与同源图分类结果，文章显式实现了具有有理2-同源的非CM椭圆曲线中2-adic像最大的情形，明确了这类曲线的generic性质。

Abstract: Let $E$ be a non-CM elliptic curve defined over $\mathbb{Q}$. We present models of curves that have 2-adic image as large as possible, given the constraint that they have a rational 2-isogeny. These curves are known to be generic, and one of the aims of this article is to make that fact explicit. Our techniques make use of modular curves and their $j$-maps, as well as classification results on isogeny-graphs that have appeared in the literature recently.

</details>


### [114] [Integer group determinants for the elementary abelian group of order 49](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09542)
*Chatchawan Panraksa*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了群 $G=C_7\times C_7$ 的群行列式的整数值集合 $S(G)$ 中被 $7$ 整除的部分，给出了 valuation 为 $10$ 和 $11$ 时的充分必要条件，并指出最小正可分值是 $43\cdot 7^{10}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群行列式的整数值集合 $S(G)$ 是群表示论与代数数论交叉的经典对象。对于 $G=C_7\times C_7$，与 $7$ 互素的值已有结论，但被 $7$ 整除的值缺乏完整分类。本文旨在填补这一空白，刻画这些可分值的具体结构。

Method: 证明结合了两个主要工具：一是从特征值出发的整性重构（integral reconstruction from character values），二是模 $7$ 下的整体单位像（global-unit image modulo $7$）的计算。在此基础上，将可分值按 $7$-adic valuation 分层，并通过 $\mathbb{Z}[\zeta_7]$ 中理想的两种加性不变量来刻画 cofactor。

Result: 每个非零可分值的 valuation 至少为 $10$，且每个 $7^{12}$ 的倍数都会出现。在 valuation 为 $10$ 和 $11$ 时，给出 cofactor 的充要条件：第一条是有界的素理想不变量带符号和；第二条要求存在一个素理想，其不变量对非零且范数整除 cofactor。这两个 cofactor 集合都不是任何正模数的同余类之并。最小正可分值为 $43\cdot 7^{10}$，最小 valuation 为 $11$ 的正值为 $8\cdot 7^{11}$。

Conclusion: 本文完成了 $C_7\times C_7$ 群行列式可分值的刻画，特别是 valuation $10$、$11$ 两层。对于素数至少为 $11$ 的情形，作者识别出额外的局部条件与实现问题，留作后续研究。

Abstract: Let $G=C_7\times C_7$, and let $S(G)$ denote the set of integer values of its group determinant. We determine $S(G)$ by classifying the values divisible by $7$; the coprime values are already known. Every nonzero divisible value has valuation at least $10$, and every multiple of $7^{12}$ occurs. At valuations $10$ and $11$, we give necessary and sufficient conditions on the cofactor in terms of two additive invariants of ideals in $\mathbb{Z}[ζ_7]$. The first condition is a bounded signed sum of prime-ideal invariants. The second requires a prime ideal with nonzero invariant pair whose norm divides the cofactor. Neither cofactor set is a union of congruence classes modulo any positive integer. The least positive divisible value is $43\cdot7^{10}$, and the least positive value of valuation $11$ is $8\cdot7^{11}$. The proof combines integral reconstruction from character values with a calculation of the global-unit image modulo $7$. We conclude by identifying the additional local conditions and realization problems that arise at primes at least $11$.

</details>


### [115] [The three Kanade-Russell identities modulo nine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09653)
*Yuma Mizuno*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了三个对称的 Kanade–Russell 恒等式在模 9 意义下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kanade–Russell 恒等式是整数分拆理论中的重要恒等式，其对称形式在模算术意义下的同余性质具有研究价值，但此前缺乏关于模 9 的证明。

Method: 采用模 9 同余的方法，对三个对称的 Kanade–Russell 恒等式进行证明。

Result: 成功证明了三个对称的 Kanade–Russell 恒等式在模 9 下成立。

Conclusion: 该结果为 Kanade–Russell 恒等式的同余性质提供了新的模 9 层面的支持。

Abstract: We prove the three symmetric Kanade-Russell identities modulo nine.

</details>


### [116] [From Pythagorean Runs to Pell-Generated Cubic Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09714)
*Anatoly Eydelzon*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究一个三次方版的Pythagorean run问题：在第二个连续方块中允许公差为2，证明了存在无穷多正整数三元组(m,A,B)满足特定立方和等式，并给出由Pell型方程导出的显式无穷族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Pythagorean run（连续平方块之和等于紧随其后的连续平方块之和，Boardman构造对任意给定长度均成立）是数论中的经典问题。本文希望将其推广到三次方情形，并允许第二个块具有公差2，从而探索更一般的"三次analogue"。

Method: 将问题转化为求解满足B>A+2m-1的三元组(m,A,B)，使立方和等式成立。通过推导将条件归结为Pell型方程48329z²-156m²=161，再化为规范形式的广义Pell方程求解，得到显式无穷族；同时分析半长度序列满足的递推关系。

Result: 证明了存在无穷多正整数三元组(m,A,B)满足所给立方和等式；获得了由Pell型方程48329z²-156m²=161给出的显式无穷族；半长度序列满足显式的二阶线性递推。

Conclusion: 立方版Pythagorean run在允许第二个块公差为2时仍有无穷多解，且解可由广义Pell方程显式参数化，半长度呈二阶线性递推。这为经典Pythagorean run问题提供了三次方推广。

Abstract: A classical Pythagorean run is an identity in which a block of consecutive squares is equal to the immediately following block of consecutive squares. Boardman's construction gives such a run for every prescribed length. We consider a cubic analogue in which the second block is allowed to have common difference 2. We prove that there are infinitely many positive integer triples \((m,A,B)\), with \(B>A+2m-1\), such that
  $$ \sum_{j=0}^{2m-1}(A+j)^3 =
\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}(B+2j)^3. $$
  An explicit infinite family is obtained from the Pell-type equation
  $$ 48329z^2-156m^2=161, $$
  which reduces to a generalized Pell equation in normalized form. The ordering condition ensures that the step-2 progression begins strictly after the consecutive block ends. The resulting sequence of half-lengths satisfies an explicit second-order linear recurrence.

</details>


### [117] [An elementary approach to Sun Kim's general theta function identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09751)
*Dazhao Tang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出Kim一般theta函数恒等式的一个初等证明方法，在统一框架下得到这些恒等式的q级数证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ramanujan模方程与theta函数恒等式密切相关，Warnaar和Kim先后推广了相关恒等式，但这些一般恒等式需要一个统一且初等的证明框架。

Method: 本文采用初等方法处理Kim的一般theta函数恒等式，在统一的框架内利用q级数方法给出证明。

Result: 在共同框架下成功获得了Kim一般theta函数恒等式的q级数证明。

Conclusion: 本文为Kim的theta函数恒等式提供了初等的q级数证明，统一了相关恒等式的处理方式。

Abstract: Ramanujan's modular equations of degrees $3$, $5$, $7$, $11$ and $23$ are closely related to certain theta function identities. Warnaar generalized the identities arising from the modular equations of degrees $3$ and $7$ to a general theta function identity. Kim subsequently obtained a further general theta function identity associated with the modular equations of degrees $5$, $11$ and $23$, and established several general theta function identities containing known partition theorems as special cases. In this paper, we present an elementary approach to Kim's general theta function identities, yielding $q$-series proofs of these identities within a common framework.

</details>


### [118] [Universal norms and de Rham representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09862)
*Laurent Berger*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明分圆扩张的Iwasawa上同调中的泛范数结果可推广到任意包含分圆扩张的p-进扩张，并给出可应用于Lubin–Tate扩张的扭曲分圆变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将p-进分圆扩张上已建立的泛范数计算结果推广到更一般的p-进扩张情形，尤其是包含分圆扩张的扩张与Lubin–Tate扩张，从而扩大原有结果的适用范围。

Method: 通过论证由分圆扩张的Iwasawa上同调中泛范数的计算可以推出任意包含分圆扩张的p-进扩张的同类结果，并进一步证明一个扭曲分圆变体以覆盖Lubin–Tate扩张。

Result: 证明了分圆扩张泛范数结果向任意包含分圆扩张的p-进扩张的推广，并建立了适用于Lubin–Tate扩张的扭曲分圆变体。

Conclusion: 分圆情形的泛范数计算具有更强的普适性，可作为推导更一般p-进扩张（包括Lubin–Tate扩张）相应结论的基础。

Abstract: The purpose of this note is to show that the computation of the universal norms in Iwasawa cohomology for the $p$-adic cyclotomic extension implies the same result for any $p$-adic extension that contains the cyclotomic extension. In addition, we prove a twisted cyclotomic variant, which applies to Lubin--Tate extensions.

</details>


### [119] [Unramified Motivic Alternating Multiple Mixed Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09917)
*Ce Xu,Jianqiang Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究交替型多重混合值，利用Brown-Glanois的下降理论识别出五族非分歧的交替多重混合值，并猜想所有真正的交替非分歧值均已被列举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重zeta值的诸多变体中，多重混合值等仍为实数，其核心问题是判定这些值何时能"下降"到level one，即表示为多重zeta值的Q-线性组合（称为非分歧）。作者进一步研究其交替版本，旨在刻画非分歧的交替多重混合值。

Method: 采用Brown和Glanois的下降理论（descent theory），对多重混合值的交替推广形式进行分析。

Result: 识别出五族非分歧的交替多重混合值。

Conclusion: 作者猜想：所有非分歧的真正交替多重混合值均已在该文中给出。

Abstract: Many variants of the multiple zeta values have been studied in recent years. There are a few among them that remain to be real numbers, such as the multiple mixed values defined by the authors as level-two generalizations, which include both Hoffman's multiple $t$-values and Kaneko--Tsumura's multiple $T$-values. A central question is when such a value descends to level one, that is, when it can be expressed as a $\Q$-linear combination of multiple zeta values. We call these values unramified. In this paper, we further consider the alternating version of the above variants and identify five families of unramified alternating multiple mixed values using the descent theory of Brown and Glanois. We conjecture that all unramified truly alternating multiple mixed values are given in this paper.

</details>


### [120] [Non-vanishing of symmetric square $L$-functions in the weight aspect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09942)
*Olga Balkanova,Dmitry Frolenkov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了权方面对称平方L函数二阶矩的新渐近公式，推出至少76.69%的有界权全纯Hecke尖形式在中心点非零，改进了此前70.37%的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升对称平方L函数在中心点非零的全纯Hecke尖形式比例下界，改进已有结果（70.37%）。

Method: 建立权方面（weight aspect）上平均意义下对称平方L函数二阶矩的渐近公式。

Result: 得到新的二阶矩渐近公式，并由此给出中心点非零比例至少为76.69%（有界权全纯Hecke尖形式）。

Conclusion: 通过二阶矩的渐近估计建立非零性判据，将非零比例下界从70.37%提高至76.69%。

Abstract: We prove a new asymptotic formula for the second moment of symmetric square L-functions in the weight aspect on average. This result implies that the associated L-function is non-vanishing at the central point for at least 76.69% of holomorphic Hecke cusp forms of bounded weight, improving the previous bound of 70.37%.

</details>


### [121] [Pencils of norm form equations and a conjecture of Thomas, II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09995)
*Francesco Amoroso,David Masser,Umberto Zannier*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究参数范形式方程 $F_t(\mathbf{x})=q$ 的整数解，在较弱条件下证明解关于 $|q|$ 和 $t$ 多项式有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作 [Am-Ma-Za2] 仅对 $F_t(\mathbf{x})=1$ 给出有效特殊化结果，且依赖关键的指标假设。本文希望推广到任意整数 $q$，并放宽条件。

Method: 修改 [Am-Ma-Za2] 的技术，基于 [Am-Ma-Za] 中的丢番图逼近方法，对相关高度进行界定；不使用对数线性形式。

Result: 在温和条件（不含 [Am-Ma-Za2] 的关键指标假设）下，证明所有整数解 $\mathbf{x}$ 关于 $|q|$ 和 $t$ 多项式有界；并给出一个含八个变量的例子。

Conclusion: 结果强调对数线性形式方法可能无法给出此类多项式界，即使对两变量 Thue 方程；丢番图逼近途径更具优势。

Abstract: We continue our studies on parametric norm forms $F_t({\bf x})$, with ${\bf x}=(x_0,x_1,\ldots,x_{d-1})$ lying in some parametric linear subvariety $W_t$ and integers $t$ sufficiently large. In a previous paper [Am-Ma-Za2] we proved some effective specialization results for integer solutions $\bf x$ of $F_t({\bf x})=1$. Here we modify our techniques to treat $F_t({\bf x})=q$ for an arbitrary integer $q$. Under mild conditions (not however including the crucial index assumption in [Am-Ma-Za2]) we show that all $\bf x$ are polynomially bounded in terms of $|q|$ and $t$. As in [Am-Ma-Za2] we use the methods of our paper[Am-Ma-Za] based on diophantine approximation techniques to bound certain heights. In particular we do not use linear forms in logarithms and indeed it seems unlikely that those can lead to such polynomial bounds, even for Thue equations in two variables with $x_2=\cdots=x_{d-1}=0$. We present an example with eight variables.

</details>


### [122] [Diophantine approximation with primes in an arithmetic progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10010)
*D. Mazumder,J. Sivaraman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对于无理数 α，存在无穷多个素数 ℓ≡u mod v 使得 ‖αℓ−β‖ ≪ ℓ^(-1/4) log^8 ℓ；并构造了一个不可数集合 S，其中每个 γ 都有无穷多个素数 ℓ 满足 ‖γℓ‖<ℓ^(-1)，且证明并非 S 中所有元素都是 Liouville 数，从而回答了 Erdős 与 Mahler 1939 年的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Vaughan（1977）结果给出了 Λ(n) 与区间指示函数 f_Δ 偏差和的界，但未涉及素数落在指定同余类并同时接近给定无理倍数的联合分布问题。作者希望将 Vaughan 的估计推广到同余类情形，并进一步研究 ‖γℓ‖<ℓ^(-1) 这一更强的小数逼近条件，以回应 Erdős–Mahler 关于此类素数逼近集合是否全为 Liouville 数的长期问题。

Method: 首先建立关于 α 的 Diophantine 逼近条件 |αv − a/q| ≤ 1/q²、q>N^{1/4} 下的一般定理，给出带同余限制 n≡u mod v 的加权和 ∑Λ(n)(f_Δ(αn−β)−2Δ) 的上界，其中 f_Δ 为 ‖θ‖<Δ 的指示函数。然后取 Δ≈ℓ^{-1/4} 等参数将该一般定理作为推论推出素数逼近界。第二部分通过构造性方法（如利用连分数或 Cantor 型构造）显式给出不可数的 γ 集合 S，并借助无条件下界论证证明 S 不全由 Liouville 数组成。

Result: 主结果：对 α∈ℝ\ℚ、β∈ℝ，任意互素 u,v≥1，存在无穷多素数 ℓ≡u mod v，使得 ‖αℓ−β‖ ≪_v ℓ^{-1/4} log^8 ℓ。一般定理给出 ∑_{n≡u mod v} Λ(n)(f_Δ(αn−β)−2Δ) ≪_v (Nq^{-1/2}+N^{3/4}+N^{5/6}Δ^{1/2}+(ΔNq)^{1/2}+N^ε q Δ^{1−ε})𝓛^8，𝓛=log(Nq/Δ)，推广了 Vaughan 1977 的同余无关版本。第二部分构造出不可数集 S⊂ℝ\ℚ，使每个 γ∈S 都有无穷多素数 ℓ≡u mod v 满足 ‖γℓ‖<ℓ^{-1}；并证明 S 中并非全为 Liouville 数。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Vaughan 关于 Λ(n) 与区间指示函数偏差的估计推广到算术级数情形，并由此得到素数幂次小数逼近的显式上界；同时构造性地证明了存在不可数多个 γ 使 ‖γℓ‖<ℓ^{-1} 有无穷多素数解，且这些 γ 不全是 Liouville 数，从而对 Erdős–Mahler 1939 年提出的问题给出了无条件的部分回答。

Abstract: Let $α\in \mathbb{R} \setminus \mathbb{Q}$, $β\in \R$, $N \in \mathbb{R}_{\ge 1}$ and $ Δ\in (0, 1/2)$.
  For any real $y$, let $\|y\|$ denote the distance from $y$ to the nearest integer. In the first part of this paper, we show that given two coprime integers $u, v \ge 1$, there are infinitely many primes $\ell \equiv u \bmod v$ such that $
  \|α\ell - β\| \ll_v \ell^{-1/4} \log^{8} \ell. $
In order to prove this result we first prove the following general theorem and then deduce the above as a corollary. Before stating the result, let us define a function on $f_Δ(θ)$ on $\R$ such that $f_Δ(θ)$ is $ 1 \text{ if } \| θ\| < Δ$ and $ 0 $ otherwise. Further, suppose that $u, v \in \mathbb{Z}_{\ge 1}$ are coprime and $a$, $q \in \Z$ are coprime with $q> N^{1/4}$ and $|αv -a/q| \le 1/q^2$. Then, for every $ε\in \mathbb{R}_{>0}$ we have \begin{equation*} \sum_{\substack{n=1 \\ n \equiv u \bmod v}}^N Λ(n) (f_Δ(αn - β) - 2Δ) \ll_v (Nq^{-1/2} + N^{3/4} + N^{5/6}Δ^{1/2} + (ΔNq)^{1/2} + N^εq Δ^{1-ε}) \mathcal{L}^8 \end{equation*} where $\mathcal{L}=\log (Nq/Δ)$. This generalises a well known result of Vaughan from 1977 proving a similar bound for the sum \begin{equation*} \sum_{\substack{n=1}}^N Λ(n) (f_Δ(αn - β) - 2Δ). \end{equation*} In the second part of this paper, we explicitly construct an uncountable set $S \subset \R\setminus \Q$ such that for every $γ\in S$ there are infinitely many primes $\ell \equiv u \bmod v$ satisfying $\| γ\ell \| < \ell^{-1}$. Further we prove unconditionally that not all the elements of $S$ are Liouville numbers. This addresses a question of Erd{ö}s and Mahler from 1939.

</details>


### [123] [A Counterexample to a Conjecture of Liu and Qian and a Refined Inverse Theorem for Restricted Sumsets in $\mathbb{Z}_p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10148)
*Jiantao Li,Xinyi Liang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究循环群 Z_p 中受限和集 |A⊞B| 的 Alon–Nathanson–Ruzsa 下界等号取到时的逆问题，否定了 Liu–Qian 猜想，并在 |A|+|B|≤p−1 条件下证明了一个精化的逆定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alon–Nathanson–Ruzsa 定理给出了受限和集 |A⊞B|≥min{p, |A|+|B|−2} 的下界，但其等号情形（临界对）的逆问题自 1996 年提出以来仍未解决。Liu 和 Qian 在至少一个集合为等差数列的假设下解决了该逆问题，并对一般情形提出了猜想。本文旨在检验并修正该猜想。

Method: 首先构造反例，说明 Liu–Qian 猜想在边界情形 |A|+|B|=p 下不成立，从而表明原猜想在无额外限制时为假。随后在自然假设 |A|+|B|≤p−1 下，结合 Liu–Qian 的结果与一个新的计数论证，给出并证明一个精化的逆定理。

Result: 发现了 Liu–Qian 猜想在边界情形 |A|+|B|=p 下的反例，证明原猜想在无附加限制时错误；并在 |A|+|B|≤p−1 条件下成功建立精化的受限和集逆定理，刻画了所有临界对。

Conclusion: Liu–Qian 的一般性猜想需附加限制才成立；在 |A|+|B|≤p−1 的自然范围内，临界对可被完全刻画，从而推进了 Alon–Nathanson–Ruzsa 逆问题的解决。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime and let $A,B$ be nonempty subsets of the cyclic group $\mathbb{Z}_p$ with $|A|\neq |B|$. The Alon--Nathanson--Ruzsa theorem gives the lower bound
  \[
  |A\rplus B|\ge \min\{p,\,|A|+|B|-2\},
  \]
  where $A\rplus B=\{a+b:a\in A,\ b\in B,\ a\neq b\}$ is the restricted sumset. The inverse problem of characterizing all critical pairs $(A,B)$ attaining equality was posed by Alon, Nathanson, and Ruzsa in 1996 and remains open. Recently, Liu and Qian solved the inverse problem under the assumption that at least one of the sets is an arithmetic progression, and proposed a conjecture for the general case. In this paper, we first exhibit a counterexample to their conjecture, which arises in the boundary case $|A|+|B|=p$. This counterexample shows that the original conjecture is false without additional restrictions. Motivated by this, we formulate and prove a refined inverse theorem under the natural hypothesis $|A|+|B|\le p-1$. Our proof relies on the results of Liu and Qian together with a new counting argument.

</details>


### [124] [Inertial multiplicity bounds for two dimensional projective representations and bounds for the number of $\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_q)$ and $\operatorname{PGL}_2(\mathbb{F}_q)$ number fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10245)
*Brandon Alberts*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用Serre模性猜想（现为Khare–Wintenberger定理）证明了若干数域计数函数的上界，其结果可与固定或受限判别式下交换扩张数目的尖锐上界相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用经典方法（类域论）可以给固定或受限判别式下交换扩张的数目给出尖锐上界。作者希望将这类上界推广到非交换情形，而模性猜想正是类域论在此情形下的对应工具。

Method: 借助Serre模性猜想（Khare–Wintenberger定理），将其作为类域论的类比工具，用于控制数域计数函数，从而推导相关上界。

Result: 证明了某些数域计数函数的上界，其形式与交换扩张数目的已知尖锐上界相仿。

Conclusion: 模性猜想可以充当类域论的角色，为数域计数问题提供与交换情形相当的上界，显示出该定理在数域计数中的有效性。

Abstract: We prove upper bounds for certain number field counting functions using Serre's modularity conjecture (now a theorem of Khare--Wintenberger). These results are comparable to sharp upper bounds for the number of abelian extensions with fixed or bounded discriminant, with Serre's modularity conjecture playing the role of class field theory.

</details>


### [125] [Faltings' Isogeny Theorem via Equidistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10302)
*Junyi Xie,Ziquan Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出Faltings同源定理的一个新证明：利用Yuan的非阿基米德等分布定理，将同态与半单性从有限域"提升"到数域，从而将Faltings定理直接归约到Tate定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Faltings同源定理是算术几何与丢番图几何的核心结果，但其经典证明较复杂。作者希望寻找一条更简洁、结构更清晰的证明路径，将问题化归为已有的Tate定理。

Method: 采用Yuan的非阿基米德等分布定理作为关键工具，通过"pumping"（提升）技术将有限域上成立的同态（Galois等变同态）与半单性结论传递到数域情形，从而把Faltings定理的证明直接归约到Tate定理。

Result: 成功建立了Faltings同源定理与Tate定理之间的直接推导关系：数域上的同态与半单性可由有限域情形经非阿基米德等分布定理提升得到。

Conclusion: Yuan的非阿基米德等分布定理为Faltings同源定理提供了新的证明途径，说明该定理本质上是Tate定理在数域情形下的推论，凸显了等分布理论在算术几何中的深刻作用。

Abstract: We give a new proof of Faltings' isogeny theorem. More precisely, we show that Yuan's non-archimedean equidistribution theorem can be used to "pump" homomorphisms and semisimplicity from finite fields to number fields, thereby reducing Faltings' theorem directly to Tate's theorem.

</details>


### [126] [Quasi-modularity of symmetric quasi-shuffles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10309)
*Killian Hong-Minh,Sergey Mozgovoy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文建立了对称多重q-ζ值的拟模性代数框架，刻画出其q-ζ值张成一级拟模形式的对称拟shuffle类，并得到有限级拟模形式与显式对称化公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重zeta值与q-模拟的（拟）模性质是数论与组合中的核心问题，但对称多重q-ζ值的拟模性缺乏统一的代数框架，其与对称/拟对称函数的关系也不清晰。

Method: 构造对称拟shuffle的代数框架，识别特定自然类并计算其q-ζ值；利用对称化公式，建立与对称函数及拟对称函数的联系。

Result: 一类对称拟shuffle的q-ζ值恰好张成一级拟模形式代数；另一类给出有限级拟模形式；并获得显式对称化公式及与对称、拟对称函数的关系。

Conclusion: 对称拟shuffle为研究对称多重q-ζ值的拟模性提供了有效代数工具，统一了一级与有限级情形，并揭示其与（拟）对称函数的深层联系。

Abstract: We develop an algebraic framework for the quasi-modularity of symmetric multiple $q$-zeta values. We identify natural classes of symmetric quasi-shuffles whose $q$-zeta values exhaust the algebra of level-one quasi-modular forms, and further classes whose $q$-zeta values are quasi-modular forms of finite level. We also obtain explicit symmetrization formulas and relate symmetric quasi-shuffles to symmetric and quasisymmetric functions.

</details>


### [127] [Sharp local estimates for smooth numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10324)
*Ofir Gorodetsky*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了光滑数的尖锐局部估计，并推导出若干推论，包括无平方因子光滑数的最优估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光滑数（所有素因子不超过给定界的整数）在解析数论与算法数论中具有基础地位，但此前对其在短区间内的局部计数缺乏尖锐估计，尤其对无平方因子光滑数的最优刻画尚不完备。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 建立光滑数的尖锐局部（短区间）估计，并以此为核心工具，通过推导若干推论得到无平方因子光滑数的最优估计。摘要未披露具体技术手段，推测涉及解析数论中的筛法、ζ函数或Buchstab型函数估计等。

Result: 得到光滑数的尖锐局部估计；作为推论，获得无平方因子光滑数的最优估计。

Conclusion: 该工作为光滑数在局部范围内的分布提供了尖锐刻画，无平方因子光滑数的最优估计是其重要应用，可望用于相关计数问题与算法复杂度分析。

Abstract: We establish sharp local estimates for smooth numbers. Corollaries are derived, including optimal estimates for square-free smooth numbers.

</details>


### [128] [On the syntomic regulator of the Hesse cubic curves and $p$-adic hypergeometric functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10340)
*Yusuke Nemoto*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入一类新的 p 进超几何函数，满足类似 Dwork p 进超几何函数的同余关系，并用其特殊值表示 Hesse 三次曲线的 syntomic 调节子，同时给出该函数与 Asakura 定义的对数型 p 进超几何函数之间的变换公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: p 进超几何函数在 p 进算术几何中具有重要作用；Dwork 型函数已有同余性质，而对数型 p 进超几何函数（Asakura）与几何对象的调节子联系密切。作者希望构造一类新的 p 进超几何函数，以刻画 Hesse 三次曲线的 syntomic 调节子，并建立不同 p 进超几何函数之间的联系。

Method: 构造新的 p 进超几何函数；证明其满足与 Dwork p 进超几何函数类似的同余关系；计算 Hesse 三次曲线的 syntomic 调节子并将其表示为新函数的特殊值；推导新函数与 Asakura 对数型 p 进超几何函数之间的变换公式。

Result: 新定义的 p 进超几何函数满足类似 Dwork 函数的同余关系；Hesse 三次曲线的 syntomic 调节子可由该函数的特殊值表达；并建立了该函数与 Asakura 对数型 p 进超几何函数之间的变换公式。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了 p 进超几何函数的理论框架，揭示了新函数与 syntomic 调节子及已有对数型 p 进超几何函数之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We introduce a new type of $p$-adic hypergeometric function, which satisfies congruence relations similar to Dwork's $p$-adic hypergeometric function. Also, we prove that the syntomic regulators of the Hesse cubic curves are expressed in terms of the special values of our new $p$-adic hypergeometric functions. We also show that there is a transformation formula between our new $p$-adic hypergeometric function and a $p$-adic hypergeometric function of logarithmic type defined by Asakura.

</details>


### [129] [Integer group determinants for abelian groups of order 24](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10423)
*Chatchawan Panraksa*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了24阶三个交换群 C24、C2×C12、C2²×C6 的整数群行列式集合，通过特征分解给出构造性分类、有限可判定检验及无限族差异，并附算法证书与系数构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数群行列式集合的确定是群行列式理论中的基本问题，对24阶交换群此前缺乏完整的分类结果。作者希望给出可构造、可判定的完整描述，并厘清不同交换群之间行列式集合的关系。

Method: 利用特征分解把每个行列式写成割圆范数之积，并要求各分量满足整数相容性条件；通过导体商与保范整体单位进行有限检验，将非零带符号整数约化为2、3处的赋值条件加上剩余素因子的有限测试；对 C2²×C6 使用归一化有理—艾森斯坦对处理奇数与偶数层，并用目标稳定子缩减检验。

Result: 得到三个群的整数群行列式集合的构造性分类；每个素数贡献有一致上界（与重数无关）；关键循环层共享同一相容群；证明 C2²×C6 的奇行列式集合真包含于 C2×C12，给出其差集中一个无限族；确定三个集合中2的带符号幂，并附精确算术证书与系数构造。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了24阶交换群的整数群行列式集合，给出有限可判定算法与构造方法，揭示了不同交换群行列式集合之间的严格包含关系，为更高阶情形提供了方法范式。

Abstract: We determine the integer group determinant sets for $C_{24}$, $C_2\times C_{12}$ and $C_2^2\times C_6$, the three abelian groups of order $24$. Character factorization expresses each determinant as a product of cyclotomic norms, whose components must satisfy integral compatibility conditions. The classifications are constructive and reduce each nonzero signed integer to valuation conditions at $2$ and $3$ and a finite test on its remaining prime factors. The tests use conductor quotients and norm-preserving global units. Each prime contribution has a uniform bound independent of its multiplicity, and successful tests yield integer coefficients realizing the prescribed determinant. The critical cyclic strata share one compatibility group. For $C_2^2\times C_6$, a normalized rational--Eisenstein pair settles the odd values and most even strata; target stabilizers reduce the remaining tests. We also prove that the odd determinant set of $C_2^2\times C_6$ is properly contained in that of $C_2\times C_{12}$, exhibit an infinite family in the difference, and determine the signed powers of two in all three sets. Exact arithmetic certificates and coefficient constructors accompany the proofs.

</details>


### [130] [On the Number of Hecke Eigenvalues of Same Sign on $\mathrm{GL}_n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10446)
*Jesse Jääsaari*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究自对偶Hecke–Maass尖形式Hecke特征值$A(m,1,...,1)$的符号分布，证明在广义Ramanujan–Petersson猜想下，几乎所有短区间$[x,x+H]$内存在大小$\gg H(\log X)^{1/n^2-1}$的正号（及负号）子集，并对GL$_2$、GL$_3$给出无条件更强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究$\mathrm{SL}_n(\mathbb Z)$自对偶Hecke–Maass尖形式Hecke特征值的符号分布规律，特别是短区间内正负号特征值的出现密度，并改进长区间内同号特征值数目的已有上界。

Method: 在广义Ramanujan–Petersson猜想（或较弱形式的界）下，分析短区间$[x,x+H]$中$A(m,1,...,1)$的符号，构造大小$\gg H(\log X)^{1/n^2-1}$的正号子集$\mathcal S$与负号子集$\mathcal S'$，条件为$(\log X)^{1-1/n^2}\ll H\ll X$；对GL$_2$、GL$_3$采用无条件方法获得更强结论。

Result: 在GRP猜想下，对几乎所有$x\sim X$，区间$[x,x+H]$含大小$\gg H(\log X)^{1/n^2-1}$的正号子集与负号子集；对GL$_2$、GL$_3$无条件得到略强结果；在较弱的GRP界下亦获相应结论；并改进GL$_2$、GL$_3$长区间同号特征值数目的上界，以及$n\geq4$时GRP猜想下GL$_n$的相关上界。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了自对偶Hecke–Maass尖形式Hecke特征值在短区间内的符号分布，给出了正负号特征值子集大小的下界，并在GL$_2$、GL$_3$情形实现无条件改进，同时推广至较弱GRP界及长区间同号特征值计数。

Abstract: We study the distribution of signs of the (real-valued) Hecke eigenvalues $A(m,1,...,1)$ of self-dual Hecke--Maass cusp forms for the group $\mathrm{SL}_n(\mathbb Z)$, where $n\geq 2$ is an integer. Our main result establishes, under Generalised Ramanujan--Petersson Conjecture, that for almost all $x\sim X$ the short interval $[x,x+H]$ contains a subset $\mathcal S$ (resp. $\mathcal S')$ of size $\gg H(\log X)^{1/n^2-1}$ such that $A(m,1,...,1)$ is positive (resp. negative) for all $m\in\mathcal S$ (resp. $m\in\mathcal S'$), provided that $(\log X)^{1-1/n^2}\ll H\ll X$. We also prove a slightly stronger result unconditionally for $\mathrm{GL}_2$ and $\mathrm{GL}_3$ Hecke--Maass cusp forms. In addition, we obtain results under weaker bounds towards Generalised Ramanujan--Petersson Conjecture. Finally, as a by-product of our methods, we improve earlier bounds for the number of Hecke eigenvalues of same sign also in long intervals unconditionally for $\mathrm{GL}_2$ and $\mathrm{GL}_3$ Hecke--Maass cusp forms, and under Generalised Ramanujan--Petersson Conjecture for $\mathrm{GL}_n$ forms when $n\geq 4$.

</details>


### [131] [Irreducibility of truncations of the Catalan generating function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10526)
*Shin-ichiro Seki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了任意正整数 n，由前 n+1 个 Catalan 数构成的生成多项式在有理数域 ℚ 上不可约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Catalan 数序列满足组合递推，其部分和多项式（即 Catalan 数生成多项式的截断）的不可约性是一个自然且具挑战性的算术性质问题，与序列整除性、p-adic 性质及经典不可约性判别法相关。

Method: 利用 Catalan 数的显式表达式 C_k = (1/(k+1))·binom(2k,k)，结合数论中的不可约性判别工具（如 Eisenstein 判别法及其推广、Perron 判别法或模 p 约化与素因子分析），对多项式 C_0+C_1x+…+C_nx^n 的系数进行 p-adic 赋值分析。

Result: 对每个正整数 n，多项式 C_0+C_1x+…+C_nx^n 在 ℚ 上不可约。

Conclusion: Catalan 数截断生成多项式具有完全的不可约性，该结果为 Catalan 数的算术性质提供了新证据，并可推广至相关组合序列多项式的不可约性研究。

Abstract: We prove that, for every positive integer $n$, the polynomial $C_0+C_1x+\cdots+C_nx^n$ is irreducible over $\mathbb{Q}$, where $C_k$ denotes the $k$th Catalan number.

</details>

### [132] [A Stress-Based Estimator for Pressure and Stress Recovery from Velocity Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09484)
*Nicolás Barnafi,Benjamín Cisternas,Ernesto Castillo,Felipe Galarce*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从速度测量反演压力场的方法：基于Navier-Stokes方程的应力-速度一阶形式，先计算完整应力场，再以低成本后处理得到压力估计；并从理论上证明有限元近似的收敛性、应力恢复对有限分辨率速度测量的稳定性，数值实验验证其在对流流态与低空间分辨率下的鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从速度测量中非侵入式估计压力场是长期存在的工程难题，现有压力恢复策略在强对流流态和低空间分辨率下往往精度不足、鲁棒性欠缺。

Method: 采用Navier-Stokes方程的应力-速度一阶（混合）有限元形式，将压力估计与应力场求解解耦：先由速度测量恢复完整应力场，再通过廉价后处理提取压力；该形式在构造上可容纳测量速度场中偏离不可压缩性的误差。理论层面建立了有限元近似格式的收敛性，以及应力恢复相对有限分辨率速度测量的稳定性。

Result: 数值实验验证了所建立的理论；所提估计器在对流主导流态中表现稳健，且在空间分辨率降低时仍保持较高精度，性能优于当前最先进的压力恢复方法。

Conclusion: 将压力恢复转化为应力场求解加低成本后处理的策略，在理论收敛性与稳定性保障下，提供了兼顾精度、鲁棒性与计算效率的非侵入式压力估计方案。

Abstract: Non-invasive pressure field estimation from velocity measurements is a longstanding engineering problem. We propose, analyze, and test a pressure-recovery method that computes a full stress field from velocity measurements, and leaves the pressure estimation as a cheap post-processing step. The method relies on a stress-velocity first order formulation of the Navier-Stokes equations, and we show that the formulation accounts for deviations from incompressibility in the measured velocity field by construction. In addition, we theoretically establish the convergence of the finite element (FE) approximation scheme, the stability of the stress recovery with respect to finite-resolution velocity measurements, and then validate this theory numerically. Our results show that the proposed estimator is robust in convective flow regimes and remains accurate at reduced spatial resolution, improving upon state-of-the-art pressure-recovery strategies.

</details>


### [133] [Constructive Tchakaloff results and well-conditioned quadrature through randomized least squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09506)
*Filip Bělík,Akil Narayan,John Turnage*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文利用随机最小二乘构造在函数子空间上精确的求积规则，引入“相对可容许性”和“参考权重集中”两个新条件，证明随机权重以任意高概率集中在渐近值附近，从而给出有限样本稳定性概率界，推广了现有随机最小二乘求积分析，并在理论上可期望正求积规则时导出其存在性结果，向计算可构造的广义Tchakaloff定理迈出实质性一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统（广义）Tchakaloff定理多为存在性结果，缺乏计算可构造性；同时随机最小二乘求积构造的现有分析适用范围有限，且缺少有限样本下的稳定性保证。该文旨在通过概率集中分析，为随机最小二乘求积规则提供更一般、更具构造性的稳定性保证。

Method: 采用随机最小二乘方法构造在给定函数子空间上精确的求积规则；提出“相对可容许性”（relative admissibility）与“参考权重集中”（reference weight concentration）两个新条件；基于这些条件分析随机权重的集中行为，并推导有限样本概率界。

Result: 证明随机最小二乘所得求积权重以预设且任意大的概率集中在渐近值附近；由此得到对非常一般的（可能复值）函数类求积规则稳定性的有限样本概率界；这些结果在理论上可期望正求积规则时特化为其存在性结果。

Conclusion: 该分析显著推广了现有随机最小二乘求积构造的理论，提供了新的稳定性界，并因过程具有算法形式，构成朝向计算可构造的广义Tchakaloff定理的实质性进展。

Abstract: We consider using randomized least squares to construct quadrature rules exact on a subspace of functions. Using new conditions that we call relative admissibility and reference weight concentration, we establish both that the quadrature weights from such a procedure concentrate close to their asymptotic values with prescribed and arbitrarily large probability, and this in turn provides useful finite-sample probabilistic bounds on the stability of the resulting quadrature rules for very general classes of possibly complex-valued functions. Our analysis both significantly generalizes the existing analysis of randomized least squares quadrature construction, and provides new bounds on stability for these rules. These results specialize to existence results for positive quadrature rules, when such rules can be theoretically expected. Because our procedures are formally algorithmic, our analysis is a substantive advance toward computationally constructive generalized Tchakaloff theorems.

</details>


### [134] [A Convergent Reaction-Drift-Diffusion Master Equation with Interaction Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09546)
*Max Heldman,Samuel A. Isaacson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 将收敛反应-扩散主方程(CRDME)框架推广到非结构网格上并引入两体相互作用势，构造了新的空间离散跳跃过程，数值验证其保持平衡与细致平衡且具二阶收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有CRDME框架无法处理粒子间相互作用（如静电排斥、体积排斥、范德华吸引），而这些相互作用对相应系统的建模至关重要，因此需要将CRDME扩展至含两体势的情形。

Method: 对粒子型随机反应-漂移-扩散模型的前向方程弱形式进行离散：空间输运项采用边平均有限元法结合质量集中求积，反应项采用有限体积与质量集中求积的混合近似，从而导出粒子在对偶网格体元间跳跃并发生反应的转移速率，得到新的CRDME。

Result: 所得空间离散CRDME在封闭区域上保持连续系统的关键物理性质：无反应时保持Gibbs-Boltzmann平衡分布，可逆反应系统中漂移-扩散通量与反应通量均满足细致平衡；与Brownian dynamics模拟对比验证了方法的正确性，并展示了随网格加密的实证二阶收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将CRDME推广到含两体相互作用势的非结构网格情形，所构造格式物理性质保持良好、与粒子模拟吻合且具二阶精度，为相互作用粒子系统的随机模拟提供了可靠离散框架。

Abstract: We extend the convergent reaction-diffusion master equation (CRDME) framework on unstructured grids to include two-body interaction potentials between particles. Such interactions are essential for modeling systems in which particles experience electrostatic repulsion, volume exclusion, or van der Waals attraction.
