Our approach discretizes the weak form of the forward equation for a particle-based stochastic reaction- drift-diffusion model to obtain a new CRDME by combining two complementary techniques: an edge- averaged finite element method with mass-lumping quadrature for spatial transport terms that include potential interactions, and a mix of finite volume and mass-lumping quadrature approximations for reaction terms. This yields transition rates for a spatial jump process of particles hopping between dual mesh voxels and undergoing reactions. The resulting spatially discrete CRDME retains key physical properties of the continuum system in closed domains. It preserves the Gibbs-Boltzmann equilibrium distribution in the absence of reactions, and satisfies detailed balance of both drift-diffusion and reactive fluxes for systems with reversible reactions. Numerical comparisons with Brownian dynamics simulations validate our method, and we demonstrate empirical second-order convergence as the mesh is refined.

</details>


### [135] [A Note on the Point-Clothoid Distance Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10179)
*Haibin Ye,Hao Ge,Gong Cheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 分析回旋曲线（clothoid）上最近点的经典算法候选筛选逻辑的完备性，证明其在一维多极小情形下仍然正确，并据此优化搜索流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算回旋曲线上离查询点最近的点是几何设计、道路与铁路线形设计及路径规划中的常见任务。Frego与Bertolazzi提出的高效算法被广泛使用，但其候选点筛选分析假设每个搜索区间内至多只有一个局部极小值。本文指出存在包含两个局部极小值的合法配置，质疑原策略是否覆盖所有可能的最小值，因此需要重新审视并证明其完备性。

Method: 借助回旋曲线渐屈线（evolute）的几何性质，对任意查询点以及任意无拐点、切角变化不超过 $2\pi$ 的平面回旋曲线段，分析距离平方函数的驻点个数与类型，从而证明候选筛选逻辑的完备性；并据此推导在两端点导数检验均不激活时无需进行内部搜索的结论，保留数值回退机制。

Result: 证明距离平方函数至多有三个驻点；若三者均为局部极值，其顺序必为“极小—极大—极小”（min-max-min）。这表明原候选筛选逻辑在一维多极小情形下仍完备。同时得出：当两端点导数检验均不激活时无需内部搜索，可省略不必要的区间中点搜索。数值实验显示迭代次数与计算时间均有降低。

Conclusion: 回旋曲线最近点算法的候选筛选逻辑在一维情形下具有完备性，无需依赖“至多一个局部极小”的假设；并可通过省略多余中点搜索来加速计算，同时保留数值回退以保证稳健性。

Abstract: Computing the closest point on a clothoid is a recurring task in geometric design, road and railway alignment, and path planning. The efficient algorithm of Frego and Bertolazzi addresses this problem, but its candidate-selection analysis assumes at most one local minimum per search interval. We exhibit admissible configurations with two local minima, raising the question of whether the existing strategy accounts for every possible minimum. Using the geometry of the clothoid evolute, we prove that, for any query point and any proper no-inflection planar clothoid segment with tangent-angle variation at most $2π$, the squared-distance function has at most three stationary points; if all three are local extrema, their order is min-max-min. This establishes the completeness of the original candidate-selection logic beyond the one-minimum premise. It also shows that no interior search is needed when neither endpoint derivative test is active, allowing unnecessary midpoint searches to be omitted while retaining numerical fallback. Numerical experiments demonstrate reductions in iteration count and evaluation time.

</details>


### [136] [An intrinsic finite element framework for scalar- and vector-valued partial differential equations on general manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09592)
*Tamara A. Tambyah,Alberto F. Martín,David Lee,Santiago Badia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种内在有限元框架，在流形图谱参数空间内构建任意阶de Rham复形协调空间，通过拓扑粘合与传输映射实现跨图连续性，并验证其几何一致性、效率与守恒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形上偏微分方程数值方法多依赖外在嵌入或单一图卡，易引入几何一致性误差、跨图坐标不兼容问题，且装配代价高。本文旨在建立完全在参数空间表达、几何仅通过度量张量进入的内在有限元方法。

Method: 将弱形式与离散化完全建立于流形的平坦参数空间，精确几何仅经求积点处度量张量进入。在多图图谱上通过纯拓扑的跨图粘合获得整个de Rham复形的任意阶协调有限元空间；针对多图上的矢量值空间构造节点基变换，借助传输映射耦合坐标系不兼容的相邻图卡，使节点自由度跨图相连。

Result: 跨图连续性在节点处成立，跳跃量为近似误差量级，所得离散场精确切于流形。表面Stokes问题数值观察到最优收敛率（未给出先验误差分析）。二维与三维cubed sphere上Hodge Laplacian实验表明：足够求积阶数下方法无几何一致性误差，且内在装配显著低于外在装配代价。含地形作为几何扰动的旋转浅水方程显示质量严格守恒，能量守恒至时间离散误差量级。

Conclusion: 该内在有限元框架可在一般图谱流形上统一处理标量与矢量方程，避免几何一致性误差并降低装配成本，同时保持质量精确守恒与能量近似守恒，适用于表面Stokes、Hodge Laplacian与旋转浅水方程等问题；但缺乏先验误差分析。

Abstract: We present an intrinsic finite element framework for the numerical approximation of scalar- and vector-valued partial differential equations on general manifolds described by atlases of charts. Weak formulations and their discretisation are expressed exclusively in the flat parametric space of the manifold, where the exact geometry enters only through the metric tensor that is evaluated at quadrature points. Conforming finite element spaces of arbitrary order for the whole de Rham complex are obtained on multi-chart atlases through purely topological inter-chart gluing. We develop a nodal change of basis construction for vector-valued spaces on multi-chart atlases, where nodal degrees of freedom couple charts with incompatible coordinate systems through transmission maps. Inter-chart continuity holds at the nodes with jumps of the order of the approximation error, and the resulting discrete field is exactly tangent to the manifold. Optimal convergence rates for this method are observed numerically for the surface Stokes problem; no a priori error analysis is provided. Numerical experiments on the cubed sphere manifold in two and three dimensions for the Hodge Laplacian problems demonstrate the intrinsic framework is free of the geometric consistency errors for a sufficient quadrature degree, and that intrinsic assembly is substantially cheaper than its extrinsic counterpart. Further considering the rotating shallow water equations that include orography as a perturbation of the geometry demonstrates that mass is conserved exactly, and energy is conserved up to the time discretisation error.

</details>


### [137] [Uniform Chebyshev approximations of functions satisfying a variation-type condition: reconstruction of time-varying signals on graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09792)
*Davide Bianchi,Sandra Saliani,Dimitrios Vavitsas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了二元连续函数Chebyshev展开的一致逼近定理，并将其应用于时变图信号的光谱图小波变换，给出了稳定的重构误差界，数值实验验证了理论与去噪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变信号在有限加权图上的光谱图小波变换通常要求小波核不随时间变化，而实际时变信号可能需要随时间变化的核；同时，直接对二元核函数进行展开会带来逼近与重构的稳定性问题，缺乏统一的一致性逼近理论保证。因此需要建立Chebyshev展开的一致逼近结果，并将其用于时变图小波变换的构造与稳定性分析。

Method: 在方形区域[-1,1]^2上引入变差型条件，证明连续函数可按第一变量做一致收敛的Chebyshev展开，且展开系数为第二变量的连续函数。将此展开应用于光谱图小波变换的尺度核与小波核，得到系数随时间变化但次数不依赖时间的逼近。分析逼近变换与其伴随算子的复合，验证其系数公式与时间无关情形一致，并利用伪逆重构，导出基于一致核误差的显式稳定性界。在传感器网络数据上进行数值实验，并在去噪任务中采用时变变换参数的软阈值方法。

Result: 在变差型条件下，连续二元函数的Chebyshev展开一致收敛且系数连续；逼近变换与伴随的复合保持与时间无关情形相同的显式系数公式；伪逆重构稳定，并给出由一致核误差控制的显式误差界。传感器网络上的数值实验验证了收敛性和稳定性估计；在去噪问题中，采用时变变换参数的软阈值图小波系数方法优于时间无关的固定参数方法。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了时变光谱图小波变换的一致性逼近与稳定性理论，说明时间变化核可以在不增加展开次数的前提下被有效逼近，并保持重构公式与稳定性；时变参数软阈值在去噪中具有实际优势。

Abstract: We prove uniform approximation theorems for Chebyshev expansions of continuous functions of two variables. Under a variation-type condition on the square $[-1,1]^2$, a continuous function admits a uniformly convergent Chebyshev expansion in the first variable whose coefficients are continuous functions of the second variable. These results are applied to the spectral graph wavelet transform of time-varying signals on finite weighted graphs: the scaling and wavelet kernels are approximated by expansions with time-varying coefficients whose degrees do not depend on time, the composition of the approximate transform with its adjoint admits the same explicit coefficient formulas as in the time-independent case, and reconstruction by the pseudoinverse is stable, with explicit bounds in terms of the uniform kernel errors. Numerical experiments on a sensor network confirm the convergence and stability estimates. In a denoising problem, soft thresholding of the graph wavelet coefficients with a time-varying transform parameter improves over its time-independent, fixed-parameter counterpart.

</details>


### [138] [Initialization-dependent BFGS trial rates for tilted absolute values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09872)
*Qiuyu Chen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文否定了Lewis与Overton (2008) 关于一维倾斜绝对值函数上BFGS算法具有与初值无关的试验归一化收敛率的猜想，构造了反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lewis和Overton在2008年猜想：对一维倾斜绝对值函数应用BFGS并配合其Armijo–Wolfe线搜索时，每个不终止执行的试验归一化收敛率仅由倾斜参数决定，与初始数据无关。本文旨在检验并反驳这一断言。

Method: 将线搜索过程归约为一个无尺度状态，该状态仅由迭代点符号与穿越零点的步长构成；随后用初等有理不等式验证两个周期性状态循环，从而对显式开区间内的每个倾斜参数构造出两个不同收敛率的不终止执行。

Result: 证明初始化无关的收敛率断言为假：对显式开区间内每个倾斜参数，均构造出两个具有不同尖锐试验归一化收敛率的不终止执行，给出一族显式反例。

Conclusion: 即便在这一经典一维模型中，非光滑BFGS的渐近试验归一化行为也可能本质上依赖于初始化，说明Lewis–Overton的收敛率猜想不成立。

Abstract: Lewis and Overton conjectured in 2008 that, for BFGS applied to the one-dimensional tilted absolute value with their Armijo--Wolfe line search, every nonterminating execution converges at a trial-normalized rate determined only by the tilt parameter and independent of the initial data. We prove that this initialization-independent rate assertion is false. In fact, for every tilt parameter in an explicit open interval, we construct two nonterminating executions with different sharp trial-normalized convergence rates. The proof reduces the line search to a scale-free state consisting of the iterate sign and the zero-crossing stepsize, and verifies two periodic state cycles by elementary rational inequalities. This yields an explicit family of counterexamples and shows that the asymptotic trial-normalized behavior of nonsmooth BFGS can depend essentially on the initialization even for this canonical one-dimensional model.

</details>


### [139] [The geometric convergence of a parallel domain decomposition method on manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09911)
*Lizhen Qin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为流形上椭圆方程的连续区域分解方法建立了收敛理论，证明了其几何收敛性，并给出了多种收敛速率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方法最初在欧氏区域情形下被提出，后由他人推广至流形上。其收敛性虽已知，但是否为几何收敛（即收敛速率是否为几何衰减）一直未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对流形上椭圆方程的连续区域分解方法进行理论分析，建立收敛理论框架，并据此推导几何收敛性及收敛速率估计。

Result: 证明了该方法的几何收敛性，并给出了关于收敛速率的多种估计（various estimates on the convergence rate）。

Conclusion: 解决了流形上连续区域分解方法几何收敛性这一开放问题，为其收敛速率提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: This paper establishes a convergence theory for a continuous domain decomposition method for elliptic equations on manifolds. This method originated in \cite{lions2} in the setting of Euclidean domains and was later adapted and generalized to manifolds by \cite{qin_wang_wang}. Although its convergence was known, whether the convergence is geometric has remained open. We prove its geometric convergence and provide various estimates on the convergence rate.

</details>


### [140] [Order Reduction of Exponential Runge--Kutta Methods: Fourth-Order Schemes for Non-Commuting Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09932)
*Thi Tam Dang,Pablo Alexei Gazca-Orozco,Trung Hau Hoang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将线性抛物问题中显式指数Runge–Kutta方法的收敛性分析从三阶推广到四阶，揭示了非交换情形下的刚性阶数约化现象，数值验证观测阶约2.75。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式指数Runge–Kutta方法用于求解线性抛物问题 $u'(t)+Au(t)=Bu(t)$（$A$ 生成解析半群，$B$ 相对 $A$ 有界）时，当 $A$ 与 $B$ 不交换会出现刚性阶数约化。已有分析仅覆盖至三阶，四阶格式的收敛性理论尚不完善，因此需要将分析框架推广到四阶。

Method: 建立全局误差递推关系，并扩展基于缺陷（defect）的分析框架，用以识别在 $A$ 与 $B$ 不交换时导致刚性阶数约化的各项；随后在非交换的对流–扩散问题上进行数值实验验证理论结果。

Result: 数值实验中，Krogstad 以及 Strehmel & Weiner 的经典四阶段四阶格式观测到的收敛阶约为 2.75，与收敛性分析给出的理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 四阶显式指数Runge–Kutta方法在非交换线性抛物问题中会发生刚性阶数约化，实际收敛阶低于名义四阶；提出的误差递推与缺陷分析框架能够准确预测该约化阶数。

Abstract: This paper extends the convergence analysis of explicit exponential Runge--Kutta methods for linear parabolic problems $u'(t) + Au(t) = Bu(t)$, where $A$ generates an analytic semigroup and $B$ is relatively bounded with respect to $A$, from the third-order case to fourth-order schemes. By establishing the global error recursion relation and extending the defect-based analytical framework, we identify the terms responsible for stiff order reduction when $A$ and $B$ do not commute. Numerical experiments are performed on a non-commuting advection-diffusion problem to validate the theoretical results. Numerical tests using the classical four-stage, fourth-order schemes of Krogstad and Strehmel \& Weiner exhibit an observed convergence order of approximately 2.75, which matches the theoretical prediction from the convergence analysis.

</details>


### [141] [Convergence of a single-ensemble multilevel scheme for McKean-Vlasov SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10000)
*Arne Bouillon,Giovanni Samaey*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为Ricketson (2015)提出的单集合多层蒙特卡洛方法提供了严格的误差-成本分析，证明全局Lipschitz条件下可达O(ε)的L^p误差与O(ε^{-2-δ})的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程数值求解需在时间与解分布两处离散，成本叠加难以承受。多层蒙特卡洛可加速，但Ricketson的单集合方案因跨层粒子交互引入相关性，此前仅能在线性漂移、确定性扩散、R为恒等的模型问题上完成成本-误差分析，缺乏一般性理论保证。

Method: 在单集合多层蒙特卡洛框架中引入额外粗粒子，通过以几何速率衰减的耦合误差逐层向粗层约束，控制跨层交互引入的相关性；在漂移、扩散项及R仅满足全局Lipschitz条件下对方案进行L^p误差-成本分析。

Result: 证明对任意p≥2与δ>0，可实现O(ε)的L^p误差，成本为O(ε^{-2-δ})（常数随δ→0而增长）。探索性实验与理论速率一致，并表明实际中小δ时常数增长不显著。

Conclusion: 该工作将单集合多层蒙特卡洛的成本-误差分析从线性模型推广至一般Lipschitz系数情形，方法与前证策略或可应用于其他单集合多层方案，如多层集合Kalman滤波。

Abstract: Numerically solving McKean-Vlasov stochastic differential equations is computationally challenging due to the compounding costs of discretizing in time and in the distribution of the solution. Multilevel ideas have been proposed to provide speed-ups. In this work, we study the multilevel Monte Carlo method proposed by Ricketson (2015) for equations whose drift and diffusion terms depend on the law of the solution $X_t$ through the expectation $E[R(X_t)]$. The scheme follows the single-ensemble paradigm, where particles interact across levels at each timestep. While cross-level feedback makes this scheme attractive in practice, the correlations it introduces have so far confined its cost-error analysis to a model problem with linear drift, deterministic diffusion, and $R$ the identity. We use additional coarse particles to enforce geometrically decaying coupling errors towards the coarser levels. This allows us to prove our main contribution, an $L^p$-error of $O(ε)$ at cost $O(ε^{-2-δ})$ for any $p\ge2$ and $δ>0$ (with a constant that grows as $δ\to0$), assuming only global Lipschitz bounds on the drift, the diffusion, and $R$. An exploratory experiment is consistent with the derived rates and suggests that in practice the constant does not grow significantly for small $δ$. Our methodology and proof strategy may also be useful for other single-ensemble multilevel schemes, such as multilevel ensemble Kalman filters.

</details>


### [142] [A Thermodynamically Consistent Model for Multicomponent Vesicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10168)
*Wangbo Luo,Zhonghua Qiao,Yanxiang Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出多组分膜的热力学一致扩散界面模型，基于Onsager变分原理导出耦合L²梯度流，构造稳定的交替ETD1与交替Strang-ETDRK2格式并证明离散能量耗散，数值实验验证二维与三维的膜变形及蛋白偏析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分生物膜涉及蛋白依赖的弯曲弹性、表面张力、体积约束及相分离等多种耦合效应，需要热力学一致且能量稳定的建模与高效数值方法。

Method: 从耦合自由能泛函（含蛋白依赖弯曲弹性、扩散表面张力、体积罚项及膜相关Ohta–Kawasaki能量）出发，应用Onsager变分原理导出耦合L²梯度流系统及其能量耗散律；构造稳定化交替ETD1格式与基于Strang型复合的稳定化交替ETDRK2格式（交替Strang-ETDRK2）。

Result: 在数值解的一定正则性假设下，证明了两种格式的离散能量耗散；二维与三维数值实验验证了离散能量耗散律，并再现了模型产生的蛋白偏析与膜变形。

Conclusion: 所提模型与数值格式在理论和实验上均满足能量稳定性；交替Strang-ETDRK2格式为耦合相场系统提供了新的高效稳定求解方案。

Abstract: We develop a thermodynamically consistent diffuse-interface model for multicomponent membranes. The proposed model is derived from a coupled free energy functional that incorporates protein-dependent bending elasticity, diffuse surface tension, a volume penalty, and a membrane-associated Ohta--Kawasaki energy. Applying the Onsager variational principle, we derive a coupled $L^2$ gradient flow system and its energy dissipation law. We then construct a stabilized alternating ETD1 scheme and a stabilized alternating ETDRK2 scheme with Strang-type composition (alternating Strang-ETDRK2). To the best of our knowledge, the proposed alternating Strang-ETDRK2 scheme has not previously been developed and analyzed for coupled phase field systems. We further prove the discrete energy dissipation for both schemes under some regularity assumptions on the numerical solutions. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions validate the discrete energy dissipation law, and present the protein segregation and membrane deformation produced by the proposed model.

</details>


### [143] [Stochastic Lanczos Quadrature for Computational Uncertainty in Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10080)
*Hassan Fifen,Issa Karambal,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 单次Lanczos运行在给出无矩阵逆矩阵近似的同时，还天然产出一个关于精确结果的高斯分布，其协方差刻画了截断所丢弃的正交补空间，并随步数增加而收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模科学计算（如贝叶斯推断）中，对对称正定矩阵求逆作用于向量是常见操作。Lanczos算法仅凭矩阵-向量乘积即可实现无矩阵近似，但固定步数截断后，探索到的Krylov子空间之外（即正交补空间）被完全忽略，而那里恰含最小特征值，对逆矩阵贡献最大。这种截断通常被当作无声的数值误差，而非可度量的不确定性。

Method: 作者证明单次Lanczos运行可同时产出无矩阵逆近似与关于精确结果的高斯分布，其协方差度量截断所弃信息并在步数增长时集中。协方差分两部分恢复正交补：(1) 截断边界处的秩一耦合项，由Lanczos残差精确给出；(2) 剩余主体上的各向同性项，其规模由投影随机Lanczos求积（Projected Stochastic Lanczos Quadrature）估计，所有探针复用同一Krylov基。

Result: 理论上证明了该分布的一致性：偏差以指数速率随求积深度衰减，方差以蒙特卡洛速率收敛。

Conclusion: 截断误差可被重新解释为可量化的后验不确定性，从而在单次Lanczos框架下同时获得逆矩阵近似及其可信度度量。

Abstract: Applying a matrix function to a vector is a common operation in large-scale scientific computing, such as Bayesian inference. When the matrix is symmetric positive definite and the function is its inverse, the Lanczos algorithm gives a matrix-free approximation from matrix-vector products alone. Stopping after a fixed number of steps keeps the approximation on the explored Krylov subspace but assigns nothing to its orthogonal complement, which holds the smallest eigenvalues and therefore the largest contributions to the inverse. This truncation is usually treated as a silent numerical error rather than as measurable uncertainty. We show that a single Lanczos run yields both a matrix-free approximation of the inverse and a Gaussian distribution over the exact result, whose covariance measures what the truncation discarded and concentrates as the number of steps grows. The covariance restores the complement in two parts: a rank-one coupling at the truncation boundary, given exactly by the Lanczos residual, and an isotropic term on the remaining bulk, whose size we estimate by Projected Stochastic Lanczos Quadrature, reusing one Krylov basis across all probes. We prove consistency, with bias decaying exponentially in the quadrature depth and variance at the Monte Carlo rate

</details>


### [144] [Analytical properties of polygonal Stokes and BGG Hessian complexes with application to Kirchhoff--Love plates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10150)
*Daniele A. Di Pietro,Jérôme Droniou,Kaibo Hu,Arax Leroy*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一种求解Kirchhoff–Love板问题的新型任意阶方法，适用于一般多边形网格，基于BGG构造得到的离散Hessian复形，证明了强制性与$k+1$阶收敛，并用数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Kirchhoff–Love板问题在一般多边形网格上构造高阶、稳定的数值方法，需要合适的离散Hessian复形作为工具，因此发展新的复形构造与分析框架。

Method: 利用Bernstein–Gelfand–Gelfand (BGG)构造获得离散Hessian复形，其结构是将Stokes复形叠加在张量化的de Rham复形之上；重点研究其中的Stokes复形，建立原始与伴随一致性估计及一致Poincaré不等式，据此推导离散Hessian复形的分析性质，进而构造并分析板问题离散格式。

Result: 所提方法具有强制性，收敛阶为$k+1$（$k\ge 0$为复形的多项式次数），多边形网格上的数值实验证实了理论收敛率。

Conclusion: 该工作通过BGG构造的离散Hessian复形建立了一套完整分析框架，为Kirchhoff–Love板问题提供了任意阶、多边形网格上最优收敛的离散方法。

Abstract: We develop and analyse a new arbitrary-order method for the Kirchhoff--Love plate problem on general polygonal meshes. The construction relies on a discrete Hessian complex obtained through the Bernstein--Gelfand--Gelfand construction, which stacks a Stokes complex on top of a tensorised de Rham complex. A key ingredient of the analysis is a detailed study of the former. We specifically establish primal and adjoint consistency estimates, as well as uniform Poincaré inequalities, and use these results to derive the analytical properties of the resulting discrete Hessian complex. The proposed method is then shown to be coercive and to converge with order $k+1$ with respect to the mesh size, where $k\ge 0$ is the polynomial degree of the complex. Numerical experiments on polygonal meshes confirm the theoretical convergence rates.

</details>


### [145] [Boundary-Level-Constrained Refinement for Suppressing Trimming-Induced High-Frequency Outliers in Explicit Isogeometric Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10161)
*Christoph Hollweck,Lukas Leidinger,Stefan Hartmann,Marcus Wagner,Roland Wüchner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 在等几何分析显式动力学中，开放节点向量与裁剪边界附近的降支撑基函数会产生高频离群值，限制临界时间步；作者提出边界层级约束细化(BLCR)策略，使最大特征频率由未裁剪内部基函数控制，从而抑制裁剪导致的时间步惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式动力学中临界时间步由半离散系统的最大特征频率决定。IGA中开放节点向量带来谱边界效应，产生高频离群值并限制可容许时间步。已有的通过扩展计算域并裁剪外部边界函数的方法只能缓解而不能保证消除该惩罚；作者希望厘清裁剪致时间步惩罚的机理并加以控制。

Method: 采用行和集中(row-sum-lumped)质量矩阵设置；分析裁剪边界附近降支撑基函数对最大特征频率的贡献，指出即便节点精确裁剪(无小切割单元)，边界相邻基函数仍可能是临界谱贡献者。进而提出Boundary-Level-Constrained Refinement (BLCR)，一种适用于LR-和THB样条的局部细化约束，约束其支撑与裁剪边界相交的基函数的细化层级相对于内部细化层级。

Result: 节点精确裁剪与任意裁剪是同一支撑缩减机理下的不同程度。在本文研究的所有配置中，BLCR约束确保最大特征频率由未裁剪的内部基函数控制，而非细化后的裁剪基函数；由此抑制了控制临界时间步的裁剪致高频离群值，在相同内部分辨率下获得比全局细化裁剪B样条更大的可容许时间步。

Conclusion: 裁剪致时间步惩罚并不依赖于小切割单元的存在；BLCR为LR-与THB样条提供了一种有效的局部细化约束，可抑制裁剪产生的高频离群值并提升显式分析的可容许时间步。

Abstract: In explicit dynamics, the critical time step is governed by the maximum eigenfrequency of the semi-discrete system. In Isogeometric Analysis (IGA), open knot vectors introduce a characteristic spectral boundary effect that can lead to high-frequency outliers and restrict the admissible time step. In the row-sum-lumped setting considered here, extending the computational patch and trimming away the exterior boundary functions mitigates this time-step penalty, but does not necessarily prevent boundary-adjacent basis functions from governing the maximum eigenfrequency. We show that reduced-support basis functions adjacent to the trimming boundary can remain critical spectral contributors even for knot-exact trimming, i.e., in the absence of small cut cells. Hence, small cut cells are not required for trimming-induced time-step penalties. Knot-exact and arbitrary trimming represent different degrees of support reduction within the same underlying mechanism. To control this effect, we propose the Boundary-Level-Constrained Refinement (BLCR) strategy, a local refinement constraint for LR- and THB-splines. BLCR constrains the refinement level of basis functions whose support intersects the trimming boundary relative to that of the refined interior. In all configurations investigated in this work, this constraint ensures that the maximum eigenfrequency is governed by untrimmed interior basis functions rather than by refined trimmed functions. Consequently, the trimming-induced high-frequency outliers governing the critical time step are suppressed, yielding a larger admissible time step than globally refined trimmed B-splines with the same interior resolution.[...]

</details>


### [146] [Order elevation of directly self-starting sub-step implicit integrators for transient dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10240)
*Jinze Li,Yaokun Liu,Kewei Chen,Hua Li,Kaiping Yu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种广义s子步隐式框架，通过释放末子步固定约束并将所有子步位置视为设计变量，构建统一Runge–Kutta表示，获得两类互补的高阶直接自启动方法（s阶可调耗散与s+1阶固定耗散，最高七阶）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接自启动隐式方法可避免辅助启动过程并保留原一/二阶控制方程，但现有格式通常固定每时间区间末子步位置，从而限制了可达精度阶数。

Method: 释放末子步固定约束，将全部子步位置作为设计变量，构造广义s子步隐式框架；对一/二阶瞬态系统给出统一Runge–Kutta表示并保持各子步有效矩阵一致；通过同时匹配数值放大因子与载荷算子推导精度条件，兼顾齐次与受迫响应；针对s=1,…,6得到两类方法族。

Result: 得到s阶族（高频数值耗散可控、子步位置可调）与s+1阶族（由选定子步位置获得、耗散固定），后者在子步数不变下最高达七阶精度，部分高阶成员为A(α)-稳定且稳定角极接近90°；幅值与相位误差分析揭示无阻尼系统中与奇偶性相关的超收敛，适当参数选择可显著提升相位或幅值精度；数值算例验证了预测收敛阶及对虚假高频响应的可控抑制。

Conclusion: 该广义框架突破了末子步固定带来的阶数限制，为直接自启动瞬态分析提供了统一、可设计的高阶隐式方法体系，并兼具耗散可控与超收敛特性。

Abstract: Directly self-starting implicit methods are attractive for transient analysis because they avoid auxiliary starting procedures while retaining the original first- or second-order governing equations. However, most existing formulations usually fix the last sub-step at the end of each time interval, which restricts the attainable order. This study develops a generalized $s$-sub-step implicit framework by releasing this constraint and treating all sub-step locations as design variables. The resulting methods admit a unified Runge--Kutta representation for both first- and second-order transient systems and preserve identical effective matrices over all sub-steps. Accuracy conditions are derived by simultaneously matching the numerical amplification factor and load operator, thereby accounting for both homogeneous and forced responses. For $s=1,~\cdots,~6$, two complementary families are obtained: $s$th-order members with user-controllable high-frequency numerical dissipation and adjustable sub-step locations, and $(s+1)$th-order members obtained by selecting the sub-step locations, with fixed dissipation. The latter reach up to seventh-order accuracy without increasing the number of sub-steps, although some high-order members are $A(α)$-stable with stability angles extremely close to $90^\circ$. Analytical amplitude and phase errors further reveal parity-dependent superconvergence in undamped systems, and appropriate parameter selections can substantially increase either phase or amplitude accuracy beyond the formal order. Numerical benchmarks confirm the predicted convergence orders and the controllable suppression of spurious high-frequency responses.

</details>


### [147] [Weakly Symmetric and Traceless Tangential-Normal Tensor Finite Elements: Application to the Brinkman Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10331)
*Xuehai Huang,Xinyue Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 构建任意维、任意阶的弱对称、逐点无迹切向-法向张量有限元；应用于不可压Brinkman方程，得到无稳定化、对粘度一致稳定、精确无散且压力鲁棒的混合方法，并给出最优误差与边界层估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要兼具弱对称性与逐点无迹性的应力张量有限元，且全局耦合自由度尽量少（仅切向-法向面矩），以构造无稳定化、对粘度参数一致稳定的混合方法并实现精确无散与压力鲁棒性。

Method: 通过局部单元矩施加对称性，全局仅耦合切向-法向面矩、无需顶点自由度；在三维及以上引入最低阶线性增广以恢复离散Korn稳定性所需的刚体运动面控制。基于物理粘性应力构造分布型混合方法，速度采用散度相容的BDM元，压力不连续；通过放松切向-法向连续性得到代数等价的应力杂交格式及无稳定化虚拟元实现。

Result: 得到任意维、任意阶的弱对称、逐点无迹切向-法向张量有限元族。混合方法无稳定化，对粘度参数一致稳定，精确无散且压力鲁棒；在自然范数下获得最优阶误差估计；在参数显式正则性假设下得到参数一致边界层估计与最优Darcy逼近阶。

Conclusion: 该有限元框架以极少全局耦合自由度实现弱对称与无迹性，为不可压Brinkman问题提供无稳定化、粘度一致稳定、精确无散且压力鲁棒的混合方法，并可通过应力杂交或虚拟元实现，兼具理论最优性与实现灵活性。

Abstract: We develop a family of weakly symmetric and pointwise traceless tangential--normal tensor finite elements in arbitrary space dimension and for all polynomial orders. Symmetry is imposed through local cell moments, while the only globally coupled stress degrees of freedom are tangential--normal facet moments; no vertex degrees of freedom are required. In dimensions three and higher, a lowest-order linear enrichment restores the rigid-motion facet control required for discrete Korn stability. As a principal application, we construct a distributional mixed method for the incompressible Brinkman equations using the physical viscous stress. Coupled with divergence-conforming BDM velocities and discontinuous pressures, the method is stabilization-free, uniformly stable with respect to the viscosity parameter, exactly divergence-free, and pressure-robust. We establish optimal-order error estimates in the natural norms. Under suitable parameter-explicit regularity assumptions, we also obtain a parameter-uniform boundary-layer estimate with optimal Darcy approximation order. Relaxing tangential--normal continuity yields an algebraically equivalent stress-hybridized formulation and a stabilization-free virtual element realization.

</details>


### [148] [Gradient-Enhanced Proximal Algorithms for Mean Field Planning on Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10370)
*Chengrun Jiang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究曲面上平均场规划问题的近端算法，提出一种梯度增强的约束投影近似方法，结合时间有限差分、曲面有限元及多项式保持恢复技术，并给出一阶/二阶算法及分裂算法中的理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均场规划需要在初末密度约束下最小化传输能量，其近端算法需反复求解时空Poisson方程，而约束投影的精确计算代价高，因此需要构建高效且可控的梯度增强近似。

Method: 采用时间有限差分、空间曲面有限元、时间多项式保持恢复（PPR）与空间参数化多项式保持恢复对约束投影进行梯度增强近似，并将该构造嵌入ISTA、FISTA及Douglas-Rachford分裂中；区分恢复更新与精确离散投影，推导残差恒等式及条件有限迭代扰动界。

Result: 建立了残差恒等式与条件有限迭代扰动界，界中保留了数据、边界与线性求解器误差；现有导数恢复估计在适当正则性与网格假设下给出高阶贡献，但本身不足以证明外层优化迭代的收敛性；在球面及更复杂的代数曲面上进行了数值实验。

Conclusion: 梯度增强的投影近似可融入多种近端算法框架，其残差与扰动分析为误差控制提供理论支撑，但外层优化收敛性仍待进一步研究，数值细化数据的局限性也被讨论。

Abstract: Mean field planning on a surface prescribes initial and terminal densities and minimizes a transport energy subject to the continuity equation. Proximal algorithms for this problem repeatedly solve a time--space Poisson equation, whose temporal derivative and surface gradient determine the density and momentum corrections. We study a gradient-enhanced approximation of this constraint projection using finite differences in time, surface finite elements in space, temporal polynomial preserving recovery, and spatial parametric polynomial preserving recovery. The same construction is incorporated into ISTA, FISTA, and Douglas--Rachford splitting. We distinguish the recovered update from an exact discrete projection and derive residual identities and conditional finite-iteration perturbation bounds that retain data, boundary, and linear-solver errors. Existing derivative-recovery estimates identify a higher-order contribution under suitable regularity and mesh assumptions; they do not by themselves establish convergence of the outer optimization iteration. Available numerical illustrations on the sphere and a more complicated algebraic surface are discussed together with the limits of the recorded refinement data.

</details>


### [149] [A quasi-interpolation method for finite element spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10374)
*Ohannes Karakashian*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 将Taylor多项式逼近与平均算子结合，构造一种拟插值技术，用于分析有限元空间的逼近性质，无需仿射等价族，可自然处理曲边界，并具教学价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元逼近分析依赖仿射等价族与参考单元，自由度需从参考单元"移植"到一般单元，处理曲边区域时较繁琐。作者希望提供一种绕开该框架、更通用且易用的分析途径。

Method: 构造一种拟插值算子：以Taylor多项式逼近局部函数，再通过平均（averaging）操作整合，使自由度在局部直接定义，而非由参考单元导出。该方法不依赖仿射等价族概念。

Result: 该拟插值技术可有效刻画有限元空间的逼近性质；能够自然地处理带曲边界的区域；对具体有限元的应用简便。

Conclusion: Taylor逼近与平均相结合的拟插值是一种强有力的有限元逼近分析工具，既规避了仿射等价族的限制、适用于曲边界区域，又因易于应用而具有教学价值。

Abstract: A combination of Taylor polynomial approximation and averaging results in a powerful quasi-interpolation technique that can be used to analyze the approximation properties of finite element spaces. With this particular approach, the notion of affine equivalent families is completely bypassed in the sense that the degrees of freedom are defined locally rather than being exported from a reference element. In particular, domains with curved boundaries can be handled in a natural manner. Finally, the ease of its application to a particular finite element makes it a valuable pedagogical tool.

</details>


### [150] [Invariant domain preservation for hybrid point-value and cell-average discretizations of hyperbolic equations on general meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10476)
*Shengrong Ding,Shumo Cui,Remi Abgrall,Kailiang Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的保不变域（IDP）框架，用于双曲守恒律的混合离散（含active flux与PAMPA方法），在单一局部稳定性条件下同时保证元胞平均与元胞边界点值的容许性，无需引入额外演化自由度或事后修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合离散中，元胞平均按守恒方式更新，而边界点值在可能非守恒的算子下演化，二者耦合。核心困难在于：仅用容许边界迹构造的守恒物理通量无法在任意CFL下提供守恒IDP保证（当内部重构值不受控时），因此需要识别并补充缺失的局部控制机制。

Method: 1) 对点值更新引入基于barrier–Legendre映射的容许性变换，处理由凹约束描述的凸容许内部，并证明逆映射在相关集合上全局定义且Lipschitz连续；2) 对守恒元胞平均更新给出结构阻塞定理，证明单状态连续物理通量无法实现守恒IDP保证；3) 结合元胞平均分解（CAD）、几何拟线性化与局部先验缩放，在通量求值前构造容许且一般间断的迹状态；4) 在显式基于迹的CFL条件下导出耦合混合更新的IDP性质，常数由局部CAD权重与迹状态波速界决定；5) 在三角形、笛卡尔、凸四边形及一般凸多边形网格上构造三阶格式。

Result: 数值结果表明：对光滑解达到设计精度阶，并严格保持物理容许性。

Conclusion: 该工作为active flux、PAMPA等混合离散提供了统一的IDP构造，通过barrier–Legendre容许变换、结构阻塞定理与CAD类IDP通量机制，在单一迹CFL条件下实现耦合更新的不变域保持，并可扩展到多种网格上的三阶格式。

Abstract: This paper presents a unified invariant-domain-preserving (IDP) framework for hybrid discretizations of hyperbolic conservation laws, including active flux and PAMPA methods, in which cell averages are updated conservatively while cell-boundary point values evolve under a possibly non-conservative operator. The main challenge is to preserve admissibility for these two coupled sets of states under a single local stability condition, without adding evolved degrees of freedom or relying on post-update repairs. For the point-value update, we introduce an admissibility transform based on a barrier--Legendre map for convex admissible interiors described by concave constraints, and prove that the inverse map is globally defined and Lipschitz continuous on the relevant sets. For the conservative cell-average update, we establish a structural obstruction theorem: the single-state continuous physical flux built from admissible boundary traces alone cannot provide a conservative IDP guarantee, for any prescribed CFL number, when internal reconstruction values are uncontrolled. This identifies the missing local control that must be supplied by an additional IDP flux mechanism. To realize this mechanism explicitly, we combine cell average decompositions (CAD), geometric quasilinearization, and local a priori scaling to construct admissible, generally discontinuous trace states before flux evaluation. Under an explicit trace-based CFL condition, with constants determined by local CAD weights and trace-state wave-speed bounds, the coupled hybrid update preserves the prescribed invariant domain. Concrete third-order schemes are developed on triangular, Cartesian, convex quadrilateral, and general convex polygonal meshes. Numerical results demonstrate the designed order of accuracy for smooth solutions and the strict preservation of physical admissibility.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [The hyperbolic class and vanishing laws in a TMF-valued four-manifold invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09819)
*Yuqi Li,Hao-Yu Sun*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 在谱Looijenga构造的显式假设下，直接计算了Gukov-Krushkal-Meier-Pei零截面不变量的双曲平面取值，其值为Hopf元素η，并由此得到光滑闭单连通spin四维流形上的完整取值公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作需在额外的cobordism-duality假设下才能得到双曲取值，本文旨在去掉该假设，直接由Looijenga限制映射导出，以完善该不变量在周期拓扑模形式中的取值理论。

Method: 在关于谱Looijenga构造的显式假设下，不使用cobordism-duality假设，直接从Looijenga限制映射计算双曲平面取值，并结合定格的annihilation与vanishing结果。

Result: 双曲平面取值为Hopf元素η，且加入双曲平面的作用等于乘以η；结合定格结果得到光滑闭单连通spin四维流形上的完整取值公式。

Conclusion: 非零符号差迫使该不变量消失，因此K3曲面的两种定向取值均为零。

Abstract: Under explicit hypotheses on the spectral Looijenga construction, we compute the hyperbolic-plane value of the zero-section invariant of Gukov, Krushkal, Meier, and Pei in periodic topological modular forms. The value is the Hopf element eta, and adjoining a hyperbolic plane acts by multiplication by this element. Earlier work obtains the hyperbolic value under an additional cobordism-duality assumption; here we derive it directly from the Looijenga restriction maps without that assumption. Together with annihilation and vanishing results for definite lattices, this gives a complete value formula on smooth closed simply connected spin four-manifolds. In particular, nonzero signature forces the invariant to vanish, so both orientations of a K3 surface have value zero.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Support Discovery With Iteratively Reweighted Least Squares for Fixed-Charge Network Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09295)
*Sindura Saraswathi,Christian Kümmerle*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）的可扩展连续优化算法，用于大规模单商品固定费用网络流问题（FCNFP），在410个实例上取得优于同类可扩展算法的目标质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCNFP将连续流量分配与离散弧激活决策耦合，是网络设计与资源分配的典型难题。精确MILP模型虽能忠实刻画固定费用结构，但在大规模网络上求解困难，因此需要可扩展的算法。

Method: 采用IRLS框架，用平滑非凸Lasry–Lions代理函数替代不连续的固定费用与线性弧成本目标，求解一系列加权二次流量子问题；每个子问题由热启动的对偶半光滑牛顿法求解，其牛顿系统具有加权图拉普拉斯结构，可借助现代拉普拉斯求解器。另提出结合目标驱动扰动重启与锚点并集受限搜索的变体，联合利用IRLS与互补FCNFP启发式发现的弧支撑。

Result: 在410个基准、合成及大规模实例上，该方法在评估的可扩展FCNFP算法中目标质量最佳：相对限时MILP参考的平均差距为1.316%，在非MILP方法中的胜或平率为90.0%。

Conclusion: 将平滑连续优化与支撑级搜索相结合，是为大规模FCNFP生成高质量可行解的有效策略。

Abstract: The fixed-charge network flow problem (FCNFP) couples continuous flow allocation with discrete arc-activation decisions, making it a canonical but computationally challenging model for a variety of network design and resource allocation problems. Exact mixed-integer linear programming formulations capture the fixed-charge structure faithfully, but often become difficult to solve on large networks. We propose a scalable continuous-optimization algorithm for large-scale single-commodity FCNFP based on an iteratively reweighted least-squares (IRLS) framework. The method replaces the discontinuous fixed-charge and linear arc cost objective with a smooth nonconvex Lasry--Lions surrogate and solves a sequence of weighted quadratic flow subproblems. Each subproblem is solved by a warm-started dual semismooth Newton method whose Newton systems have weighted graph-Laplacian structure, enabling the use of modern Laplacian solvers. To further improve the discovered arc supports of the challenging underlying combinatorial problem, we also develop an algorithmic variant that incorporates objective-driven perturbation restarts and an anchor-union restricted search that jointly leverages supports discovered by IRLS and by complementary FCNFP heuristics. Computational experiments on 410 benchmark, synthetic, and large-scale instances show that our method obtains the best objective quality among the evaluated scalable FCNFP algorithms, with a mean gap of $1.316\%$ to a time-limited MILP reference and a win-or-tie rate of $90.0\%$ among the non-MILP methods. The results indicate that combining smooth continuous optimization with support-level search is an effective strategy for producing high-quality feasible solutions to large-scale FCNFP.

</details>


### [153] [Communication-efficient ADMM over Hierarchical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09361)
*Binh Nguyen,Shuangqing Wei,Truong X. Nghiem*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出层次交替方向乘子法（hADMM），用于树结构网络上的层次优化问题，通过重排增广拉格朗日构建查询-响应通信机制，显著降低通信成本，并在凸性假设下保证渐近收敛，在温和条件下实现线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ADMM的层次优化方法在树结构网络上的通信成本较高，亟需提升网络通信效率以适配层次化优化场景。

Method: 基于ADMM提出hADMM算法，通过重排增广拉格朗日，在节点间建立显式利用层次树结构的查询-响应通信机制；并利用线性矩阵不等式进行收敛率分析，刻画不同网络拓扑下理论可达的最大收敛率。

Result: 与现有ADMM类层次优化方法相比，hADMM显著降低通信成本；在凸性假设下保证渐近收敛，并在温和条件下达到线性收敛；三项数值实验表明其兼容任意树形网络结构，且在通信效率上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: hADMM为树结构网络上的层次优化提供了通信高效的分布式求解方案，兼具收敛性保障与拓扑适应能力，适用于各类树形网络场景。

Abstract: This paper develops a novel distributed optimization algorithm based on the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) to solve hierarchical optimization problems over tree-structured networks, termed hierarchical ADMM (hADMM), with a particular focus on enhancing communication efficiency across the network. By rearranging the augmented Lagrangian to establish a query-response communication mechanism between nodes that explicitly exploits the hierarchical tree structure, the proposed algorithm significantly reduces communication costs compared to existing ADMM-based methods for hierarchical optimization. Furthermore, hADMM guarantees asymptotic convergence under convexity assumptions. We also present a convergence rate analysis based on linear matrix inequalities to characterize the maximum theoretically achievable convergence rates across different network topologies, showing that the proposed hADMM attains linear convergence under mild conditions. Three numerical experiments demonstrate that hADMM is compatible with arbitrary tree network structures and outperforms existing approaches in terms of communication efficiency.

</details>


### [154] [On the spectral radius properties of a key matrix in periodic impulse control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.07616)
*Swati Patel,Patrick De Leenheer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性微分方程系统在周期脉冲控制下的定性行为，核心结论是控制矩阵谱半径关于控制周期具有凸性，并据此确定有效控制的最优与最大周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期脉冲控制在现实中广泛存在（如定期施药、定期干预），控制周期的选取直接影响系统稳定性与干预成本，但周期与系统定性行为之间的定量关系尚缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 对由线性微分方程描述的系统施加周期脉冲控制，构造一个依赖于控制周期及控制效果的"控制矩阵"，用以表征受控系统的定性行为；进而分析该矩阵谱半径的性质，重点考察其作为控制周期函数的凸性。

Result: 证明了控制矩阵的谱半径关于控制周期是凸函数，并利用该凸性给出了判定有效控制所需的最优周期与最大周期的依据。

Conclusion: 谱半径对控制周期的凸性为周期脉冲控制的设计提供了理论工具，可确定保证系统稳定的周期上界与最优周期；文末结合一个源于实际场景的例子验证了该结论的应用价值。

Abstract: In this work, we consider the periodic impulse control of a system modeled as a set of linear differential equations. We define a matrix that governs the qualitative behavior of the controlled system. This matrix depends on the period and effects of the control interventions. We investigate properties of the spectral radius of this matrix and in particular, how it depends on the period of the interventions. Our main result is on the convexity of the spectral radius with respect to this period. We discuss implications of this convexity on establishing an optimal and maximum period for effective control. Finally, we provide an example motivated from a real-life scenario.

</details>


### [155] [Powell-Style Model-Based Derivative-Free Optimization with Complexity Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09441)
*Abraar Chaudhry,Katya Scheinberg,Scholar Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为Powell的基于模型的信赖域无导数算法提供变体，推导其复杂度界，并结合随机子空间与噪声情形进行理论与数值分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Powell提出的基于低阶多项式插值并维护插值集几何结构的无导数信赖域方法在实践中表现优异，但缺乏与其它无导数方法可比的复杂度理论保证，本文旨在弥补这一理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: 提出Powell型基于模型信赖域无导数算法的变体，保持低阶多项式插值及插值集几何管理机制；在随机生成子空间中应用该方法；将子空间基于模型信赖域方法的分析扩展到函数评估含噪声的情形；并进行广泛的数值比较。

Result: 导出的复杂度界使该类方法在理论上可与其它无导数方法竞争；在随机子空间中应用时获得了近乎紧的复杂度；完整纳入Powell几何处理方式的分析得到扩展，并通过数值实验将实际性能与理论性能相联系。

Conclusion: 完整考虑Powell几何处理的无导数信赖域方法具有有竞争力的复杂度保证，随机子空间策略可达到近紧复杂度，且相关分析可推广至噪声函数评估场景，实现了理论与实践的衔接。

Abstract: We propose variants of model-based trust region derivative-free algorithms that are closest to methods initially proposed and implemented by Powell. These methods rely on low degree polynomial interpolation and carefully maintain geometry of the interpolation sets. We are able to derive complexity bounds for these methods that make them theoretically competitive to other derivative-free methods. Applying these methods in randomly generated subspaces recovers what we believe to be nearly tight complexity. This paper builds on recent results where complexity of a much simplified version of Powell's methods was derived. Here, we extend the analysis to fully incorporate Powell's geometry handling approach and conduct an extensive numerical comparison of the model-based trust region methods connecting practical and theoretical performance. We also extend the analysis of subspace model-based trust region methods to the case of noisy function evaluations.

</details>


### [156] [Afterimage Slow Regions in First-Order Methods for Linear Conic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09449)
*Shucheng Kang,Heng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性锥规划中一阶方法的慢收敛现象，提出"慢区域族"（slow region family）刻画状态空间中慢收敛发生的位置，并发展"残像原理"（afterimage principle）用于构造慢区域；在ADMM、sGS-ADMM与PDHG上验证假设，并给出LP、SOCP、SDP的慢区域实例集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有分析多刻画慢收敛发生的时间阶段或问题实例，本文转而关注慢收敛在状态空间中"何处"出现，即从时间/实例视角转向状态空间视角。

Method: 针对参数化平均不动点迭代定义慢区域族：在慢区域上，单步仅将状态朝不动点集推进其距离的一小部分，故轨道可长期保持初始距离。为构造慢区域，提出残像原理：构造一个中心族与邻近的花瓣族，二者共享同一极限参数使残差场彼此接近，但其不动点集或前向漂移相距甚远，借此由花瓣几何为中心族认证出慢区域。最后验证ADMM、sGS-ADMM、PDHG的常设假设。

Result: 给出LP、SOCP与SDP的慢区域实例集，展示慢区域形态的多样性；并对ADMM、sGS-ADMM、PDHG验证了常设假设成立。

Conclusion: 慢区域族与残像原理为理解一阶锥规划方法的慢收敛提供了状态空间层面的新工具，可统一解释并构造多类算法与多类锥问题中的慢收敛现象。

Abstract: First-order methods for linear conic programming often stall on long plateaus. Existing analyses characterize when during a run or on which problem instances slow convergence occurs; we instead ask where in the state space slow convergence is present. We introduce the slow region family for parameterized averaged fixed-point iterations. On a slow region, one step moves the state by only a small fraction of its distance to the fixed-point set, so an orbit starting there keeps almost its initial distance for arbitrarily many iterations. We then develop the afterimage principle to construct them. A center family and a nearby petal family share the same limit parameter, so their residual fields become close, while their fixed-point sets or forward drifts stay far apart. The petal geometry then certifies a slow region for the center. We verify the standing assumptions for ADMM, sGS-ADMM, and PDHG, and give an LP, SOCP, and SDP gallery showing how varied slow regions can be.

</details>


### [157] [Unifying Variational View of Accelerated Primal-Dual Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09454)
*Cheng Chang,Mehran Mesbahi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出统一变分框架，通过ELR原理从增广Bregman拉格朗日量系统生成仿射约束凸优化中的加速原始-对偶流，给出收敛保证并推广到概率分布优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速原始-对偶流虽已有诸多结果，但缺乏统一生成机制，且现有工作多限于特定几何（如欧氏几何）和有限维问题，亟需一个能覆盖一般Bregman几何、并统一现有原始-对偶镜像流与ADMM流的变分框架。

Method: 将Euler-Lagrange-Rayleigh(ELR)原理应用于耦合增广Bregman拉格朗日量，在一般Bregman几何下系统推导加速原始-对偶动力学；证明该框架将现有原始-对偶镜像流与ADMM流作为特例纳入；在温和假设下建立流的收敛性；并将该算法开发框架从有限维优化扩展至概率分布上的约束优化。

Result: 框架统一生成一般Bregman几何下的加速原始-对偶动力学，恢复一族已有原始-对偶镜像流与ADMM流为特例；在温和假设下证明这些流具有O(e^{-b_t})收敛速率；框架从有限维优化推广到概率分布上的约束优化。

Conclusion: ELR原理与增广Bregman拉格朗日量的结合为仿射约束凸优化提供了统一的变分视角，既能复现并统一既有加速原始-对偶方法，又给出加速收敛保证，并可延伸至概率分布空间上的约束优化。

Abstract: We develop a unifying variational framework for accelerated primal-dual flows in affinely constrained convex optimization. In particular, it is shown that applying the Euler-Lagrange-Rayleigh (ELR) principle to coupled augmented Bregman Lagrangians systematically generates accelerated dynamics over general Bregman geometries and recovers a family of existing primal-dual mirror and Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) flows as special cases. We then establish $\mathcal{O}(e^{-b_t})$ convergence guarantees for these flows under mild assumptions. Lastly, we show that the proposed framework for algorithmic development extends from finite-dimensional optimization to constrained optimization over probability distributions.

</details>


### [158] [Global Optimization Framework for Automated Low-Thrust Gravity-Assist Trajectory Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09515)
*Ryo Iijima,Kenshiro Oguri,Toshinori Kuwahara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于凸剪枝的宽域搜索框架，结合推力正则化的局部低推力优化，在九次引力借力的BepiColombo场景中显著扩展了可行解数量与发射窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力借力轨迹设计需搜索大量轨迹段组合，但对所有组合逐一施用非线性规划（NLP）进行低推力优化在计算上不可行。传统框架因此先用Lambert计算、简化深空机动等轻量模型做宽域搜索并剪枝，但为保效率仅处理低维、约束有限的建模（每段至多几次脉冲机动），无法承载低推力设计所需的约束多变量优化，存在过早剪除可行轨迹的风险。

Method: 提出可在宽域搜索阶段直接求解约束多变量问题的凸剪枝方法，在保持计算效率的同时提升建模维度与约束能力；并在宽域搜索阶段嵌入基于推力正则化的鲁棒局部低推力优化算法，实现自动化全局探索与优化。

Result: 在受BepiColombo启发的九次引力借力场景中验证：所提框架发现36,335个解，远超传统方法的4,664个，并将可行发射窗口拓宽约一年。

Conclusion: 将凸剪枝与推力正则化局部优化结合的框架，可在宽域搜索阶段处理约束多变量问题，避免过早剪枝，实现自动化全局探索与优化，显著提升多引力借力低推力轨迹设计的解空间覆盖与发射窗口范围。

Abstract: Gravity-assist trajectory design requires searching numerous leg combinations, but applying nonlinear programming (NLP) for low-thrust trajectory optimization to all combinations is computationally prohibitive. Therefore, conventional frameworks first perform broad search using lightweight trajectory models such as Lambert-based calculation and simplified deep-space maneuver models, while pruning infeasible candidates. However, to maintain efficiency, conventional approaches handle only low-dimensional formulations with limited constraints such as up to a couple of impulse maneuvers per leg. Consequently, they cannot accommodate the constrained multivariable optimization required for low-thrust design, running the risk of prematurely pruning viable trajectories. To address this, this paper introduces a convex pruning approach capable of solving constrained multivariable problems directly within broad search while retaining the computational efficiency. We then augment the broad search stage with a robust local low-thrust optimization algorithm based on thrust regularization, which together enable global exploration and optimization in an automated fashion. We apply the proposed framework in a BepiColombo-inspired scenario involving nine gravity assists, successfully demonstrating its broad search capability to discover a far greater number of solutions (36,335) compared to a conventional approach (4,664) while identifying a wider feasible launch window by one year.

</details>


### [159] [Oracle Complexity of Stochastic Fixed-Point Equations with Nonexpansive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09524)
*Jelena Diakonikolas,Cristóbal Guzmán,David Martínez-Rubio*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在一般范数下、通过带方差界随机Oracle访问的非扩张自映射，找到满足‖T(x)-x‖≤ε的点的Oracle复杂度，给出基于递归锚定技术的算法与近乎匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究固定点残差较小的点的计算复杂度，尤其是随机Oracle、非扩张映射、一般范数这一更具挑战性的设定，并刻画其最优Oracle复杂度。

Method: 针对具有弱Rademacher型q>1的任意范数，提出基于递归锚定（recursive anchoring）技术的算法；对型-2空间（如p∈[2,∞]的ℓ_p空间）进行分析，并构造高维ℓ_∞范数实例的近匹配下界。

Result: 对型-2空间，算法达到随机Oracle复杂度Õ(σ²ε⁻³+ε⁻¹)（高概率）；对高维ℓ_∞范数实例证明近乎匹配（至多差多对数因子）的下界，且该下界对任意成功概率为常数的随机算法成立；结果还扩展到"稀疏"噪声情形，排除通过非匹配ℓ_p范数度量方差来改进ε依赖性的可能。

Conclusion: 本文刻画了一般范数下非扩张自映射近似固定点问题的随机Oracle复杂度上界与下界，在ℓ_∞高维情形下近乎最优，并表明方差度量范数的选择无法带来复杂度上的改进。

Abstract: We study the oracle complexity of computing a point with small fixed-point residual $\|T(x)-x\| \leq ε$, for a general norm $\|\cdot\|$ and a self-map $T$ of a compact convex set. We study this problem in the setting where $T$ is nonexpansive with respect to the same norm $\|\cdot\|$ and accessed via an unbiased stochastic oracle with bounded variance $σ^2$. We provide an algorithm that solves such instances for any norm with a weak Rademacher type $q > 1$, with high probability. The algorithm is based on a recursive anchoring technique. For type-$2$ spaces, such as $\ell_p$-spaces for $p \in [2, \infty]$, our algorithm attains stochastic oracle complexity $\tilde O(σ^2 ε^{-3} + ε^{-1})$. We further prove a near-matching lower bound (i.e., matching up to poly-log factors) for such $\ell_{\infty}$-norm instances in high dimensions. Our lower bound holds against any randomized algorithm that succeeds with constant probability. It further extends to settings with ``sparse'' noise, where variance measured with respect to any $\ell_p$ norm is of the same order, ruling out the possibility of improving oracle complexity as a function of $\varepsilon$ by measuring variance in a non-matching $\ell_p$ norm.

</details>


### [160] [A Function-Space Framework for BDDC Preconditioning in Control- and State-Constrained Sparse Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09541)
*Harbir Antil,Keegan L. A. Kirk,Umarkhon Rakhimov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为带箱约束、L1稀疏与Moreau-Yosida正则化状态约束的椭圆最优控制问题，在无穷维层面构造了BDDC预条件子，并证明其两级加性Schwarz表示、全局-局部等价与适定性，数值实验表明GMRES迭代数近似网格无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含箱约束控制、L1稀疏及Moreau-Yosida正则化状态约束的椭圆最优控制问题，其活动集半光滑牛顿线性化产生的界面系统规模大、条件数差，需要可扩展且对网格与正则化参数稳健的求解预条件技术。

Method: 在无穷维层面直接构建BDDC（约束平衡区域分解）预条件子；消去内部变量得到由子域局部算子组装的关于状态与伴随变量迹的Schur补；证明全局-局部等价性，并在可验证的原始约束条件下建立局部与部分组装问题的适定性；给出BDDC算子的两级加性Schwarz表示（独立局部求解加有限维粗空间求解）；采用协调有限元实现，测试分布式与Neumann边界控制情形，并考察粗空间加浓与Moreau-Yosida罚参数按h^2缩放的影响。

Result: 当子域直径H与网格尺寸h之比固定时，GMRES迭代数近似与网格细化无关，且随H/h增大仅温和增长；加浓粗空间显著改善收敛性并降低对控制正则化与稀疏权重的敏感性；对状态约束问题，将Moreau-Yosida罚参数按h^2比例缩放可使GMRES迭代数随网格细化近似保持常数。

Conclusion: 所提无穷维层面构造的BDDC预条件子对含箱约束、L1稀疏与正则化状态约束的椭圆最优控制界面系统具有良好可扩展性与参数稳健性，粗空间加浓及罚参数h^2缩放是提升收敛性能的有效手段。

Abstract: We develop a Balancing Domain Decomposition by Constraints (BDDC) preconditioner for the interface systems arising from active-set semi-smooth Newton linearizations of elliptic optimal control problems with box-constrained controls, $L^1$-sparsity, and Moreau--Yosida regularized state constraints. The domain decomposition and BDDC construction are formulated directly at the infinite-dimensional level. Eliminating the interior variables yields a Schur complement for the state and adjoint traces assembled from local subdomain operators. We prove a global--local equivalence result, establish well-posedness of the local and partially assembled problems under verifiable conditions on the primal constraints. The BDDC operator admits a two-level additive Schwarz representation with independent local solves and a finite-dimensional coarse solve. We implement the preconditioner using conforming finite elements and test it for distributed and Neumann boundary control. For a fixed ratio of subdomain diameter $H$ to mesh size $h$, the GMRES iteration counts are nearly independent of mesh refinement and grow only moderately as this ratio increases. Enriching the coarse space substantially improves convergence and reduces sensitivity to the control regularization and sparsity weights. For the state-constrained problem, scaling the Moreau--Yosida penalty parameter proportionally to $h^2$ yields nearly constant GMRES iteration counts as the mesh is refined.

</details>


### [161] [The Car Resequencing Problem with Makespan Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09602)
*Xinyi Guo,Jean-François Côté*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带回流车道（RL）的汽车再排序问题，以最小化再排序完工时间为目标，证明其NP完全性并提出两阶段分支定价-约束规划（BP-CP）精确算法，揭示RL能显著提升柔性并支持更小、更经济的缓冲设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车再排序问题需在两个相邻车间之间的中间缓冲区重新排列车辆顺序，以满足下游车间的目标序列。传统缓冲区仅含前向车道，柔性受限；本文引入带回流车道（RL）的新变体——前向车道与回流车道均遵循FIFO但方向相反，车辆可经RL在前向车道间循环，从而提升再排序柔性，但也增加了问题复杂度。研究动机在于以最短时间完成目标序列变换，并探讨RL对缓冲区设计与准时制运作的价值。

Method: 首先证明该问题为NP完全问题。进而设计两阶段精确算法BP-CP（分支定价与约束规划相结合）：第一阶段提出三种主问题（MP）版本，每种配备定义于特定图结构上的子问题；利用分支得到的部分整数解，第二阶段构建约束规划模型，或产出可行解，或向MP添加可行性割（feasibility cut）。

Result: 实验表明，BP-CP显著扩大了单个生产批次可求解实例的规模，尤其是在MP适度松弛时效果最佳。对比分析显示，RL明显增强了再排序柔性，使较小缓冲区在不同再排序复杂度下也能达到与不含RL的较大缓冲区相当的完工时间。

Conclusion: 扩大车道容量本身对再排序性能的提升有限，甚至可能降低效率。RL相当于若干条前向车道，支持更小且更具成本效益的缓冲区设计，同时维持高效的准时制运作，具有重要的管理启示意义。

Abstract: Problem definition: The car resequencing problem involves rearranging the sequence of cars between two adjacent production shops via an intermediate buffer. This study explores a new variant that aims to minimize the resequencing makespan when the buffer features several forward lanes and a return lane (RL). Both types of lanes follow first-in-first-out rules, but operate in opposite directions. Through the RL, cars can circulate between forward lanes, thereby increasing resequencing flexibility and complexity. The goal is to position cars over time to complete the target sequence change in the shortest possible time. Methodology/results: We prove the problem is NP-complete and develop an exact two-stage branch-and-price approach integrated with constraint programming (BP-CP). Three versions of the master problem (MP) are proposed for the first stage, each paired with a subproblem defined on a specific graph. Using partial integer solutions from branching, a constraint-programming model built in the second stage yields either a feasible solution to the problem or a feasibility cut added to the MP. Experimental results indicate that BP-CP greatly increases the scale of solvable instances for a production batch, especially with a moderately relaxed MP. A comparative analysis shows that the RL markedly enhances resequencing flexibility, enabling smaller buffers to attain makespans comparable to those of larger buffers without the RL across varying resequencing complexities. Managerial implications: Expanding lane capacity alone yields limited gains in resequencing performance and may even reduce efficiency. The RL acts as several forward lanes, supporting a smaller and cost-effective buffer design while maintaining efficient just-in-time operations.

</details>


### [162] [Primal-Dual Inexact Newton-MR for Nonconvex Optimization with Equality Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09683)
*Oscar Smee,Fred Roosta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于共轭残差(CR)内求解器的不精确原始-对偶牛顿法，用于求解含非线性等式约束的优化问题，可直接处理子问题的不定性，避免对拉格朗日Hessian的正则化，兼顾全局收敛性与大规模非凸问题上的实证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含非线性等式约束的优化问题广泛存在于科学、工程及机器学习中，常用方法包括序列二次规划与原始-对偶牛顿法。但其经典分析依赖强假设，尤其是拉格朗日Hessian在约束Jacobian零空间上的正定性。该假设在实践上常需要强正则化或使用正定Hessian替代；同时在大规模场景下精确求解原始-对偶牛顿子问题往往计算不可行。

Method: 提出一种不精确原始-对偶牛顿法，内层求解器采用共轭残差(CR)方法。利用CR近期被证明的性质，包括负曲率检测、迭代单调性与下降保证，在子问题不定性出现时直接加以处理，从而避免对拉格朗日Hessian的有害正则化，并自然支持不精确求解。

Result: 建立了最坏情况下的全局收敛性保证，并在大规模非凸问题上展现出很强的实证性能。

Conclusion: 通过在原始-对偶牛顿框架中引入基于CR的不精确内求解器，可在不依赖零空间Hessian正定性假设、不做有害正则化的前提下有效处理非凸等式约束优化，兼具理论收敛保证与大规模实证效果。

Abstract: Optimization problems with nonlinear equality constraints arise throughout science, engineering, and increasingly in machine learning. Prominent methods for solving such problems include sequential quadratic programming and, more broadly, primal-dual Newton methods. Classical analyses of these methods typically rely on strong assumptions, perhaps most notably positive definiteness of the Lagrangian Hessian on the null space of the constraint Jacobian. In practice, this assumption often necessitates strong regularization or the use of a positive definite Hessian surrogate. Moreover, in large-scale settings, solving the primal-dual Newton subproblem exactly is often computationally infeasible. To address these issues, we propose an inexact primal-dual Newton method with an inner solver based on the conjugate residual (CR) method. Exploiting recently established properties of CR, including negative-curvature detection, iterate monotonicity, and descent guarantees, our method handles indefiniteness in the subproblem directly as it arises. Our method thereby avoids detrimental regularization of the Lagrangian Hessian while naturally accommodating inexact solves. We establish worst-case global convergence guarantees and demonstrate strong empirical performance on large-scale, nonconvex problems.

</details>


### [163] [Counterexamples for BFGS-type methods under arbitrary strong Wolfe constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09686)
*Rui Diao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决quasi-Newton理论中长期未决的开放问题：对任意Armijo参数 $c_1 \\in (0,1)$，构造 $C^\\infty$、下有界、梯度Lipschitz的目标函数，使BFGS类及HS-CG等拟牛顿方法在一维搜索下迭代序列的梯度范数恒为1、永不收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Dai(2002)给出BFGS线搜索失败的反例以来，该失败是否在现实线搜索参数下普遍存在一直是quasi-Newton理论的核心开放问题。Dai的反例仅限于很小的Armijo参数（$c_1 \\le 1/84 \\approx 0.0119$，六点循环情形下 $c_1 \\le 69/7480 \\approx 0.0092$），且目标函数无下界，因此无法说明普通参数下的收敛性质。Dai在与J. C. Gilbert讨论后提出问题：对每个 $c_1 \\in (0,1)$ 是否都存在理论反例。本文正面回答这一悬而未决的问题。

Method: 对任意给定的线搜索参数对 $0 < c_1 < c_2 < 1$，在最小可能维数 $n=2$ 上构造 $f \\in C^\\infty(\\mathbb{R}^2)$，下有界且梯度Lipschitz连续。构造利用平面内非衰减的共轭下降轨道，并配合显式的管状插值，其双峰轴向曲率剖面可将Armijo比率置于 $(0,1)$ 中任意位置。迭代步取为搜索射线上的标准第一局部极小点，同时满足强Wolfe、弱Wolfe、Armijo与Goldstein条件（常数 $c_1,c_2$）。

Result: 对所构造函数，任意属于广泛共轭类 $\\mathcal{C}$ 的方法（包括经典全记忆BFGS、任意记忆 $m \\ge 1$ 的L-BFGS、Broyden正族及Hestenes-Stiefel共轭梯度法）在第一局部极小点线搜索下都产生无限迭代序列，且满足 $\\|\\nabla f(x_k)\\| = 1$ 对所有 $k \\ge 0$ 成立，即梯度范数不下降、永不收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作彻底解决了Dai(2002)提出的开放问题：在 $C^\\infty$、下有界、梯度Lipschitz的二维光滑非凸函数上，对任意 $c_1 \\in (0,1)$ 的Armijo参数乃至整个 $(c_1,c_2)$ 区间，BFGS类方法（含BFGS、L-BFGS、Broyden正族）及HS-CG可在线搜索下发生非收敛。这表明该失败现象并非小参数或病态函数的特例，而是BFGS类方法在现实线搜索参数下的一般性理论缺陷。

Abstract: Whether the Broyden-Fletcher-Goldfarb-Shanno (BFGS) method and its variants can fail to converge on smooth nonconvex functions under realistic line-search parameters has remained a central open problem in quasi-Newton theory since the landmark counterexample of Dai (2002), which was confined to small Armijo parameters ($c_1 \le 1/84 \approx 0.0119$ on Powell's geometry, or $c_1 \le 69/7480 \approx 0.0092$ on his six-point cycle) and an objective function unbounded below. In this paper, we resolve the long-standing open question, posed by Dai (2002) following a discussion with J. C. Gilbert, of whether such counterexamples exist in theory for every Armijo parameter $c_1 \in (0, 1)$. Specifically, for every prescribed pair of line-search parameters $0 < c_1 < c_2 < 1$, we construct an objective function $f \in C^\infty(\mathbb{R}^2)$, bounded below and with Lipschitz continuous gradient, on which every method in a broad conjugacy class $\mathcal{C}$ equipped with the first-local-minimizer line search generates an infinite sequence of iterates with $\|\nabla f(x_k)\| = 1$ for all $k \ge 0$. The class $\mathcal{C}$ encompasses the classical full-memory BFGS method, limited-memory BFGS (L-BFGS) with arbitrary memory $m \ge 1$, the Broyden positive family, and the Hestenes-Stiefel conjugate gradient method. The steps are the standard first local minimizers along the search rays and simultaneously satisfy the strong Wolfe, weak Wolfe, Armijo, and Goldstein conditions with constants $(c_1, c_2)$. The construction operates in the minimal possible dimension $n = 2$, exploiting a non-decaying conjugate descent orbit in the plane coupled with an explicit tubular interpolation whose two-bump axial curvature profile places the Armijo ratio anywhere in $(0, 1)$.

</details>


### [164] [Heavy-Ball Method under Randomized Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09743)
*Chang He,Shuzhong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机化参数调度对重球法在一般光滑凸目标上的加速作用，通过两个层级的随机化（区间内采样评估时刻、随机化时间边界本身）分别达到 O(1/K^{4/3}) 与 O(1/K^{3/2}) 的末迭代收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典重球法在一般光滑凸函数上仅有 O(1/K) 的收敛率，缺乏多项式级别的加速保证；作者希望借助预定义的随机化参数调度，探索重球法能否突破该经典速率，并与梯度下降的银步长调度结果进行比较。

Method: 区分两个随机化层级：其一，在边界确定的时间区间内随机采样梯度评估时刻，构造固定时间与 anytime 调度；其二，进一步将时间边界本身随机化，据此设计参数调度并给出末迭代的期望与几乎必然收敛分析。

Result: 在确定性时间边界下，固定时间与 anytime 调度取得期望末迭代函数值间隙 O(1/K^{4/3})，anytime 调度还满足几乎必然同阶速率；引入随机化时间边界后，期望与几乎必然的末迭代速率均提升至 O(1/K^{3/2})。

Conclusion: 这是首个在一般光滑凸函数上为重球法给出全局非渐近收敛保证、并相对经典 O(1/K) 速率实现多项式级提升的结果；同时表明重球法严格优于普通梯度下降配合银步长调度所能达到的最佳已知速率 O(1/K^{\log_2(1+\sqrt{2})})。

Abstract: We study how predefined randomized parameter schedules accelerate the heavy-ball method on general smooth convex objectives. Our analysis distinguishes two levels of randomization: sampling gradient-evaluation times within intervals whose boundaries are deterministic, and additionally randomizing the time boundaries themselves. With deterministic time boundaries, we construct fixed-time and anytime schedules that achieve an expected last-iterate function value gap of order $\mathcal{O}(1/K^{4/3})$; the anytime schedule also satisfies the same rate almost surely. We then use randomized time boundaries and obtain the improved last-iterate rate $\mathcal{O}(1/K^{3/2})$, both in expectation and almost surely. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first global nonasymptotic convergence guarantee for the heavy-ball method on general smooth convex functions that improves polynomially over the classical $\mathcal{O}(1/K)$ rate. Our result shows that the heavy-ball method achieves a strictly better convergence rate than the best known result $\mathcal{O}(1/K^{\log_2(1+\sqrt{2})})$ attainable by plain gradient descent with silver stepsize schedules.

</details>


### [165] [Closed-loop $α$-Potential Stochastic Differential Games via a BSDE Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09756)
*Xun Li,Liangquan Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将开环α-势随机微分博弈（SDG）研究推广至闭环情形，运用BSDE方法给出参数α的精确估计，并推导N-玩家异质代理平均场博弈中最小势近似误差的N-一致上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为作者前期开环控制研究（\cite{GLZ2025}）的延续，需将α-势SDG分析拓展到闭环框架；闭环情形引入反馈效应，其误差结构与开环情形本质不同，值得系统刻画。

Method: 采用倒向随机微分方程（BSDE）方法，在模型中同时纳入一阶与二阶敏感性状态过程以及控制过程的敏感性，并针对含平均场型相互作用的N-玩家异质代理博弈进行分析。

Result: 推导出参数α的精确估计，并得到最小势近似误差的N-一致上界。与开环估计相比，闭环上界包含反馈诱导的贡献项，这些项一般不随N增大而消失，因此该估计通常不能保证α→0。

Conclusion: 闭环α-势SDG的近似误差因反馈效应而具有非消失的N-一致项，导致无法一般性地保证α趋于零；这揭示了闭环与开环情形在势近似上的本质差异。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the closed-loop $α$-potential stochastic differential game (SDG) problem as a continuation of our prior research on open-loop control (see \cite{GLZ2025}). By utilizing the backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) approach, we derive a precise estimate for the parameter $α$. Compared to our earlier work \cite{GLZ2025}, this study incorporates both first- and second-order sensitivity state processes, as well as the sensitivity of the control process. A distinguishing feature of this work is that, in the context of $N$-player heterogeneous agent games involving mean-field type interactions, we derive an $N$-uniform upper bound for the minimal potential approximation error. In contrast to the corresponding open-loop estimates, the closed-loop bound contains feedback-induced contributions that need not vanish with $N$. Consequently, our present estimate does not in general guarantee $α\rightarrow 0$.

</details>


### [166] [Arithmetic Scar Accessibility and Generic Schrodinger Control on Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09758)
*Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限紧致度量图（内部顶点满足Kirchhoff条件、外部顶点满足固定Dirichlet-Neumann分配）上自由薛定谔方程的内部控制问题。作者给出了一个算术判据，刻画哪些"scar"可被固定目标度量的高频本征函数精确触及，并在此基础上结合GGCC的图论刻画，证明了在有理无关（从而Lebesgue几乎处处的）度量下，GGCC是自由设定中任意正时刻精确可控的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量图上自由薛定谔方程的内部精确可控性何时成立，尤其是GGCC这一图几何控制条件是否充分必要，尚需在谱层面给出刻画。已有的可触及性/ scar 构造通常局限于有理无关度量，作者希望突破这一限制，理解共振度量、有理相关度量下的高频本征函数质量集中现象，以及它对有限频率观测退化和精确可控性的影响。

Method: 针对指定的本原环（primitive cycle）或外部到外部的路径，构造"scar"：将割丛集合（secular set）的正则 scar 层（regular scar stratum）与目标长度向量的轨道闭包环面（orbit-closure torus）求交，由此得到算术可触及性判据。进一步在轨道闭包流上引入内蕴Diophantine条件，定量估计本征序列上支撑集之外的质量衰减。

Result: 1）所给算术判据能保证相应 scar 可被固定目标度量的精确高频本征函数触及，且可对超出有理无关类的共振度量成立。2）在内蕴Diophantine条件下，构造是定量的：沿精确本征序列，指定支撑之外的质量多项式衰减，从而当该支撑不受控时给出有限频率观测的多项式退化律。3）有理无关度量具有满相位轨道闭包，因此使每个本原障碍都可触及。4）结合已知的GGCC图论刻画与充分性，证明对每个有理无关（从而Lebesgue几乎处处）度量，GGCC是自由设定中任意正时刻精确可控的充要条件。

Conclusion: GGCC在有理无关度量（进而Lebesgue几乎处处度量）下对自由薛定谔方程的精确可控性是充要的；scar 可触及性由割丛集合的算术结构与轨道闭包环面的相交决定，内蕴Diophantine条件给出质量衰减与有限频率观测退化的定量描述。

Abstract: We study internal exact controllability of the free Schrödinger equation on finite compact metric graphs with Kirchhoff conditions at interior vertices and a fixed Dirichlet-Neumann assignment at exterior vertices. For a prescribed primitive cycle or exterior-to-exterior path, we identify an arithmetic criterion ensuring that the corresponding scar is accessible by exact high-frequency eigenfunctions of a fixed target metric. The criterion is formulated by intersecting the regular scar stratum of the secular set with the orbit-closure torus of the target length vector, and it can hold for resonant metrics beyond the rationally independent class. Under an intrinsic Diophantine condition on the orbit-closure flow, the construction is quantitative: along an exact eigensequence the mass outside the prescribed support decays polynomially, yielding a polynomial degeneration law for finite-frequency observation when that support is uncontrolled. Rationally independent metrics have full phase-orbit closure and therefore make every primitive obstruction accessible. Combining this spectral result with the known graph-theoretic characterization and sufficiency of the Graph Geometric Control Condition (GGCC), we prove that, for every rationally independent and hence for Lebesgue-almost every metric, GGCC is necessary and sufficient for exact controllability at every positive time in the free setting.

</details>


### [167] [MPFA: A Pareto Front Approximation Method for Riemannian Bi-objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09857)
*Kaiping Liu,P. -A. Absil,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Hermite插值的黎曼流形上光滑双目标优化的Pareto前沿逼近方法（MPFA），可无需多个初始点即生成连续近似Pareto前沿，建立收敛性并分析逼近误差，数值实验及稀疏主成分分析应用验证其有效性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黎曼流形多目标优化数值算法通常需要多个初始点才能刻画Pareto前沿，难以直接获得连续的前沿近似，作者希望克服这一局限。

Method: 面向黎曼流形上的光滑双目标优化问题，基于Hermite插值技术构建Pareto前沿逼近（MPFA）方法，在无需多个初始点的条件下生成连续近似Pareto前沿。

Result: 证明了所提方法的收敛性，并对所得Pareto前沿的逼近误差进行了分析；在多个测试问题上的数值实验表明该方法能以高精度和合理计算代价有效逼近Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 该方法在黎曼流形双目标优化中兼具理论保证与计算效率，并成功应用于稀疏主成分分析的双目标形式，展示了其在数据分析问题中的实际适用性。

Abstract: We propose a Pareto front approximation (MPFA) method for smooth bi-objective optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds based on a Hermite interpolation technique. Compared with the existing multiobjective optimization numerical algorithms, the proposed method can generate a continuous approximate Pareto front without multiple initial points. We establish convergence of the proposed method and analyze the approximation error of the resulting Pareto front. Numerical experiments on several test problems demonstrate that the proposed approach can effectively approximate the Pareto front with high accuracy and reasonable computational cost. Furthermore, the method is applied to a bi-objective formulation of sparse principal component analysis, illustrating its practical applicability in data analysis problems.

</details>


### [168] [The Global Asymptotic Stability Problem for Linear MPC Is Undecidable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09930)
*Johan Löfberg*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于带约束的有限时域线性模型预测控制（MPC），判定其全局渐近稳定性是不可判定的，即使在时域为1、权重取单位矩阵、优化器唯一且全局可行等极简条件下依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究普遍认为MPC的不稳定性或不可判定性可能源于长预测时域、维度增长、递归可行性失效或优化器非唯一等因素。本文旨在厘清这些因素是否为不可判定性的必要成因。

Method: 采用归约（reduction）方法，构造四类归约：1）预测状态盒约束；2）硬输入盒约束；3）二次软化的输入盒约束；4）将状态与输入维度分别固定为3和6。所有归约均在时域为1、状态/输入/终端权重为单位矩阵、优化器唯一且全局可行条件下进行。

Result: 在上述极简设定下，判定受约束有限时域线性MPC的全局渐近稳定性仍为不可判定。四类归约分别覆盖不同约束形式，且第四类将维度锁定为固定值。

Conclusion: 不可判定性并非由长时域、维度增长、递归可行性失效或优化器非唯一性引起，而是源于问题本质。该结论对MPC稳定性分析的算法可行性构成根本性限制。

Abstract: We prove that deciding global asymptotic stability for constrained finite-horizon linear model predictive control is undecidable. This holds at horizon one with identity state, input, and terminal weights, unique optimizers, and global feasibility. Separate reductions cover predicted-state boxes, hard input boxes, and quadratically softened input boxes. A fourth reduction fixes the state and input dimensions to three and six. Hence undecidability is not caused by long horizons, growing dimensions, failures of recursive feasibility, or nonuniqueness.

</details>


### [169] [Boundary control for optimal mixing by two-dimensional second-grade fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09946)
*Kush Kinra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究二维不可压缩二阶流体中非扩散标量的最优边界混合，以Navier-slip边界切向牵引为控制，目标为终端混范数加控制代价及可选涡度奖励，证明适定性、最优控制存在性、可微性、伴随方程及大惩罚下唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在二阶流体（具有空间滤波加速度与广义涡度）中，如何通过边界混合有效控制非扩散标量的混合，并将该控制问题置于严格的数学框架下。

Method: 以Navier-slip条件中的切向牵引为边界控制；提升非齐次边界数据使控制g及其时间导数进入状态方程；证明状态适定性、控制-状态映射方向可微性，建立弱后向伴随形式与对偶恒等式得到一阶变分不等式。

Result: 证明了被动标量的全局状态适定性及主动标量在控制无关公共局部区间上的适定性；建立了最优控制存在性、所需拓扑下的方向可微性以及弱后向伴随公式；当β=ζ=0时，附加伴随正则性给出足够大控制惩罚γ下最优控制唯一性。

Conclusion: 该工作为二阶流体中非扩散标量的最优边界混合控制提供了完整的数学分析，包括适定性、最优性条件与唯一性结果。

Abstract: We study optimal boundary mixing of a nondiffusive scalar transported by a two-dimensional incompressible second-grade fluid. The control is the tangential traction in a Navier-slip boundary condition, and the principal objective is the terminal $(H^1(Ω))'$ mix-norm, supplemented by a quadratic control cost and an optional enstrophy reward. The second-grade constitutive law introduces a spatially filtered acceleration and a generalized vorticity; after lifting the nonhomogeneous boundary data, both $\mathbf{g}$ and $\partial_t\mathbf{g}$ enter the state equation, where $\mathbf{g}$ is the boundary control variable. We prove global state well-posedness for a passive scalar and well-posedness on a common, control-independent local interval for an active scalar. We then establish existence of an optimal control, directional differentiability of the control-to-state map in the topology required by the terminal objective, and a weak backward adjoint formulation. A duality identity yields the first-order variational inequality. In the absence of the friction coefficient $β$ and enstrophy reward weight $ζ$ (that is, $β=ζ=0$), the additional adjoint regularity implies uniqueness of the optimal control for all sufficiently large control penalties $γ$.

</details>


### [170] [Beyond Verified Answers: Solver-Informed Self-Distillation for Bootstrapping Operations Research Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09957)
*Rui Zhu,Minglong Cao,Chenyu Zhou,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出SOLID框架，利用求解器伪影进行自蒸馏，无需验证答案或外部评估器即可自提升运筹优化语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练LLM进行运筹优化（OR）建模存在三大局限：依赖人工专家或更强模型验证的合成数据，监督难以扩展；信用分配要么粗糙（结果奖励只评分整条轨迹，不定位关键决策），要么成本高（过程监督需额外评估器）；特权自蒸馏因使用部署时不可得的求解器上下文而导致风格不匹配。

Method: SOLID：求解器信息引导的在线策略自蒸馏框架。执行多次rollout产生的候选程序，对其目标函数进行聚类，选取多数组的伪影作为伪参考；随后使用组相对优势和稠密自监督信号进行模型更新，全程无需验证答案或外部评估器。

Result: 在多个OR基准上，SOLID相比仅使用结果奖励的组相对训练，提升了通用模型和OR微调模型的求解准确率。模型可仅凭自身rollout生成的求解器伪影反馈实现改进。

Conclusion: 求解器伪影可作为可扩展的自提升监督来源，无需可信答案即可提升OR建模能力，使自蒸馏成为实用且无评估器的稠密监督方式。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) can translate natural-language descriptions into operations research (OR) formulations. Post-training techniques including reinforcement learning and on-policy self-distillation have further improved this capability. However, three limitations remain in training LLMs for OR formulations. First, training commonly relies on synthetic formulations validated by human experts or stronger models, constraining scalable supervision. Second, credit assignment is either coarse or costly: outcome rewards score an entire trajectory without locating the responsible modeling decision, whereas process-level supervision requires an additional evaluator. Third, privileged self-distillation can induce style mismatch by using solver context unavailable at deployment. We find that a model can improve from solver-artifact feedback generated by its own rollouts, making self-distillation a practical, evaluator-free source of dense supervision. Therefore, we propose SOLID: Solver-Informed On-Policy LearnIng through Self-Distillation, a novel framework for self-improving OR language models without verified answers or external evaluators. SOLID executes candidate programs from multiple rollouts, clusters their objectives, and selects a majority-group artifact as a pseudo-reference. The model then performs updates using group-relative advantages and dense self-supervision signals. Across multiple OR benchmarks, SOLID improves solution accuracy for both general-purpose and OR-tuned models over outcome-only group-relative training. These results show that solver artifacts can support scalable self-improvement without trusted answers.

</details>


### [171] [Null-controllability for some linear parabolic systems with controls acting on different parts of the domain and its boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09959)
*Guillaume Olive*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究常系数线性抛物型系统在多个分布于不同子域和/或边界上的控制作用下的零可控性，在一维情形下证明了Kalman秩条件，并在仅有分布控制时建立了Carleman估计等相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性抛物型系统的零可控性通常依赖控制作用的位置与数量，当多个控制分别作用于不同分布子域或边界时，其可控性判定条件尚需明确的理论刻画，尤其缺乏类似有限维系统的秩条件。

Method: 针对常系数线性抛物型系统，考虑多个控制分别作用于不同分布子域和/或边界的情形；在一维情形下通过秩条件分析证明Kalman秩条件；对仅有分布控制的情形，采用Carleman估计方法建立相关结果。

Result: 在一维情形下证明了Kalman秩条件；在仅有分布控制的情形下，建立了包括Carleman估计在内的相关结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为多控制作用下的常系数线性抛物型系统零可控性提供了秩条件判据，并通过Carleman估计扩展了分布控制情形的理论结果。

Abstract: In this work we study the null-controllability properties of linear parabolic systems with constant coefficients in the case where several controls are acting on different distributed subdomains and/or on the boundary. We prove a Kalman rank condition in the one-dimensional case. In the case where only distributed controls are considered we also establish related results such as a Carleman estimate.

</details>


### [172] [Boundary approximate controllability of some linear parabolic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09982)
*Guillaume Olive*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究两类线性抛物系统的边界近似可控性，给出充分条件，在一维单控制下证明其必要性，并结合矩形域控制位置给出刻画，最后给出分布可控但边界不可控的级联反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两类线性抛物系统（常系数耦合的n个热方程系统、由带空间变系数的一阶偏微分算子耦合的2×2级联系统）的边界近似可控性尚需系统刻画，尤其是充分/必要条件、控制域位置的影响以及分布与边界可控性的差异。

Method: 基于H.O. Fattorini提出的一般性刻画方法，对每类系统在任意空间维数下证明边界近似可控的充分条件；在一维且仅有一个控制时证明该条件也是必要的；对耦合热方程系统在矩形域上按控制域位置进行分类刻画；并构造级联系统反例比较分布可控性与边界可控性。

Result: 对两类系统在任意维数下获得了边界近似可控的充分条件；在一维单控制情形证明了条件的必要性；在矩形域上给出了依赖控制域位置的刻画；构造出分布可控成立而边界可控不成立的级联系统。

Conclusion: 两类线性抛物系统的边界近似可控性可由统一的一般性刻画框架处理，条件在低维单控制情形具有必要性，控制域位置对矩形域结果有决定性影响，且分布可控性一般不蕴含边界可控性。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the boundary approximate controllability of two classes of linear parabolic systems, namely a system of $n$ heat equations coupled through constant terms and a $2 \times 2$ cascade system coupled by means of a first order partial differential operator with space-dependent coefficients. For each system we prove a sufficient condition in any space dimension and we show that this condition turns out to be also necessary in one dimension with only one control. For the system of coupled heat equations we also study the problem on rectangle, and we give characterizations depending on the position of the control domain. Finally, we exhibit a cascade system for which the distributed controllability holds whereas the boundary controllability does not. The method relies on a general characterization due to H.O. Fattorini.

</details>


### [173] [Observability and controllability for the Schrödinger equation on polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10096)
*Wei Qu,Zhiwen Duan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究多面体上Schrödinger方程的可观测性与可控性：当初始数据具有$H^s\,(s>d/2)$正则性时，以边界奇异集的任意非空开邻域为控制区域，可观测性与可控性成立，与特征函数的控制区域一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多面体边界的奇异集对特征函数集中现象具有关键作用，其特征函数控制区域已被研究，但Schrödinger方程在多面体上的可观测性与可控性是否能在同样的奇异集邻域上成立尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明思路具体借鉴Burq--Zworski黑箱控制方法，通过预解估计导出可观测性不等式；预解估计的证明灵感来自Cekić--Georgiev--Mukherjee关于多面体上特征函数集中的工作，使用半经典测度并密切依赖多面体中台球流的动力学性质；最后用标准HUM论证由可观测性推出可控性。

Result: 对$\mathbb{R}^d\,(d\geq2)$中的凸多面体，当初始数据具备$H^s\,(s>d/2)$正则性时，以边界奇异集的任意非空开邻域为控制区域，可观测性与可控性均成立，且该控制区域与特征函数情形相同。

Conclusion: 多面体上Schrödinger方程的可观测性与可控性可在边界奇异集的开邻域上实现，其证明依赖于预解估计、半经典测度与台球流动力学性质，并通过HUM由可观测性推得可控性。

Abstract: We consider the observability and controllability for the Schrödinger equation on polyhedra. We show that for the Schrödinger equation on convex polyhedra in $\mathbb{R}^d\,(d\geq2)$, when the initial data possess $H^s\,(s>d/2)$ regularity, observability and controllability hold, with the control region being an arbitrary nonempty open neighborhood of the singular set of the boundary, which is the same as the control region for eigenfunctions. Our proof of observability is a concrete adaptation of the method in the Burq--Zworski black box control work, where the observability inequality is proved via a resolvent estimate. Hence, before proving observability, we present an observability resolvent estimate on polyhedra. The idea of the proof of this resolvent estimate comes from the case of eigenfunction concentration on polyhedra considered by Cekić--Georgiev--Mukherjee, which uses semiclassical measures and relies closely on the dynamical properties of the billiard flow in polyhedra. Finally, by a standard HUM argument, we show that observability implies controllability.

</details>


### [174] [A Robust Binary Nonlinear Solver for Multi-stage Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10145)
*Kashif Rashid*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多阶段决策框架，用于以通用方式建模各类序贯决策问题，并配套设计了一种结合蒙特卡洛搜索与增量概率强化学习的鲁棒随机求解方法，以处理该框架导出的二进制非线性优化问题，并通过示例应用进行演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序贯决策问题形式多样，缺乏统一、通用的建模方式，且由此产生的二进制非线性优化问题难以用常规方法有效求解，因此需要兼具通用性与鲁棒性的建模与求解方案。

Method: 提出多阶段决策框架以通用化地表述各类序贯决策问题；针对该框架导出的二进制非线性优化问题，设计鲁棒随机求解程序，采用蒙特卡洛搜索并结合增量概率学习与强化机制。

Result: 所提出的框架与方法通过一个示例应用进行了演示，表明二者能够协同用于序贯决策问题的建模与求解。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了通用的多阶段决策建模框架及相应的鲁棒随机求解手段，为二进制非线性序贯决策问题提供了可行途径。

Abstract: This document describes two developments. The first is a Multi-stage Decision Framework that permits the formulation of various sequential decision-making problems in a generic manner. The second concerns a robust stochastic solution procedure designed to handle the binary nonlinear optimization problem stemming from the proposed decision framework. The method adopts Monte-Carlo search with incremental probabilistic learning with reinforcement. The two developments are described herein along with a demonstrative application example.
  Key words: Multi-stage, decisions, binary, nonlinear systems, probabilistic and reinforcement learning

</details>


### [175] [Distributed Vector Assembly via Selector-Anchored Diffusion with Local Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10201)
*Diana Vieira Fernandes,Carlos Santos Silva,Soummya Kar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出Selector-Anchored Diffusion with Local Innovation (D+LI)，一种分布式向量组装原语，可从独立拥有的权威块构造完整局部副本，并给出收缩性、ISS稳定性与KL有限长度收敛等理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统中各参与方独立持有权威数据块，需要在不集中原始数据的前提下构造完整局部副本；同时纯平均与原始加性选择器源无法隔离该副本，因此需要新的机制来保证收敛与稳定性。
