Method: D+LI采用正交选择器保持块身份、局部创新锚定每个所有者的贡献，并通过Laplacian扩散在网络中传播这些贡献。理论分析基于误差算子分解：坐标上等于单位阵减一个以所有者为基础的Laplacian。

Result: 误差算子给出与总维度和块维度无关的全局收缩因子；对时变外部目标，建立有限T的ISS界及Lipschitz协调映射（含非势映射）下的自洽估计器-控制器增益条件；对半代数势问题，在显式时间尺度分离条件下证明耦合决策-组装迭代的KL有限长度收敛，且每次外层迭代通信预算固定有限。三个数值实验验证了收缩紧性、有限时域跟踪、可容许KL时间尺度及离散与非势外层映射。

Conclusion: D+LI为分布式向量组装提供了具备收缩性、ISS稳定性与KL收敛保证的理论框架，适用于势与非势、离散与非势外层协调映射，并通过数值实验得到验证。

Abstract: We introduce Selector-Anchored Diffusion with Local Innovation (D+LI), a distributed vector-assembly primitive that constructs complete local replicas from independently owned authoritative blocks. Orthogonal selectors preserve block identity, local innovation anchors each owner's contribution, and Laplacian diffusion propagates these contributions across the network. We show that pure averaging with raw additive selector sources cannot isolate this replica. The D+LI error operator decomposes coordinatewise as the identity minus an owner-grounded Laplacian, yielding global contraction with a factor independent of the total and block dimensions. For time-varying outer targets, we establish a finite-T input-to-state stability (ISS) bound and a self-consistent estimator-controller gain condition for Lipschitz coordination maps, including non-potential ones. For semi-algebraic potential problems, we prove Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) finite-length convergence of the coupled decision-assembly iteration with a fixed finite communication budget per outer iteration under explicit timescale-separation conditions. Three numerical experiments demonstrate contraction tightness and finite-horizon tracking, verify an admissible KL timescale, and illustrate discrete and non-potential outer maps.

</details>


### [176] [Circuit-Embedded Feeder-State Reconstruction for Low-Voltage Distribution Networks under Extreme Measurement Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10229)
*Diana Vieira Fernandes,Carlos Santos Silva,Soummya Kar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于物理的增强电路重构框架，在极稀疏信息下重建低压配电网状态，并在459节点葡萄牙馈线上验证，电压RMSE达0.0033 p.u.。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低压配电馈线对内部状态信息需求增加，但实时遥测通常仅限馈线首端有功与无功功率，下游用电与发电仅有静态合同元数据描述，形成极端稀疏的可观测性条件，亟需在此条件下重构网络状态。

Method: 构建"增强电路"：先由馈线首端量测、匹配的元数据及类别级负荷曲线确定母线级负荷与发电先验；再以这些先验参数化辅助恒功率元件，并通过阻抗支路与物理馈线耦合；最后求解所得非线性交流电路平衡，得到满足基尔霍夫定律的物理母线电压、相角与潮流。同时采用加权最小二乘（WLS）以加权残差拟合方式重构状态。

Result: 在真实的459节点葡萄牙低压馈线模型上评估，使用672个15分钟快照的样本内标定合成基准。全部快照均收敛；电压RMSE、MAE与最大绝对误差分别为0.0033、0.0014和0.0337 p.u.。RMSE低于两个独立设定的静态先验WLS基线。直接先验交流潮流校验得到的电压幅值高度吻合，验证了增强电路对先验的忠实实现。

Conclusion: 该物理增强电路重构框架能在实时遥测极度稀疏的条件下，将有限信息映射为基尔霍夫一致的物理母线状态量，精度优于静态先验WLS基线，且与直接先验潮流结果一致，适用于低压配电馈线的状态重构。

Abstract: Low-voltage (LV) distribution feeders increasingly require internal state information, yet real-time telemetry is often limited to feeder-head active and reactive power, while downstream demand and production are described only by static contract metadata. This paper proposes a physics-based augmented-circuit reconstruction framework for this extreme-sparsity regime. Feeder-head measurements, matched metadata, and class-level profiles first determine bus-level load and production priors. These priors parameterize auxiliary constant-power elements coupled to the physical feeder through impedance branches, and the feeder state is reconstructed by solving the resulting nonlinear AC circuit equilibrium. Thus, the augmented-network physics maps the sparse information set into Kirchhoff-consistent physical-bus voltages, angles, and flows, whereas weighted least squares (WLS) reconstructs the state through weighted residual fitting. The method is evaluated on a real 459-bus Portuguese LV feeder model using an in-sample-calibrated synthetic benchmark of 672 fifteen-minute snapshots. It converged for every snapshot and achieved voltage RMSE, MAE, and maximum absolute error of 0.0033, 0.0014, and 0.0337 p.u., respectively. Its RMSE was lower than those of two independently specified static-prior WLS baselines. A direct-prior AC power-flow check produced closely matching voltage magnitudes, confirming that the augmented circuit faithfully realizes the constructed priors.

</details>


### [177] [An $O(1/T^3)$ algorithm for minimizing convex quadratic functions over the $L_1$ ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10314)
*Yuyuan Ouyang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究单位L1球上的凸二次最小化问题，提出一种一阶算法，在子问题可精确求解时达到O(L/T^3)的目标值误差，并首次实现O((L/ε)^{1/3})的一阶oracle复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中该类问题的最优收敛率为O(L/T^2)，本文希望突破该瓶颈，从信息复杂度理论角度寻求更优的一阶oracle复杂度，并为未来可实现的O(L/T^3)算法提供思路。

Method: 提出一种新颖的一阶算法：将单位L1范数球中向量的分量分解为"好"部分与"坏"部分，并对"坏"部分使用对称秩-1（SR1）更新；算法设计受弱型L1估计与调和分析中好坏部分分解思想启发。

Result: 在子问题可精确求解的假设下，经T次梯度评估后目标值误差上界为O(L/T^3)；从信息复杂度理论看，该算法首次达到O((L/ε)^{1/3})的一阶oracle复杂度，优于文献中O(L/T^2)的收敛率。

Conclusion: 该算法在理论上实现了更优的收敛率与oracle复杂度，但当前版本尚不具备实际可实施性；作者期望其能为未来可实施且高效的O(L/T^3)收敛率算法提供启发。

Abstract: We study convex quadratic minimization over the unit $L_1$ ball in which the maximum eigenvalue of the Hessian matrix is bounded by a positive constant $L$. We propose a novel first-order algorithm with objective value error bounded by $O(L/T^3)$ after $T$ gradient evaluations, assuming that the subproblems involved in the algorithm can be solved exactly. To the best of our knowledge, the best convergence rate of algorithms in the literature is $O(L/T^2)$. From the perspective of information-based complexity theory, our proposed algorithm is the first in the literature that achieves the $O((L/\varepsilon)^{1/3})$ first-order oracle complexity, although its current version is not necessarily practical for implementation. We hope that our proposed algorithm could shed some light on future implementable and efficient $O(L/T^3)$-convergence-rate algorithms. The proposed algorithm incorporates a decomposition of components of vectors in the unit $L_1$-norm ball to "good" and "bad" parts, and uses symmetric rank-1 (SR1) updates on the bad parts. The proposed algorithm was developed after the author instructed the OpenAI ChatGPT 6 (Astra) model to study the problem using ideas of weak-type $L^1$ estimates and good-bad part decomposition in harmonic analysis and a recent result.

</details>


### [178] [Convergence of a Randomized Newton Method in Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10465)
*Edward Huynh,Björn Engquist*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了一种带状态相关方差的随机牛顿优化算法，用于求解一般非凸函数的唯一全局极小点，并在有界区域设定下证明了全局几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对一般非凸目标函数的全局极小点定位问题，传统牛顿法缺乏全局收敛保证，且随机噪声的引入使收敛性分析更复杂，因此需要研究兼具探索性与收敛性的随机牛顿算法。

Method: 将牛顿算法与具有状态相关方差的高斯加性噪声耦合，在非凸目标函数和有界区域设定下进行收敛性分析。证明依赖算法的两个关键性质：非退化探索性（保证在有限步后进入极小点邻域）和衰减噪声性（以高概率产生压缩并防止无限次离开极小点邻域）。

Result: 在有界区域设定下，证明了该随机牛顿优化格式的全局几乎必然收敛性。

Conclusion: 该算法通过非退化探索与噪声衰减的协同机制，实现了对一般非凸函数唯一全局极小点的全局几乎必然收敛，为非凸随机优化提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We analyze a stochastic Newton optimization scheme for locating the unique global minimizer of a general nonconvex objective function. The method couples a Newton algorithm to additive Gaussian noise with state-dependent variance. In the bounded domain setting, we prove global almost sure convergence. The proof is based on two features of the algorithm: a nondegenerate exploratory property that ensures entrance into a neighborhood of the minimizer after a finite number of steps, and a decaying-noise property that yields contraction with high probability and prevents infinitely many exits from the neighborhood of the minimum.

</details>
